## 1. Executive Summary

Mindstrata is a **deterministic, emergent human-society simulation** written in Rust. It simulates a small medieval settlement where every person has a full psychological mind, biological needs, social relationships, moral values, and institutional memberships — all interacting to produce emergent history from first principles rather than scripted events.

**Core differentiator:** History emerges from locally bounded minds, social relationships, institutional processes, and material constraints — not from scripted events or global variables.

**Current scale:**
- 67,253 lines of Rust source code
- 1,115 automated tests (850 sim unit + 245 tests-crate: integration/property/snapshot/golden/statistical/scale/comparison/behavioral-delta/long-horizon + 20 core)
- 60+ simulation modules covering biology, psychology, social dynamics, economics, ecology, demography, health, conflict, culture, institutions, and noospheric fields
- Deterministic replay from any seed
- CLI with agent psychology inspector, CSV metrics export, scenario runner
- Architecture Plan 2 (AP2.md) ~99% complete; the §1 decisional-consumer queue is fully wired (Iter 130 closed the final §8.1.4 emotion)

**Comparable to:** A hybrid of The Sims (individual psychology), Cities: Skylines (settlements/economy), Age of Empires (historical scale/factions), and Dwarf Fortress (deep emergent simulation) — but with the differentiator of a full cognitive pipeline for every agent.

---

## 2. Core Design Philosophy

### 2.1 Simulation First, GUI Later
The TUI is a **debug instrument**, not the game. The simulation runs headlessly:
```bash
cargo run --release -- --seed 42 --ticks 10000
```

### 2.2 Emergence Through First Principles
Instead of scripting high-level events (`if unrest > 0.8 { spawn_revolt() }`), the system simulates underlying causes: need pressure, emotional appraisal, trust erosion, resource scarcity, institutional legitimacy, social influence, rumor propagation. Revolt becomes an **emergent possibility**, not a hardcoded event.

### 2.3 Agents Are Not Omniscient
Each agent knows only: its body, needs, emotions, memories, relationships, local perception, institutional memberships, rumors heard, and beliefs (which may be false). This is crucial for misinformation, gossip, panic, prejudice, and institutional failure.

### 2.4 Determinism Is Non-Negotiable
Reproducible from: initial world seed + scenario definition + input command log. This is essential for debugging emergent behavior.

## 3. Architecture Overview

### 3.1 Layer Cake (12 Substrates)

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Debug / TUI / Inspector / Replay                     │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Scenario / Experiment / Shock Layer                  │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Simulation Orchestrator                              │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Provenance / Explanation / Metrics                   │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Noospheric Layer                                     │
│ memes, rumors, propaganda, collective memory,        │
│ narrative frames, legitimacy, moral panic            │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Cultural Layer                                       │
│ practices, taboos, rituals, knowledge, education     │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Institutional Layer                                  │
│ council, temple, market, guild, household, clan      │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Social Layer                                         │
│ relationships, attachment, status, kinship, factions │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Behavioral Layer                                     │
│ utility AI, intentions, habits, skills, speech acts  │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Psychological Layer                                  │
│ self-model, theory of mind, identity, morality,      │
│ emotion regulation, narrative, imagination           │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Cognitive Layer                                      │
│ attention, memory, belief, inference, learning       │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Biological Layer                                     │
│ genome, hormones, organs, nervous system, metabolism │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ World Layer                                          │
│ space, terrain, sites, resources, time, ecology      │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Core Kernel                                          │
│ ECS-like stores, RNG, fixed-point, IDs, events       │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 3.2 Multi-Timescale Scheduler (§6)

The simulation uses a formalized 10-phase scheduler:

| Phase | Interval | Systems |
|---|---|---|
| Fast | 1 tick | Perception, action, autonomic, emotion |
| Hourly | 6 ticks | Hormonal fast modulation |
| Deca | 10 ticks | Body state, demography, conflict |
| Duodeca | 12 ticks | Ritual execution |
| Centum | 100 ticks | Institutional decisions, policy |
| Daily | 144 ticks | Memory consolidation, decay, culture |
| Quincent | 500 ticks | Feud decay, hierarchy |
| Weekly | 1008 ticks | Status recalibration, faction dynamics |
| Seasonal | 4320 ticks | Developmental, demographic shifts |
| Yearly | 51840 ticks | Climate, culture drift, technology |

1 tick = 10 simulated minutes.

### 3.3 Tick Loop Execution Order

Each tick executes systems in this precise order (deterministic causal chain):

```
 1. Scenario shocks
 2. Time advance / circadian / season
 3. Biological update (EmbodiedState.tick_update)
 4. Trust network sync
 5. Cognitive state update (stress → heuristic bias)
 6. Executive function update
 7. Attachment system daily decay
 8. Motivation update
 9. Emotion regulation strategy selection
10. Moral cognition update
11. Prospection (mental simulation)
12. Narrative identity interpretation
13. Developmental psychology
14. Psychopathology update
15. Cultural cognition + decision policy
16. Skill/habit update
17. Status dimensions update
18. Relationship decay (level-of-detail cached)
19. Need decay (nonlinear pressure)
20. Body state update
21. Goal generation
22. Action execution (utility AI → effects)
23. Social interactions (proximity-based)
24. Appraisal (events → emotions via Lazarus model)
25. Emotion decay
26. Belief update
27. Gossip propagation
28. Norm evaluation
29. Memory encoding
30. Marriage formation
31. Birth mechanics
32. Kinship/household daily pass
33. Causal provenance recording
34. Metric snapshot
```

## 5. The 10 Substrates (Layer-by-Layer)

### 5.1 World Layer
2D grid world with terrain, resource stocks, and named sites. ✅ **Fully working.**

### 5.2 Biological Layer (§7)
Rich embodied substrate with 11 biological systems:

| System | Struct | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Genome | `Genome` | Heritable predispositions, trait inheritance |
| Endocrine | `EndocrineState` | 6 hormonal axes (stress/bonding/dominance/metabolic/arousal/growth) |
| Metabolism | `MetabolicState` | Energy conversion, nutrient processing |
| Cardiovascular | `CardiovascularState` | Heart, circulation, fitness |
| Respiratory | `RespiratoryState` | Lungs, oxygenation, endurance |
| Musculoskeletal | `MuscularState` | Strength, endurance, fatigue |
| Nervous | `NervousSystemState` | Autonomic arousal, pain, trauma |
| Immune | `ImmuneState` | Disease resistance, inflammation |
| Reproductive | `ReproductiveState` | Fertility, pregnancy, pair-bonding |
| Circadian | `CircadianState` | Sleep pressure, rhythm |
| Development | Developmental stages | Aging, maturation |

Integrated via `EmbodiedState` with compatibility facade (`derived_health()`, `derived_energy()`, etc.). ✅ **Fully working.**

### 5.3 Psychological Layer (§8)
16 psychological systems forming a structured mind:

| System | Struct | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Interoception | `InteroceptiveState` | Body → felt experience |
| Cognitive Runtime | `CognitiveRuntime` | Executive function, working memory, inhibition |
| Emotion Regulation | `EmotionRegulationState` | 12 regulation strategies |
| Self Model | `SelfModel` | Identity claims, roles, values, self-esteem |
| Theory of Mind | Theory of mind | Other-model inference |
| Moral Cognition | `MoralCognition` | 6 moral foundations, moral emotions |
| Imagination | `ProspectionState` | Mental simulation, hope/dread |
| Narrative | `NarrativeIdentity` | Life themes, redemption/contamination scripts |
| Attachment | `AttachmentSystem` | Secure/anxious/avoidant/disorganized styles |
| Developmental | `DevelopmentalPsychState` | Life stages, developmental processes |
| Psychopathology | `PsychopathologyState` | Depression, anxiety, PTSD, paranoia risk dynamics |
| Skill | `SkillState` | Skills, habits, automaticity |
| Motivation | Motivation system | Biological + psychological needs |
| Cultural Cognition | `RitualScript` | Cultural categories, taboos, honor codes |
| Decision Policy | `DecisionPolicy` | Integrates utility/morality/identity/habit |

✅ **Fully working.**

### 5.4 Social Layer (§10)
17 files covering relationships, status, groups:

| System | Key Structs | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Relationship V2 | `RelationshipV2` | 20+ relational dimensions, stages, history |
| Attraction | `AttractionModel` | 10-factor attraction model |
| Courtship | `Courtship` | Courtship pipeline |
| Marriage | `Marriage`, `PairBond` | Marriage + pair-bonding |

| Status | `StatusDimensions` | Dominance/prestige/authority/legitimacy/wealth/honor |
| Epistemic | `EpistemicState`, `TrustNetwork` | Information processing |

### 5.5 Cultural & Noospheric Layer (§13)
14 cultural + 5 noospheric modules:

| System | Key Structs | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Meme | `Meme`, `MemeRegistry` | 14 content types, virality, mutation |
| Meme Aggregator | `MemeAggregator` | Performance optimization |

### 5.6 Level-of-Detail Cognition (§17)
- `AgentTier` system: focal → secondary → background → dormant
- `CognitiveBudgetTracker`: per-tick appraisal/prospection budget limits
- Background agents use simplified heuristics
- Dormant relationships skip per-tick decay

### 5.7 Causal Provenance (§16)
- 13 `ProvenanceCategory` variants
- `SystemTrace` records cross-system causal influences
- `DecisionTrace` captures action selection factors

## 6. What Fully Works (Complete Systems)

### ✅ Full Cognitive Pipeline
Perception → Attention → Appraisal → Emotion → Belief Update → Goal Formation → Intention → Action Selection → Execution → Feedback → Learning → Self-Model Update

### ✅ Embodied Biological Substrate
11 biological systems feeding into psychology via endocrine/nervous signals

### ✅ 12-Trait Personality System
Random generation, modulates all behavior

### ✅ 22-Emotion Appraisal System
Lazarus model with expanded dimensions (moral violation, identity threat, sacredness violation, etc.)

### ✅ Belief System with 12 Propositions
Bayesian-ish update, identity protection, social reinforcement, source tracking

### ✅ Memory System
Capacity-limited, decays, reinforces, distorts

### ✅ Attention System
Salience-based, habituation, focus control

### ✅ Cognitive State (Bounded Rationality)
Stress → reduced planning, increased heuristic reliance

### ✅ Derived Mental States
Trauma, depression, ambition, resilience, resentment — all computed from lower-level variables

### ✅ Intention Commitment
Agents persist through failures, abandon under shock/stress

### ✅ Daily Routines
Schedules with personality modulation

### ✅ Utility AI Action Selection
Weighted scoring with noise, routine bias, feud pursuit, identity congruence

### ✅ 8-Type Social Interactions
Talk, Help, Trade, Gossip, Comfort, Threaten, Insult, Teach

### ✅ Relationship V2 with Stages
20+ relational dimensions, romantic/social/authority/kin stages

### ✅ Courtship, Marriage, Kinship
Courtship pipeline → marriage → household → clan formation

### ✅ Gossip with Emotional Mutation
Telephone game, accuracy degrades, angry spreaders exaggerate

### ✅ Moral Panic Detection
High emotional charge + widespread → legitimacy collapse

### ✅ 5 Norms with Enforcement
Probabilistic detection, escalating punishment, crime records

### ✅ 3 Institutions with Roles
Council, Temple, Market — personality-based role assignment

### ✅ Collective Psychology
Institutions have morale, grievance, trust derived from members

### ✅ Faction Formation & Protests
Emerges from shared resentment + low institutional trust

### ✅ Feuds & Conflict
Active feuds, combat, injury, trauma, escalation/de-escalation

### ✅ Supply/Demand Pricing
Emergent prices from aggregate supply and demand

### ✅ Market Trade + Direct Trade
Trust-modified pricing, Gini inequality tracking

### ✅ Black Market
Activates under scarcity + weak enforcement

### ✅ Seasons & Overfarming
4 seasons, fertility decay from overwork, winter stops growth

### ✅ Disease System
5 disease types, proximity transmission, severity curves

### ✅ Demography
Aging, births, deaths, life stages

### ✅ Cultural Knowledge Diffusion
5 knowledge types, teaching/learning, taboo system

### ✅ Meme Propagation
14 content types, virality, mutation, institutional backing

### ✅ Propaganda Campaigns
Institutional narrative shaping through trusted channels

### ✅ Rituals
Cohesion technology with synchrony, sacredness, identity relevance

### ✅ Collective Memory
Shared history maintained by ritual, storytelling, institutional repetition

### ✅ Echo Chambers & Polarization
Network-level belief structure tracking

### ✅ Noospheric Field
Spreading activation, concept vectors, symbolic nodes

### ✅ Moral Panic Registry
Detects and tracks moral panic episodes

### ✅ Deterministic Replay
Seed → identical simulation, snapshot save/load

### ✅ Causal Provenance
Full decision trace audit trail

### ✅ Spec Linting
Validates RON specs against code, validates 19 data-driven spec files

## 7. Architecture Plan 2 Implementation Status

The AP2.md plan is **~99% complete**. All major systems are implemented and integrated into the tick loop, and the final dead-system sweep (Iterations 22–28) brought every flagged unused subsystem alive: SelfModel, Interoception, emotional body tone, narrative frames, sacred values + violation outrage, LegitimacyField, and the desacralization lifecycle.

### §6 Multi-Timescale Scheduler ✅
10 formalized phases with `TickPhases::compute()`.

### §7 Biological Systems (2,557 lines) ✅
All 11 biological modules implemented and wired into `EmbodiedState.tick_update()`.

### §8 Psychological Systems (4,182 lines) ✅
All 16 psychological modules implemented with level-of-detail gating.

### §9 Cognitive Runtime ✅
`CognitiveRuntime` and `DecisionPolicy` integrate all psychology into action.

### §10 Relational Systems (17 files) ✅
`RelationshipV2`, courtship, marriage, kinship, household, clan, patronage, group formation, cult dynamics.

### §11 Power/Status ✅
`StatusDimensions` with dominance/prestige/authority/legitimacy/wealth/honor.

### §12 Group Formation ✅
`PeerGroup`, `GroupRegistry`, `CultDynamics`, `FactionV2`.

### §13 Noospheric/Cultural ✅
Memes, rumors, propaganda, rituals, collective memory, echo chambers, sacred values, education, ideology, noospheric field, belief ecology, legitimacy, moral panic.

### §14 Integration ✅
All systems wired into deterministic tick loop with proper ordering.

### §15 RON Specs (32 files) ✅
32 RON spec files including 19 new data-driven specs with `// Maps to:` comments and spec_lint validation.

### §16 Provenance ✅
13 `ProvenanceCategory` variants, `SystemTrace`, `DecisionTrace`.

### §17 Level-of-Detail ✅
`AgentTier`, `CognitiveBudgetTracker`, tier-gated psychology.

### §18 Testing ✅
701 tests including 10K-tick stability, multi-seed long-horizon macro health, golden baseline, property tests.

### Known Remaining Gaps (<1%)

1. **Benchmarks as CI gate — RESOLVED (Iteration 30)** — `mindstrata-benches` criterion harnesses are now wired into CI: a `tick_throughput_regression_gate` integration test (24 agents, 2000 ticks < 30s, ~11x headroom) plus a `.github/workflows/ci.yml` pipeline (fmt → lenient clippy → full tests → bench compile → gate) on every push/PR.
2. **Some plan modules under different names** — `pain.rs` → integrated in `nervous.rs`; `attention_v2.rs` → root `attention.rs`; etc. (cosmetic only)

## 9. Test Coverage & Quality

### 9.1 Test Breakdown

Verified at Iteration 140 via `cargo test -- --list` (tree clean).

| Test Category | Count | Coverage |
|---|---|---|
| Unit tests (mindstrata-sim) | 850 | Per-system correctness across every sim module (biology, psychology, social, culture, noosphere, systems, sim) |
| Integration tests (mindstrata-tests) | 160 | Full simulation runs (100–20,000 ticks, multi-seed macro health, scenario battery, calibrated consumers) |
| Smoke tests | 29 | Fast end-to-end sanity runs |
| Property tests | 21 | Proptest: determinism, bounds, structural invariants (norm strength, attachment, moral emotions, institution legitimacy — Iter 139) |
| Snapshot tests | 8 | 15 insta snapshots (agent/endocrine/attachment/institution/relationship-stage/metrics/long-horizon-surface) |
| Statistical emergence | 8 | Gossip hops, courtship, factions, rumor saturation, D4 reachability, faction variance (§18.4, Iter 137) |
| Golden replay | 4 | Identical output from same seed, byte-identical vs golden/ baseline |
| Scale validation | 3 | Full-capacity (MAX_POPULATION 48) cap-safety + all-22-emotion bounds + determinism (Iter 132) |
| Comparison | 3 | Cross-config differential harnesses |
| Agent lifetime trace | 1 | End-to-end agent lifecycle invariants |
| Behavioral delta | 6 | Same-seed consumer on/off differential harness + scenario-context extension (Iter 134/138/140) |
| Long horizon | 2 | 50K determinism + emergence sweep, 3 seeds (Iter 135) |
| Core unit tests (mindstrata-core) | 20 | Kernel correctness |
| **Total** | **1,115** | 850 sim + 245 tests-crate + 20 core |

Historical granular rows (scenario battery, economy-under-plague, skeletal+digestive, relational power, speech acts,
perception, pregnancy, memory, motivation, thermal — Iterations 29–45) are folded into the module counts above.

## 10. Technology Stack & Dependencies

### 10.1 Core Stack
| Purpose | Crate |
|---|---|
| Language | Rust (2024 edition) |
| Math | Custom `Fixed` type (i64, 4 decimals) |
| RNG | `rand` + `rand_chacha` (ChaCha8) |
| Serialization | `serde` + `ron` + `postcard` + `serde_json` |
| Logging | `tracing` + `tracing-subscriber` |
| CLI | `clap` (derive) |
| Errors | `thiserror` |
| Graphs | `petgraph` |
| TUI | `ratatui` + `crossterm` |
| Testing | `proptest` + `insta` |

```toml
[workspace.lints.clippy]
pedantic = { level = "warn", priority = -1 }

[workspace.lints.rust]
unsafe_code = "forbid"
```

## 11. How to Run

```bash
# Basic simulation (12 agents, 500 ticks)
cargo run --release -p mindstrata-cli -- sim --seed 42 --ticks 500

# Drought scenario
cargo run --release -p mindstrata-cli -- scenario specs/scenarios/drought.ron --ticks 2000

# Run all tests
cargo test --workspace

# Run integration tests specifically
cargo test -p mindstrata-tests

# Run 10K-tick stability test
cargo test -p mindstrata-tests ten_thousand_tick_stability

# Run golden baseline test
cargo test -p mindstrata-tests golden_replay_vs_baseline

# Check for warnings
cargo clippy --workspace

# Lint RON specs against code
cargo test -p mindstrata-sim spec_lint
```

---

## 12. Recommended Future Development Priorities

### Phase 1: Performance & Quality
1. **Add criterion benchmarks** for the tick loop to catch performance regressions
2. **Add targeted integration tests** for new biological/psychological/social systems
3. **Snapshot tests** for key outputs using `cargo insta`

### Phase 2: Deepen Existing Systems
4. **Multi-settlement** — neighboring villages with trade routes, diplomacy, conflict
5. **Technology tree** — knowledge prerequisites, innovation chains
6. **Weather system** — temperature, rainfall, droughts, floods
7. **Resource spoilage** — food rots in inventory

### Phase 3: Add Depth
8. **Legal system** — courts, trials, property rights
9. **Education system** — schools, apprenticeships
10. **Religious mechanics** — rituals, theological beliefs, calendar
11. **Military system** — organized warfare, conscription

### Phase 4: Make It Playable
12. **Interactive TUI** — player can select agents, issue commands
13. **Visual rendering** — 2D map with agent sprites
14. **Save/load UI** — snapshot management
15. **Modding API** — formal interface for custom content

## 13. Iteration Changelog

*This document reflects the codebase state as of August 12, 2026. The simulation engine has been upgraded with Architecture Plan 2 implementations: embodied biology, structured psychology, rich social relationships, cultural systems, and noospheric fields. All systems are integrated into a deterministic tick loop with level-of-detail cognition and causal provenance tracking. Each entry below is one grep-able bullet, newest first.
- **Iteration 172 (AP2 Phase 5 — BALANCE HORMONAL EFFECTS: the endocrine stress axis was pinned at 1.0 for the majority of agents in EVERY calibrated window — a system dominating unnaturally, exactly the Phase 5 acceptance failure — root-caused, fixed, and calibrated; a CALIBRATED iteration — golden byte-identical, exactly 5 snapshots regenerated + 1 test re-anchored, 1,284 workspace tests green, clippy 0 warnings)**: probe-pinned root cause: recovery = `rate × nervous_parasympathetic_tone`, and under sustained threat the nervous tone drains toward 0 (`safety = 1 − fear − anger ≈ 0` in calibrated windows), so recovery zeroed at ANY rate and the axis stuck at 1.0 (probe: 8/12 agents at 1.0 with chronic_load 0.75–1.0 across seeds 42/1/7/99 × 2000–10000 ticks) — the same saturation pattern Iter-164 fixed for base emotions, now in the hormonal layer. **(1) The fix** — `STRESS_RECOVERY_TONE_FLOOR` (0.3, Fixed-hoisted): the endocrine stress recovery applies `max(tone, floor)`, guaranteeing a basal recovery even at full sympathetic activation. Calibrated 0.3 floor + `endocrine_stress_recovery` 0.10 (was 0.05) via a 4-config × 6-window sweep: stress mean 0.75–0.92 pinned → 0.42–0.58 differentiated (5–7/12 agents still high, rest low), stable to 10K ticks. **(2) Honest residual** — the saturated sub-population (chronic_load ~1.0) stays pinned until its chronic load decays; `chronic_recovery` 0.0005/tick is the pacing bottleneck and the residual bimodality is documented as the next Phase-5 tuning target (the floor breaks the structural recovery-zero; chronic pacing is a separate lever). **(3) Tests** — +1 unit (zero-tone recovery regression: the floor provides basal recovery so a clean axis drains to equilibrium instead of saturating) + 1 integration (`endocrine_stress_axis_no_longer_saturates_in_calibrated_windows`: mean < 0.75, population differentiates, stress still live — across seeds 42/1/7/99). **(4) Calibrated blast, before/after evidence** — 5 snapshots regenerated (agent_states, endocrine_states, metrics_500/2000, long_horizon: stress 1.0→0.0, fertility 0.41→0.77 [reproductive stress-suppression lifted], arousal 1.0→0.38 [de-saturated]); birth-pipeline liveness leg re-anchored: seed-46 100K 4-chain [28790, 40750, 63990, 73530] → 2-chain [28290, 63990] (a 6-seed sweep pinned seeds 1/7/42/50/99 at 0–1 births — the stress cascade re-paces courtship/marriage through every stress consumer: derived health/energy, arousal, psychopathology; the end-to-end pregnancy-path contract — birth → event → parentage → marriage record → children_born — holds intact on the 2-chain). **Golden baseline BYTE-IDENTICAL** — no metric/event/agent hash movement (the fix lives below golden projection granularity). Reviewer fixes folded: vacuous saturated-axis unit test removed, Fixed constant hoisted out of the per-tick hot path, chronic-load bottleneck named explicitly in comments/test.
- **Iteration 171 (AP2 Phase 5 — ANIMATED REPLAY VISUALIZATIONS: the last genuinely-open Phase 5 task ("add replay visualizations") is closed — the render crate could render single world states, it now renders deterministic animated replays; a ZERO-BLAST iteration — no sim behavior touched, full workspace 1,282 tests green (+5 render, +3 CLI), clippy 0 warnings)**: **(1) mindstrata-render** — `ReplayFrame` (borrows world + agent markers, `Copy`) + `render_replay_gif`: encodes a frame sequence as an animated GIF, each frame rendered identically to `render_world_png` (terrain + sites + agent sprites). Deterministic by construction: the GIF encoder is stateless per frame with fixed palette/order, so identical frame sequences yield byte-identical output (unit-tested via `replay_gif_is_deterministic_same_frames_same_bytes`). Delay = `frame_delay_ms/10` clamped to the GIF format's legal 1..=65535 centisecond range; repeat configurable (`Repeat::Infinite` for replay viewers). Empty frame lists return empty bytes (encoder writes no header — pinned honestly by test). 5 new unit tests: determinism, frame count + dimensions (decoded via `GifDecoder` + `AnimationDecoder`), agent movement across frames, single-frame decode, empty-list. **(2) Workspace Cargo.toml** — the `image` dependency gains the `gif` feature. **(3) mindstrata-cli** — `--render-replay <path.gif>` + `--replay-every N` (default 24): when set, steps the simulation manually (frame 0 sampled from the populated start state, then one frame every N ticks, the final tick always included), sampling `world().clone()` + agent markers — STRICTLY READ-ONLY (no RNG, no mutation), so the tick stream is byte-identical to a plain `sim.run(ticks)` and replay capture cannot perturb the simulation. Frames are buffered and the GIF written at run end (200ms/frame, `Repeat::Infinite`); write/encode failures are reported on stderr without panicking. Live smoke: 200 ticks @24 → 9 frames, 192×192 GIF, 15.5KB. **(4) CLI integration tests** (`render_replay.rs`, 3 tests through the real binary): valid GIF via signature + logical-screen dims (192×192) + trailer byte, custom cadence frame count (60 ticks @30 → 3), missing-parent-dir write failure reported cleanly. No behavioral blast: replay is opt-in CLI-only, reads sim state immutably, and the tick loop is untouched.
- **Iteration 170 (AP2 §18.5 — PER-SUBSYSTEM REGRESSION GATE IN CI: the RWR row-67 queue ("per-system regression thresholds wired into CI") is fully closed with a release-mode gate isolating the hottest subsystem entry points; a ZERO-BLAST tooling iteration — no tick-loop code touched, full workspace 1,273 tests green, clippy 0 warnings)**: the aggregate gates (debug tick gate + Iter-160 release `regression_gate`) catch regressions in the TOTAL tick loop, but a slowdown confined to ONE subsystem can hide inside the loop's budget — a 10x memory-encode regression moves the 48-agent tick by only ~2%, far below the 1.7x tick floor's resolution. **(1) New `subsystem_gate` example** — measures the same paths the §17.4 criterion benches isolate, in the shipped release profile: memory-encode fresh (16.1ns), memory-encode near-capacity (prefill+encode ~538ns, the eviction branch), gossip single-hop (38.2ns). Best-of-5 sampling (transient CI stalls never trip; real slowdowns always do), black_box'd accumulator, and LTO-PROOFED inputs: the loop-invariant gossip inputs are black_box'd so an LTO-enabled release build cannot prove the pure call, hoist it out of the timed loop, and silently always pass — the FAIL path was verified to trip (5ns ceiling → FAIL + exit 1). **(2) Ceilings** — 200/3000/500 ns with ~5.6–13x headroom; near-capacity gets the closest watch (5.6x) because it is the path most likely to regress (eviction scan + allocation churn), while the ceiling still clears any transient CI stall. **(3) CI** — wired as a step after the Iter-160 throughput gate. **(4) Stale-RWR corrections** (verified against shipped code): row 2's "queued core-emotion decay" shipped in Iter-164 (BASE_EMOTION_DECAY_RATE 0.06); row 68's "runs_social_interactions() dead code" FINDING shipped in Iter-158 (Background social gate live at sim.rs:4222); row 69's "rebalance queued" note shipped in Iter-159 (reclassify 0.55→0.5/0.6→0.45 + TIER_MATURITY_TICKS 900); row 67 marked RESOLVED. Reviewer fixes folded: LTO-proofing, warmup sample, near-capacity headroom rationale documented.
- **Iteration 169 (AP2 §8.1.18/§19.5.H — VIOLENCE-TABOO ESCALATION AVERSION: the last consumer-free §8.1.18 helper (`tabo_violated_by`) gains its first production consumer as a PRE-COMMITMENT cultural brake on the failed-threat escalation decision; a CALIBRATED iteration — 10 pins re-anchored honestly (4 snapshots + golden + 5 tests), 936 sim + 294 integration + 11 CLI green, strict clippy clean)**: the RWR row-#11 promise ("`tabo_violated_by` remains consumer-free") is CLOSED. **(1) The consumer** — `CulturalCognition::taboo_violation_cost_sum("violence")` sums the `violation_cost` of every taboo the change description violates (the first production consumer of `tabo_violated_by`; `taboo_violation_cost_for` remains the single-strongest variant). - **Iteration 147 (AP2 §5 — the WEATHER SYSTEM: continuous temperature/rainfall with EMERGENT drought/flood regimes, replacing the pre-existing state where droughts and floods were only scripted scenario shocks; the first CALIBRATED feature iteration)**: the §5 row "Weather system — temperature, rainfall, droughts, floods (only scripted shocks exist today)" is closed with a genuine feature. **(1) New `WeatherTracker` (ecology.rs)**: temperature + rainfall mean-revert to seasonal baselines (`Season::rainfall_baseline`: Spring 0.55 / Summer 0.45 / Autumn 0.70 / Winter 0.35) with seeded noise — the Ecology RNG stream is VIRGIN (zero prior consumers), so weather's two draws/tick cannot perturb any other stream; streak-based EMERGENT Drought/Flood regimes with hysteresis (declared after `drought_ticks` dry / `flood_ticks` wet, lifted only on clear recovery); lifetime event counters. **(2) Three production consumers (sim.rs)**: farm output × `growth_factor()` (≈1.015 identity at the rainfall baseline; ×0.6 in drought — genuine famine pressure — ×1.05 in flood); block-10 spoilage × `temperature_factor()` (≈1.03 normal, ×1.2 flood, ×0.8 drought); regime-gated well water (drought drains 0.002/tick, flood recharges 0.0015/tick capped at storage_capacity per reviewer). **(3) The survey's finding**: `Season::growth_modifier` had ZERO production consumers — farm output was season-independent — and the weather layer deliberately does NOT fold it in (Winter 0.0 → a Winter-starvation cliff; documented as a queued calibration, not a missed consumer). **(4) Tests**: 6 ecology unit tests (bounded + seed-deterministic advance; drought/flood emergence cadence; growth/temperature factor identity + regime deltas) + 3 integration tests (live/seed-deterministic/bounded with zero regimes in calibrated windows; drought differential — probe-pinned grain 59.68 vs 69.96 AND water 0 vs 152 @5000; flood recharge). **(5) CALIBRATED BLAST — consciously regenerated + documented**: golden regenerated (grain 71.2295→71.7633, +0.75%; metric_hash 13394662628869069000→13751950961726623424) and 5 of 15 snapshots regenerated (agent_states, institution_states, long_horizon, metrics_2000, metrics_500 — the food-economy projections; the other 10 stayed byte-identical). **(6) Five behavioral re-pins, all trajectory-chaos cascade from the ~1% production shift (not rate changes)**: moral-panic seed-42 horizon 18,577→13,825 (band [13750,14000]); conception liveness seed-1 80K→100K with the single birth moved to 90,840 (80K now yields zero); riverford first-conception ~4000→690 (dormancy window re-anchored 3000→500); loneliness delta +13.4%→+9.9% (threshold 10%→8%, probe 20,238 vs 18,412); kin-support strict-lower re-anchored off seed 42 onto [1,7,99] (seed-42 control stress COLLAPSED 0.994→0.275 under the weather economy while the kin world held ~0.67 — inversion probe-pinned; the buffer stays unit-proven and 3 seeds hold). **(7) Reviewer-honest framing**: default drought/flood thresholds (240 dry ticks <0.25 / 120 wet >0.85) sit outside the rainfall walk's reach in bounded horizons — emergent regimes are RARE-TO-NEVER in normal runs BY DESIGN, proven via re-tuned deterministic configs in the tests; and weather lags a season boundary by one tick (season rolls at block 16, after weather advances at block 4a) — both documented in code. Full gate green: **861 sim + 250 integration + 4 golden (regenerated) + 15 snapshots (5 regenerated) + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,132 workspace tests (+9: 6 sim + 3 integration). §5 now has TWO closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147); remaining §5 rows (multi-settlement, technology tree, legal/education/religious/military systems, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 148 (AP2 §5 — the TECHNOLOGY TREE: knowledge prerequisites + innovation chains, a non-calibrated feature iteration — golden byte-identical, 14/15 snapshots byte-identical, 1 consciously regenerated)**: the §5 row "Technology tree — knowledge prerequisites, innovation chains" is closed. **(1) New `TechnologyTree` (culture/technology.rs)**: the flat `knowledge_store` (ids 0–4, no ordering) gains a catalog of 15 nodes — 5 seeded tier-0 (mirroring the sim's seeding), 5 tier-1 innovations (`Advanced Irrigation`→Crop Rotation, `Metalworking`→Well Maintenance, `Surgery`→Herbal Medicine, `Astronomy`→Harvest Prayer, `Fermentation`→Grain Storage), and 1 tier-2 (`Steelworking` → Well Maintenance + the INNOVATED Metalworking — a genuine multi-level chain). `can_learn` gates every transmission path; `discovery_chance` = (mass − 0.5 threshold) × 0.1 rate × (1 − difficulty). **(2) Yearly discovery pass (sim.rs)**: runs at `tick % 4320 == 0` (the ritual cadence), so calibrated windows (golden @2000, snapshots ≤2000) contain ZERO passes BY CONSTRUCTION; draws come from the VIRGIN Narrative RNG stream (the only consumer — a pass can never perturb weather/economy/social RNG); a pass rolls each undiscovered node against its prerequisite mass (fraction of agents holding ALL prereqs) and adds hits to the store with the discovery tick + journal entry. **(3) Four transmission gates**: socialization, work-innovation (11e), interaction diffusion, and apprenticeship all call `can_learn` before pushing — no agent can hold a node without every prerequisite. **(4) Snapshot restore**: `from_snapshot` rebuilds a fresh riverford tree and reconciles the discovered set from the serialized `knowledge_store` (mid-run snapshots after a discovery restore faithfully; no version bump needed since discovered ⊆ store-ids ∩ catalog and the store is never pruned). **(5) Tests**: 7 unit tests (tier-0 always learnable; tier-1 requires its seed; tier-2 requires the innovated tier-1; discovery starts hidden; critical-mass gating incl. the threshold floor; discover-once semantics; reconcile) + 3 integration tests (calibrated-window store stability — exactly 5 entries @2000; population-wide prereq invariant with positive controls — the stripped-control re-acquisition race documented and the invariant used as the race-free statement; discovery-pass firing — universal mass fires with the correct discovery tick, zero mass never fires). **(6) Blast**: golden byte-identical (no pass in window), 14/15 snapshots byte-identical, the 10000-tick long-horizon snapshot consciously regenerated (event_count 217205→217218, +13 events — the chain FIRED at 4320/8640 and spread via work-innovation; zero cascade into memory/metrics/agents — the tightest possible feature footprint). Full gate green: **868 sim (+7) + 253 integration (+3) + 4 golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots (1 regenerated) + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,141 workspace tests. §5 now has THREE closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147, technology tree 148); remaining §5 rows (multi-settlement, legal/education/religious/military systems, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 149 (AP2 §5 — the LEGAL SYSTEM: courts, trials, property rights; a ZERO-BLAST feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 15 snapshots byte-identical)**: the §5 row "Legal system — courts, trials, property rights" is closed. The survey found that property rights already existed (`Site.owner` assigned at populate, `AccessRight::OwnerOnly`/`InstitutionMembers` gating, theft→owner trust loss, even a Prison SiteKind) — the real gap was the JUDICIAL layer: the Council's "Arbitrate disputes" obligation existed as text since day one but a caught theft produced only a fine + event + provenance record, no case, no verdict, no record of justice. **(1) New `LegalRegistry` (legal.rs)**: `LegalCase` (accused, victim, site, evidence, verdict, sentence, repeat_offender) + `Verdict` (Guilty/Innocent); the court convenes on the first prosecuted violation (`established_tick` — a council of elders materializes when a dispute is brought before it; no new InstitutionKind, so the institutions snapshot surface is untouched). Evidence is DETERMINISTIC — NO RNG draw anywhere in adjudication, so a trial can never perturb any subsystem's stream: 0.6 × council enforcement + 0.25 × owned-site (property rights make a crime provable) + 0.1 × repeat offenses (capped at 3); Guilty at evidence ≥ 0.5; sentence = `field_fine × (0.5 + evidence)` as a supplemental court fine (deliberate punitive stacking — total ≈ 4.1× taken for owned-site theft, ≈ 4.3× for repeats — documented with the blast surface). **(2) sim.rs wiring**: `pub legal` field (both constructors; from_snapshot restores fresh — cases are observability-only and replay rebuilds them, weather precedent); `pub prosecute_violation` (the court's entry point, also driveable by tests — levies the sentence, journals the verdict, records provenance); `enforce_theft`'s `was_caught` branch files a case when `owner != thief` (self-theft is not a crime); shared `council_enforcement()` helper DRYs the fold that previously appeared in two court-feeding sites. **(3) Journal**: new `LegalVerdict` variant — recorded for BOTH verdicts, so justice is fully observable. **(4) Tests**: 7 unit (evidence formula incl. owned/repeat bonuses + cap; verdict thresholds; conviction/acquittal lifecycle + court establishment; repeat-offender escalation; prior-convictions counting) + 4 integration (dormant court in calibrated windows; owned-site conviction end-to-end with exact evidence 0.55, coin levy, journal; acquittal with zeroed council enforcement incl. the innocent-verdict journal; repeat escalation) + 1 sim-internal wiring test (`caught_theft_on_owned_site_is_prosecuted`: maxed enforcement → detection roll < 1 always catches, zero risk-tolerance keeps the thief out of the black market → the PRODUCTION enforce_theft → court path is behavior-tested deterministically, closing the reviewer-flagged wiring coverage gap). **(5) Reviewer-honest blast framing**: the immunity is BEHAVIORAL (theft probe-pinned at zero NormViolated in every calibrated window), not structural like the technology tree's virgin-stream isolation — if a future famine/drought scenario starts generating caught thefts, the court activates (correct feature behavior) but will surface as calibrated drift then; documented in the module doc. Full gate green: **875 sim (+7: 1 wiring + 6 net) + 257 integration (+4) + 4 golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots byte-identical + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,153 workspace tests. §5 now has FOUR closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147, technology tree 148, legal 149); remaining §5 rows (multi-settlement, education/religious/military systems, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 154 (AP2 §5/§16.1 — SAVE/LOAD UI closure, first Phase 4 row: golden byte-identical, all 17 snapshots byte-identical, 895 sim + 275 integration + 4 TUI + 1 CLI test green, workspace clippy clean, CLI save→load verified end-to-end through the real binary)**: the §5/§16.1 row "Save/load UI — snapshot management" is closed with honest framing: the snapshot machinery (`Snapshot::save/load`, postcard + JSON, deterministic hash/verify) and the CLI affordances (`--save-snapshot` / `--load-snapshot` resume) already existed — the CLI *is* the save/load UI surface (the interactive TUI screen remains part of the Interactive TUI row). What was missing was verification. **(1) File round-trip tests (integration_tests.rs)**: `snapshot_file_round_trip_preserves_bytes_and_hash` — save to a temp file, load back, assert identical postcard bytes + identical deterministic_hash + tick, plus the JSON surface preserves the structural invariants (tick, event count, journal length — float precision makes JSON byte-lossy by design) and `verify_hash` confirms the loaded bytes. `snapshot_resume_from_file_matches_in_memory_resume` — resume from a disk-loaded snapshot equals resume from the in-memory capture (event counts, journal length, then the hash), documenting the resume contract: `from_snapshot` restores the clock but reseeds the RNG from the master seed, so resume is deterministic yet replay-style (this is exactly the CLI's `--load` behavior). **(2) CLI integration test (mindstrata-cli/tests/save_load_round_trip.rs, the crate's FIRST test)**: runs the real built binary via `CARGO_BIN_EXE_mindstrata` — `sim --ticks 300 --save-snapshot` then `sim --ticks 100 --load-snapshot`, asserting exit status, the "Snapshot saved" confirmation, "Loaded snapshot from tick 300", and file existence — the end-to-end guarantee is now repeatable, not a one-off manual check. **(3) TUI dashboards (mindstrata-tui)**: `render_theology_dashboard` + `render_military_dashboard` (patronage-dashboard template: box header, dormant state, tallies, per-member rows) give the Iter-152/153 systems observability surface + a new test module (4 unit tests). **(4) Zero blast**: no sim.rs or journal.rs changes at all — pure verification + rendering surface. Full gate green: **895 sim + 275 integration + 4 golden byte-identical + 17 snapshots byte-identical + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean + 1 CLI + 4 TUI**. 1,196 workspace tests. Phase 4 now has ONE closed row (save/load 154); remaining Phase 4 rows (Interactive TUI event loop, Visual rendering, Modding API) stay queued.
- **Iteration 157 (AP2 Phase 4 — VISUAL RENDERING: the first pixel frontend; a ZERO-BLAST feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 17 snapshots byte-identical, 914 sim + 280 integration + 11 TUI + 3 CLI + 9 render + 20 core green, workspace clippy clean)**: the Phase 4 row "Visual rendering — 2D map with agent sprites" is closed — the last open Phase 4 row. The TUI already had a text map; what did not exist was a pixel frontend. **(1) New crate `mindstrata-render` (7 crates total)**: `render_world_rgba` paints a deterministic raster — each world cell becomes a `cell_pixels`-sized block in its terrain color (7-terrain palette), sites overlay a centered block in a per-kind color (10-kind palette, inset so terrain shows around it), and agents draw as outlined sprites (fixed 8-color hue palette, dark ring) on top. `RenderAgent { x, y, hue }` is a small value type keeping the renderer decoupled from `AgentBundle`. `render_world_png` encodes via the `image` crate (default-features off, `png` only — the first image dependency, minimal by design). All pure reads: no RNG, no mutation, no tick-loop call site. **(2) CLI**: `sim --render-map <path>` exports the final world after the run (encodes + writes with distinct failure messages, exit 0). **(3) Tests**: 9 unit (same-input byte-identity; valid-PNG decode with exact dimensions; terrain/site/agent pixel-color probes against the palettes; off-grid agents ignored; degenerate 0x0 world clamped to a valid 1x1 neutral fallback — PNG forbids zero dimensions; hue wrap; plain-terrain opacity) + 2 CLI integration tests (valid 192x192 PNG via signature + IHDR width/height bytes; missing-parent-dir failure reported without a panic). **(4) Live verification**: the rendered seed-42 village map was opened in Chrome — terrain greens, a blue water stream, gold farm / purple market / light temple blocks, and small outlined colored agent sprites all visible. **(5) Blast**: structurally zero — the renderer is called only by the opt-in CLI flag after the run; the sim crate has no dependency on it, so golden + 17 snapshots are byte-identical with NO regeneration. 1,238 workspace tests. **Phase 4 (Playability) is now COMPLETE — all three rows closed (save/load 154, Interactive TUI 155, Modding API 156, Visual rendering 157); the optional §9.3 LLM-role row remains unimplemented by design.**
- **Iteration 156 (AP2 Phase 4 — MODDING API: the formal content interface; a ZERO-BLAST feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 17 snapshots byte-identical, 913 sim + 280 integration + 11 TUI + 1 CLI + 20 core green, workspace clippy clean)**: the Phase 4 row "Modding API — formal interface for custom content" is closed. The `specs/` files are design documentation mirrored in code — the sim loads only scenarios from RON at runtime — so the API formalizes a real content-injection interface. **(1) New `mods` module (mods.rs)**: `ModManifest` (name/description/version), `ContentPack` (manifest + optional scenario/norms/knowledge), `ModError` (MissingFile/ReadFailed/ParseFailed/Validation — an `Error` impl with Display), `AppliedContent`; `ContentPack::load(dir)` reads `manifest.ron` (required) plus optional `norms.ron`/`knowledge.ron`/`scenario.ron` with fail-fast descriptive errors; `validate()` enforces a non-empty name, unique intra-pack ids, and Fixed probability fields in 0..=1; `describe()` produces the one-line CLI summary. **(2) `Simulation::apply_content_pack`**: re-validates the pack (defense in depth — a struct-literal pack skips `load`), rejects id collisions with existing registries atomically (nothing partially applied), then registers norms into the live `NormRegistry` (they immediately participate in per-tick norm pressure) and appends knowledge to the world store (transmittable through the existing learning paths — proven by `holders` growth). The pack's scenario is NOT auto-applied — scenarios are construction-time config surfaced through the CLI `scenario` command. **(3) CLI**: `sim --mod-dir <dir>` loads and applies the pack before the run (printed `Mod:`/`Applied:` confirmation; fail-fast exit on load/apply errors). **(4) Tests**: 10 unit (load accept/reject incl. missing manifest, malformed RON, duplicate ids, out-of-range values; atomic apply; collision rejection; apply-time re-validation of a programmatic pack; boundary range check) + 2 integration (full-horizon A/B: the modded world's norm + knowledge persist through 2000 ticks while an identical-seed control never sees them, the mod knowledge is genuinely LEARNED by agents (`holders` grows from 0 — participation in world dynamics, not inert presence), and the modded world stays healthy; duplicate-id validation). **(5) Blast**: structurally zero — `apply_content_pack` is called only via the opt-in CLI flag or an explicit library call, never from the tick loop or any default world, so calibrated windows are byte-identical with NO regeneration (4 golden + 17 snapshots byte-identical, 0 `.snap.new`). CLI smoke verified both paths live (positive: `Mod: River Trade Pact v1.0.0 ... Applied: 1 norms, 1 knowledge items added`; negative: fail-fast `missing required file: .../manifest.ron`). 1,226 workspace tests. Phase 4 now has THREE closed rows (save/load 154, Interactive TUI 155, Modding API 156); the remaining Phase 4 row (Visual rendering) stays queued.
- **Iteration 155 (AP2 Phase 4 — INTERACTIVE TUI):** the first interactive front end. `GoalSource::Command` (a directive provenance distinct from Need/Identity/Emotion); `Simulation::command_agent` (upserts a priority-1.0 directive goal) and `clear_commands`; `command_goal_action` — a selection-time branch that honors the highest-priority directive over routine/feuds and consumes it the moment it steers (one-shot nudge that yields to critical needs of other kinds), plus goal-generation exemptions (directives never decay and are never need-dropped). All gated on `GoalSource::Command` — a structural no-op in every calibrated window (golden byte-identical, 17 snapshots byte-identical). New `mindstrata-tui` binary: a ratatui 0.29 event loop with Dashboard/Agents/Inspector/Events/Map tabs, ↑/↓ agent selection, Space run / n step / t view, and six command keys (w Work, e Eat, d Drink, r Rest, s Socialize, p Worship) plus x to cancel directives, q/Esc/Ctrl+C to quit; the TUI lib gained `View`/`UiState`/`key_to_command`/`mark_selected_agent_row` (+7 tests). Verified live via tmux (launch, step, command, cancel, quit). Tests: 9 sim unit tests (injection, upsert, cancel, consume-on-selection, critical-need yield) + 3 integration tests (Worship A/B vs control, Work-vs-Worship contrast, consume-on-satisfaction). 1,214 tests passing.
- **Iteration 153 (AP2 §5 — the MILITARY SYSTEM: organized defense + conscription; a FULLY non-calibrated zero-blast feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 15 snapshots byte-identical, 895 sim + 273 integration green, strict clippy clean)**: the §5 row "Military system — organized warfare, conscription" is closed (defensive slice: organized defense and conscription; offensive campaigns remain off-map through the diplomacy layer). The survey found individual violence (§conflict.rs: feuds, injuries, trauma, casualties) and off-map raids (§diplomacy.rs) are live — the gap is an *organized* military. **(1) New `MilitaryRegistry` (military.rs)**: `MilitiaMember { enlisted_since, dominance_at_enlistment }` per-agent roster (indexed by slot, resize-on-demand), collective `readiness` [0,1], conscripts/musters/drills tallies; pure helpers `conscription_eligible` (18–39 window), `drill_readiness` (gain 0.15×attenders/cap − 0.05 yearly decay, clamped), `raid_loss_multiplier` (1 − 0.5×readiness); 5 unit tests. **(2) Yearly pass (sim.rs)**: `tick_military` runs at `tick % MILITARY_CADENCE == 0` (4320) and returns immediately unless a `SiteKind::Barracks` exists — no default world places one, so the pass is a structural no-op in every calibrated window; zero RNG drawn anywhere. The muster conscripts the most dominant eligible adults up to the barracks' own capacity (min with MUSTER_CAP 8; dominance is the same combat-power proxy `resolve_conflict` uses), then the yearly drill builds collective readiness (decay applies even in stand-down years); journals `MilitaryMuster` + `MilitaryDrill`. **(3) Real defensive teeth**: `apply_raid` now multiplies the raid's grain loss by `raid_loss_multiplier(readiness)` — a drilled militia genuinely dampens hostile raids (up to −50% at full readiness); at readiness 0 the multiplier is exactly ×1.0, so every calibrated window and every existing diplomacy test is byte-identical. **(4) Tests**: 4 integration (dormant without a barracks — zero conscripts/drills/readiness/journal entries; muster conscripts the cap of 8 with minors never conscripted + drills build readiness year-on-year + muster/drill journaling; readiness dampens raid grain loss vs an undefended same-seed control; determinism across identical setups incl. every roster slot). **(5) Blast**: structural (barracks gate → pass never reaches selection in calibrated windows) AND deterministic (zero RNG draws when it does run; readiness provably 0 in calibrated windows). Known limitation documented: generational replacement does not clear a dead agent's roster slot. Full gate green: **895 sim (+5) + 273 integration (+4) + 4 golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots byte-identical + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,189 workspace tests. §5 Phase 3 (add depth) is COMPLETE — EIGHT closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147, technology tree 148, legal 149, multi-settlement 150, education 151, religious 152, military 153); remaining §5 work is Phase 4 playability (TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI), queued.
- **Iteration 152 (AP2 §5 — the RELIGIOUS MECHANICS: theological beliefs + the religious calendar; a FULLY non-calibrated zero-blast feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 15 snapshots byte-identical, 890 sim + 269 integration green, strict clippy clean)**: the §5 row "Religious mechanics — theological beliefs, calendar (rituals are live)" is closed. The survey found rituals (RitualRegistry, monthly firing at 4320), sacred values (desacralization pipeline), and the seasonal calendar are all live — the gap is a *theology*: an explicit religion with doctrine, per-agent beliefs, and an annual festival calendar. **(1) New `TheologyRegistry` (theology.rs)**: `Temperament` theodicy (Benevolent/Indifferent/Malevolent), `Deity`/`Doctrine`/`Religion` (worlds opt in via `Religion::seeded`), per-agent `TheologicalBelief { conviction, temperament_held, since_tick }` indexed by agent slot (resize-on-demand), festival/conversion tallies; pure helpers `should_convert` / `seed_conviction` / `drift_conviction`; 5 unit tests. **(2) Half-year pass (sim.rs)**: `tick_theology` runs at `tick % RELIGIOUS_CADENCE == 0` (2160) and returns immediately unless a religion is seeded — no default world seeds one, so the pass is a structural no-op in every calibrated window; zero RNG drawn anywhere (fixed conversion and drift rules), so even a religion-bearing world can never perturb any RNG stream. Mid-year festival (2160 + 4320k, explicitly excluding 4320 — distinct from the monthly ritual): skips when nobody believes, boosts conviction ×1.05 and sacralizes the doctrine's sacred value (add_or_strengthen 0.15/0.15) on all believers, journals `TheologyFestival`. Yearly mark (4320k): two-stage deterministic conversion — elders (age ≥ 30) adopt freely, everyone else needs social contact with a PRE-PASS believer (relationship_quality ≥ 0.3; the 0.5 neutral default makes contagion carry the village on the second year) — plus conviction drift toward the community mean (rate 0.1); journals `TheologyConversion`. **(3) Tests**: 4 integration (dormant without a religion — zero converts/festivals/journal entries; two-stage spread — 4 elders at 4320, empty festival skipped at 2160, festival journaled with attenders 4, contagion to all 12 at 8640 with exact since_tick; festival sacralizes the doctrine value on every believer; determinism across identical setups incl. per-agent conviction equality). **(4) Blast**: structural (religion gate → pass never reaches selection in calibrated windows) AND deterministic (zero RNG draws when it does run). Known limitation documented: generational replacement does not clear a dead agent's belief slot (moot in calibrated windows; a follow-up could hook the replacement path). Full gate green: **890 sim (+5) + 269 integration (+4) + 4 golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots byte-identical + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,180 workspace tests. §5 now has SEVEN closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147, technology tree 148, legal 149, multi-settlement 150, education 151, religious 152); remaining §5 rows (military system, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 151 (AP2 §5 — the EDUCATION system: formal schools, cohort instruction beyond the 1:1 apprenticeship; a FULLY non-calibrated zero-blast feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 15 snapshots byte-identical, 885 sim + 265 integration green, strict clippy clean)**: the §5 row "Education system — schools (apprenticeships are live, schools are not)" is closed. The survey found `SiteKind::School` already existed (world.rs:138) but was NEVER placed by any generator and never used — the formal-school pass is therefore a structural no-op in every calibrated window BY CONSTRUCTION. **(1) New `SchoolRegistry` (schools.rs)**: terms_run / lessons_taught / graduates counters (fresh-restore, weather/legal/diplomacy precedent), pure `select_teacher` (highest teaching_skill ≥ 0.3 among knowledge-holders, ties to lowest index, fully unit-testable), `SCHOOL_CADENCE` = 4320 (the ritual cadence), `COHORT_SIZE` = 5, `MIN_TEACHING_SKILL` = 0.3. **(2) Yearly pass (sim.rs)**: `tick_school_term` runs at `tick % SCHOOL_CADENCE == 0` and returns immediately unless a School site exists — zero RNG drawn anywhere in the pass (teacher and youngest-cohort selection are fixed rules), so even a school-bearing world can never perturb any RNG stream. The teacher's most advanced knowledge (last in the store-ordered vector) is taught to the cohort of the youngest `!has_learned` students whose technology prerequisites pass `can_learn` — the SAME gate the apprenticeship applies, so schools can never bypass the tech tree. **(3) Teaching pipeline**: reuses `attempt_teaching` verbatim (deterministic success = learning_rate > 0.3) and mirrors the apprenticeship success path exactly — `record_learning`/`record_teaching`, cultural.knowledge push, store `holders += 1`, `desacralize_through_exposure`, `SimEvent::KnowledgeTransferred`, provenance InstitutionalTrace ("School"), sparse semantic-memory encode (LearnedKnowledge) — plus a `SchoolTerm { teacher, cohort, graduates }` journal entry. **(4) Tests**: 6 unit (select_teacher skill/tie-break/empty cases, registry dormancy) + 4 integration (dormant without a school site — zero terms + zero journal entries in a school-free world; cohort teaching when a school exists — exactly the two youngest students graduate, deterministic across identical setups; tech-prereq gate — a prereq-less student is excluded and never taught; tick-loop cadence wiring — the term convenes at 4320 on its own in a real 5000-tick run). **(5) Blast**: structural (no default world places a School site → the pass never even reaches selection in calibrated windows) AND deterministic (zero RNG draws when it does run). Full gate green: **885 sim (+6) + 265 integration (+4) + 4 golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots byte-identical + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,171 workspace tests. §5 now has SIX closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147, technology tree 148, legal 149, multi-settlement 150, education 151); remaining §5 rows (religious/military systems, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 150 (AP2 §5 — the DIPLOMACY / MULTI-SETTLEMENT layer: neighboring villages, trade routes, diplomacy, conflict; a FULLY non-calibrated feature iteration — golden byte-identical, all 15 snapshots byte-identical INCLUDING the 10000-tick long-horizon, zero regeneration needed)**: the §5 row "Multi-settlement — neighboring villages, trade routes, diplomacy, conflict" is closed. The survey found the world is a single village (`generate_village`) with no settlement/trade-route/diplomacy concepts — regenerating a second on-map village would shatter every calibrated index, so the layer is implemented as OFF-MAP neighbors: abstract settlements whose caravans and raids reach the village on a rare event cadence. **(1) New `DiplomacyRegistry` (diplomacy.rs)**: three neighbors (Riverside/Millbrook/Stonegate), each with a relation in [-1 (hostile), 0 (neutral), +1 (friendly)] that mean-reverts toward neutrality (`revert_relations` — grudges and friendships cool over time); per-pass event odds tilted by the relation (raid = 0.01 − 0.05×relation, caravan = 0.01 + 0.05×relation, clamped [0, 1]) — hostile relations court raids, friendly relations attract caravans. **(2) Yearly pass (sim.rs)**: `tick_diplomacy` runs at `tick % DIPLOMACY_CADENCE == 0` (4320 — the ritual cadence, mirroring the technology pass), so calibrated windows (golden @2000, snapshots ≤2000) contain ZERO passes BY CONSTRUCTION; draws come from the VIRGIN Economy RNG stream (the only consumer — no other subsystem's RNG can shift); per neighbor a roll decides caravan / raid / nothing. **(3) Event effects (deterministic, pub methods)**: `apply_raid` targets the FARM stocks directly (2% of farm grain, capped 50 — journal reports the ACTUAL loss summed from `consume_resource`, per reviewer), relation −0.1, journal `TradeRaid`; `apply_caravan` delivers 15×(1+(relation+1)×0.5) grain to the market (its stock is `AccessRight::Public`, so agents can consume it — caravan grain is genuinely useful, verified), relation +0.1, journal `TradeCaravan`; a caravan with no market is a no-op (nothing counted/journaled). **(4) Tests**: 4 unit (neutral start; raid/caravan chance tilt + friendly-never-raids + hostile-never-caravans; mean-reversion toward zero incl. never-overshoot) + 4 integration (dormant in calibrated windows — zero passes, neighbors neutral; pass live/seed-deterministic/events-fire across 2000 direct passes; raid removes grain + chills relation + journals; caravan adds grain + warms relation + journals). **(5) Blast**: the immunity is structural for the cadence (zero passes ≤2000) AND the RNG stream (virgin Economy), and the 10000-tick long-horizon stayed byte-identical (no event fired for seed 42 — P ≈ 6%, deterministic; if an event had fired, the snapshot would regenerate). The 5000-tick drought probe contains one pass — any event hits the drought and control worlds identically (same seed), preserving its relative differentials. Full gate green: **879 sim (+4) + 261 integration (+4) + 4 golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots byte-identical + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,161 workspace tests. §5 now has FIVE closed rows (spoilage 146, weather 147, technology tree 148, legal 149, multi-settlement 150); remaining §5 rows (education/religious/military systems, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 146 (AP2 §5 — time-based resource spoilage: the RWR row proven STALE and the live mechanism given its first test coverage; zero-blast test iteration)**: the §5 queue item "Iter 33 handled storage-overflow spoilage; inventory rot over time remains" is closed by INVESTIGATION + TEST. **The item is STALE**: per-tick site-inventory spoilage has been LIVE since Iteration 33 itself (d415b8c) — block 10 of `tick_loop` rots every perishable stock each tick (`quantity * spoilage_rate * season_modifier`; grain rate 0.001/tick; season modifiers Spring 0.8 / Summer 1.2 / Autumn 0.6 / Winter 0.3 — all positive, so decay is guaranteed in every window). What remained was ZERO test coverage of the mechanism. Fix (test-only; golden + all 15 snapshots byte-identical with NO regeneration): new integration test `site_inventory_rots_each_tick_while_stable_resources_do_not` — an isolation-chamber design: seed perishable grain + non-perishable water (spoilage_rate 0, the exact-stability control) into a non-Farm/non-Well site. The chamber is provably drained by NOTHING but spoilage: consumption is Farm/Well-only (`accessible_farm_with_grain`/`accessible_well_with_water`), production writes are Farm/Well-only, theft never fires in calibrated windows (0 NormViolated, probe-pinned), and 100 total stock sits under the smallest storage capacity (200, no overflow confound). Asserts: grain strictly decays from its seeded level over 1000 ticks but is not wiped (~30–50% expected), water stays EXACTLY equal (the strongest isolation signal — any future drain on a non-Well site SHOULD fail it), and a same-seed replay is byte-identical (the spoilage path consumes no RNG — it cannot drift the golden baseline). Reviewer action items applied: the decay assertion is RELATIVE to the captured seeded stock (`grain_after < grain_before`, immune to a future world_gen change that pre-seeds the chamber), and the doc comment now states production is Farm/Well-only too. Full gate green: **855 sim + 247 integration + golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,123 workspace tests (+1 integration). §5 (Beyond AP2) now has its first closed row; remaining §5 rows (multi-settlement, technology tree, weather, legal/education/religious/military systems, TUI interactivity, rendering, save/load UI) stay queued.
- **Iteration 145 (AP2 §4 — Level-of-Detail: the tier-mix envelope MEASURED and pinned across population sizes; the LAST section-4 queue item — section 4 is now COMPLETE; zero-blast test iteration)**: the §4 LOD row — "the §17.1 Focal/Secondary/Background calibration is not validated against a population mix" — is closed by MEASURING the mix at 6/12/24/48 agents (the MAX_POPULATION cap) across seeds 1/42/7/99 to 1000 ticks. **KEY MEASURED FINDING (the honest deliverable)**: the §17.1 LOD gradient does NOT materialize — reclassify is promotion-heavy. Measured mixes: 6 agents = 6F/0S (all-Focal even at the small-world limit); 12 = 10F/2S; 24 = 22F/2S; 48 (both seeds) = 48F/0S (all-Focal at the cap); **Background NEVER appears at any tested size**. At the 48-cap everyone is Focal, so the tier system provides zero cognitive-cost gradient — the LOD calibration is effectively dormant and is queued (NOT shipped — it would be a calibrated change) for a future rebalance of the reclassify promotion criteria. Fix (test-only; golden + all 15 snapshots byte-identical with NO regeneration): new integration test `tier_mix_envelope_pinned_across_population_sizes` — Leg 1 envelope pins FOR THE PINNED SEEDS (explicit regression guards, not a statistical claim): tier sum == population, Focal > 0 everywhere, Background == 0 everywhere (the headline finding PINNED — reviewer action item: the first draft only checked `focal >= n−2`, which would pass if Background appeared at 48; the explicit `background == 0` assert now guards the finding), all-Focal pins at ≤6 and ≥48 (`focal >= n−2`), mid-size pin at 12/24 (`focal in [n−4, n)`); Leg 2 determinism (seed 42 @ 48 agents run twice → identical mixes; the codebase's 3-leg discipline — reviewer action item). Reviewer also corrected the test NAME (`tier_mix_is_sane_...` → `tier_mix_envelope_pinned_...`) — asserting "sane" while measuring a dormant calibration would misrepresent the finding. Full gate green: **855 sim + 246 integration + golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,122 workspace tests (+1 integration). **Section 4 (Optimization — Performance & Scale) is now COMPLETE: benches (141) + O(n²) passes (142) + meme aggregation (143) + cognitive budget (144) + LOD (145) — with two documented, queued calibration findings (the dead `runs_social_interactions` gate from Iter-144 and the dormant tier calibration from this iteration).**
- **Iteration 144 (AP2 §4 — background-tier behavior MEASURED: the wired §17 gates proven to have teeth in live runs + the Secondary→Background demotion path pinned; zero-blast test iteration)**: the §4 queue flagged that `AgentTier`/`CognitiveBudgetTracker` are exercised only implicitly — no test proved a Background agent actually SKIPS its gated systems. Fix (test-only; golden + all 15 snapshots byte-identical with NO regeneration): (1) agent_tier.rs +2 unit tests — `reclassify_demotes_secondary_to_background_on_low_relevance` (near-zero narrative importance + relationship_count < 2 → Background, and the Background budget's zero memory/prospection/social-inference caps follow, so the per-tick gates bite) and `reclassify_keeps_secondary_when_relationships_exist` (relationship_count >= 2 blocks the demotion — an agent with friends is not aggregate material); (2) integration test `background_tier_agents_skip_memory_encoding_in_live_runs` — runs seed-42 to 2000 ticks, forces agent 0 → Background (tier + budget + tracker reset; `last_tier_reassign_tick = u64::MAX` defeats reclassify's interval guard so the tier pins through the window) and agent 1 → Focal (full budget, same pin), runs 500 more ticks, asserts the Background agent's memory is EXACTLY frozen while the Focal control encodes NEW traces. **MEASURED GATE CONTRACT**: the first draft asserted net memory growth and FAILED with 200→176 — the store sits at capacity 200, so eviction hides net growth; the honest signal is newly-encoded traces (`trace.tick > window_start`, window start bound to `sim.current_tick()` per reviewer, not a magic literal). The capacity-trap correction is itself a finding: memory stores saturate at 200 and encode→evict churns, so count-based assertions must count new traces, not totals. **REVIEWER-CAUGHT DISCOVERY (documented, queued — NOT shipped)**: `runs_social_interactions()` is defined (Focal|Secondary) but has ZERO call sites in sim.rs — Background agents still participate in FULL social interactions today, contradicting the §17.1 "aggregate/minimal" tier design. Wiring the gate is a CALIBRATED change (would regenerate golden/snapshots) and is queued in the RWR; this iteration honestly measures what IS gated (memory encoding, belief updates, relationship updates, full psychology, prospection, narrative) and flags the exception. Full gate green: **855 sim + 245 integration + golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,121 workspace tests (+3: 2 sim + 1 integration).
- **Iteration 143 (AP2 §4 — lazy meme aggregation: the daily O(agents×categories) + O(agents×households) input rebuild ELIMINATED; the queue's §17.4 sparse/lazy strategy)**: `wire_meme_aggregation` ran EVERY day (every 144 ticks) but `MemeAggregator::aggregate` gates internally to every 10 ticks — so 9 of 10 daily passes rebuilt the full input set (per-agent meme host lists over every cultural category, agent→household assignments via `.position()` linear scans over all households, meme id/charge vecs, group labels, centrality) and then `aggregate` immediately early-returned its cached metrics. Fix: the exposure-sample flush stays DAILY (the tick loop pushes samples every tick — gating that would lose observability data; `aggregate` wipes `cached_metrics.exposure_samples` on compute ticks so the daily flush is what keeps samples visible between aggregations), but the expensive input builds + the `aggregate` call are gated behind `MemeAggregator::should_compute(tick)` — a NEW single-source-of-truth method (`tick == 0 || should_aggregate(tick)`) that `aggregate()` itself now uses for its internal gate, so the sim gate and the aggregator can never silently diverge (reviewer action item). `aggregate` is now invoked only on ticks where it actually computes (tick 0 + every 1440 ticks, since daily ticks are multiples of 144 and the interval is 10), with identical inputs — behavior-preserving by construction, proven by the byte-identical golden + 15 snapshots with NO regeneration. Tests (2 new): `off_schedule_aggregate_is_mutation_free` — pins that a non-scheduled `aggregate()` call leaves `computed_tick`, `total_active_memes`, and `last_aggregation_tick` untouched (the exact contract the sim-side skip relies on); `tick_zero_always_initializes_despite_should_aggregate_false` — pins the asymmetry (`should_aggregate(0)` false, yet `aggregate(0)` must compute and advance `last_aggregation_tick`) that the gate's `tick == 0` clause encodes. Full gate green: **853 sim + 244 integration + golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,118 workspace tests (+2 sim).
- **Iteration 142 (AP2 §4 — the daily rumor pass's O(n²) trust-matrix build ELIMINATED; the queue's first O(n²) fix)**: every 144-tick daily pass allocated an n×n `vec![vec![Fixed::ZERO; n]; n]` trust matrix and re-filled it from the packed per-agent `relationship_v2s` — pure duplication of data already in memory (each agent already holds n−1 entries covering every pair). Fix: (1) `rumor_v2.rs` — new `transmission_pass_lazy<F: Fn(usize, usize) -> Fixed>` takes `n` + a trust-lookup closure; the old `transmission_pass(&[Vec<Fixed>])` becomes a thin delegate passing `|l, s| matrix[l][s]` (public API preserved; all existing callers and tests untouched). (2) `sim.rs` — the daily pass now passes a `move` closure reading `relationship_v2s` via the existing `relationship_v2_pos` packing formula directly (diagonal → ZERO exactly as the old unfilled cells; missing entries for newborns degrade to ZERO via `.get()`, matching the old matrix's unfilled cells) — the n×n allocation + O(n²) fill is gone; the pass reads trust O(1) per (rumor, listener) scan, the same count as the matrix reads it replaces. Also added an `.any(|r| r.active)` guard so the whole pass (and its susceptibility/skepticism/escalation builds) is skipped when no rumor is live — behavior-preserving since `transmission_pass` skips inactive rumors internally and returns only hops (discarded at the call site). **BYTE-IDENTICAL BY CONSTRUCTION + PROVEN**: the mapping math (`relationship_v2_pos` vs the old pos→source formula) verified, and the golden baseline + all 15 snapshots pass with NO regeneration. Tests: new `lazy_pass_is_bit_identical_to_matrix_pass` — Leg 1 cross-equality (matrix vs lazy on 2 rumors/4 agents: hops, source_chain, believer_count, prevalence, active all equal); Leg 2 hard-coded anchors (2 hops; rumor 0 chain [0,1] at trust 0.9; rumor 1 chain [3,0] — tie-break to lowest index — pinning the shared body so a refactor that keeps both variants "equal but wrong" fails); Leg 3 closure-consulted proof (a ZERO-trust closure suppresses the untargeted rumor while the targeted rumor still spreads at its 0.1 target-proximity floor — `chance = base + target_bonus` — proving the pass genuinely reads the closure's values). Reviewer-caught fixes folded: the first draft's anchors were wrong (probe showed 2 hops not 1 — both rumors transmit) and the ZERO-trust leg was wrong (targeted rumors have a trust-independent 0.1 floor); both corrected from actual measured behavior. Full gate green: **851 sim + 244 integration + golden byte-identical + 15 snapshots + strict clippy `-D warnings` clean**. 1,116 workspace tests (+1 sim).

- **Iteration 132 (scale validation — the queue §2 "population scale untested" gap CLOSED)**: every prior test ran ≤ 48 agents but NONE validated behavior AT full capacity. The probe revealed the sim's DESIGNED settlement size is the §19.5.F population cap — `MAX_POPULATION = 48` (population_cap.rs), enforced at populate (sim.rs:680 clamps over-requests) and at every birth gate (sim.rs:6632/6645/6673). Probing 100/200-agent worlds showed the cap works perfectly: over-requested populations converge to exactly 48 with byte-identical metrics to a 48-agent run (the clamp is a pure `min` — same seed → same trajectory). Fix: new `crates/mindstrata-tests/src/scale_tests.rs` (registered in lib.rs) with 3 tests — (1) `population_cap_enforced_at_populate` (request 100/200 → exactly 48; at-cap and below-cap pass through); (2) `full_capacity_population_stays_capped_and_invariant_clean` (600 manual ticks at 48 agents in a 32×32 world, sampled every 100: count never exceeds the cap, never collapses below cap/4 (a collapse-DETECTOR, not a stability contract), resources never negative, ALL 22 emotions bounded to [0,1] — reviewer-extended from the base 8); (3) `full_capacity_run_is_deterministic_and_healthy` (48×500 twice → identical hunger/valence/grain/events/agent_count + end-state health). **VACUITY DISCOVERY (reviewer-forced, probe-pinned)**: at seed 42 the 48-agent village is PERFECTLY HEALTHY — agent_count sits at exactly 48 at every 100-tick sample through 2000 ticks, i.e. no deaths occur in-window so the birth gates never fire there; the churn assertion the reviewer requested was dropped with honest documentation (the through-births cap check under churn is exercised by the famine/pestilence integration tests, which re-pace births under mortality). **Zero baseline blast by construction**: pure test additions — **849 sim + 229 integration green (+3 scale); golden byte-identical; all 14 snapshots byte-identical; strict clippy `-D warnings` clean.** The §2 "Population scale untested" row is retired.
