Fixes: (1) `cargo clippy --fix` auto-applications (~23 suggestions across actions.rs, sim.rs, snapshot.rs, skill.rs, decision_policy.rs, theory_of_mind.rs, legitimacy.rs, factions.rs, ecology.rs, health.rs, market.rs, neural_like.rs, attention.rs, agent_lifetime_trace.rs, property_tests.rs, integration_tests.rs); (2) **25 manual `field_reassign_with_default` fixes** (struct-literal initializers with `..Default::default()` in the biology/psychology/social test modules — attention ×4, immune ×2, attachment ×2, cultural_cognition ×2, developmental ×2, epistemic ×4, cardiovascular/circadian/development/metabolism/musculoskeletal/cognitive_runtime/decision_policy/narrative/psychopathology ×1 each); (3) the sim.rs:2940 §11.1 status-loop refactor — the 70-line `for i in 0..agents.len()` became `for (i, agent) in self.agents.iter_mut().enumerate()` (the index is still needed for `AgentId::new(i)`, the `emotions[i]` parallel-array reads, and `crime_record(i)`), with the nested kinship scan's cross-agent `agents[j].age` read precomputed into a local `agent_ages: Vec<Fixed>` (disjoint-borrow resolution — pure reads, deterministic); (4) `sort_by_key` with `std::cmp::Reverse` for the descending salience/retrieval-score sorts (attention.rs:437, memory.rs:487 — the memory one also halves the retrieval_score calls per comparison); (5) `clone_from` for the council-member assignment (sim.rs:9469); (6) the tautological `mean > 0.5 && mean > 0.04 × 10` (0.4 < 0.5, RHS dead) simplified to the stronger `mean > 0.5` anchor with the subsumption documented; (7) bench crate-level `#![allow(missing_docs)]` for the criterion-generated harness fns; (8) doc-comment/blank-line hygiene. **PROVABLY BEHAVIOR-NEUTRAL**: the strict gate + full suites verify the sweep changed nothing — **849 sim + 226 integration green (+1 ignored); golden byte-identical; all 14 snapshots byte-identical; NO regeneration, NO re-pins.** The §3 "Clippy debt" row is now retired (93 → 0 lines).

- **Iteration 130 (AP2 §8.1.4 — the fourth and FINAL CALIBRATED consumer: awe → scandal-damage reverence; the §8.1.4 emotion family is now COMPLETE)**: the Iter-129 audit left ONE live-producer emotion unwired (awe, producer `|future_implication| × identity_relevance × (1 − expectedness)` — the overwhelming-significance appraisal). Awe → ritual was rejected (monthly rituals inert ≤2000; would disturb the §13.6 cluster-bonding calibration) and awe → conservatism was a near-saturation dead signal; awe → §11.1 scandal erosion is semantically perfect — the awe-struck population experiences institutional failings as smaller than they are ("reverence forgives", the daily erosion's emotional counterweight to the monthly ritual boost, completing the §11.1 legitimacy loop) and deterministic (pure function of the emotion, no RNG). Fix: (1) `AWE_REVERENCE_RATE` (0.15) + `AWE_REVERENCE_FLOOR` (0.85) + the public pure helper `appraisal::awe_reverence_factor(awe, rate, floor)` = `1 − awe × rate` floored (ONE-SIDED identity-at-zero, never fully cancels erosion — same never-zero design as the Iter-122 despair pacifier); (2) sim.rs — the daily §11.1 moral-cognition `scandal_damage` call multiplies the witnessed-violation severity by the factor. **WIRING DISCOVERY (the iteration's key finding)**: awe CONVERGES to a world-determined equilibrium (~0.67 by tick 130) regardless of the start value, and scandal erosion only begins ~tick 120 (anger must accumulate first) — a one-shot injection differential is ERASED before erosion starts (probed: both runs land at identical legitimacy, focal agents floor at 0 by ~tick 2000, non-focal agents never run moral cognition). The sim-level wiring test therefore uses a manual-tick harness that re-injects awe=1.0 before EVERY tick (treatment factor pinned 0.85, control natural 0.899 — scandal-site probe-verified), asserting the reverent run preserves strictly more legitimacy. **REVIEWER-CORRECTED BLAST FRAMING**: the producer is U-SHAPED on `|future_implication|` (= 1 − threat − need_pressure), so awe fires on significance in EITHER direction — wonder at fortune in calm worlds, dread of disaster in dire ones — NOT only calm worlds; the zero baseline blast is CONSUMER-side: erosion floors legitimacy at 0 in every calibrated window regardless, the 0.5-anchored grievance/theft consumers never activate (legitimacy < 0.5 after erosion starts), and the continuous motivation-context shift (~0.05 mid-window) stays below decision granularity. Honest observability: liveness is proven by input-pinning (the weakest observability of the §8.1.4 set — relief had the panic re-anchor, nostalgia the decay-delta), and the 20000-tick panic window plus all famine pins pass unchanged. **golden + all 14 snapshots byte-identical, NO regeneration**. Tests: appraisal unit (`awe_reverence_factor_is_identity_at_zero_and_shields` — identity at zero, exact 0.925 at 0.5, exact floor 0.85 at full, floor-binds-at-high-rate); sim-level wiring test `awe_reverence_shields_legitimacy_from_scandal_erosion` (manual-tick re-injection differential, 200 ticks mid-erosion below the floor, same seed/call order); 3-leg integration test `awe_reverence_shields_legitimacy_is_live_and_deterministic` (Leg A producer live mean > 0.5 @5000; Leg B shipped fold contract exact values; Leg C seed-determinism). **Row 2: COMPLETE — all 12 expanded-family §8.1.4 channels live (loneliness, tenderness, humiliation, contempt, despair, envy, disgust, moral_outrage, gratitude, relief, nostalgia, awe — every emotion in the AP2 §8.1.4 family now has a production consumer).** **Blast radius — ZERO baseline drift**: **849 sim + 226 integration green (+1 ignored); golden + 14 snapshots byte-identical with NO regeneration; clippy clean on touched files.**

- **Iteration 129 (AP2 §8.1.4 — the third CALIBRATED consumer: nostalgia → collective-memory preservation; the meaningful-past channel)**: the Iter-128 audit left two live-producer emotions unwired (awe, nostalgia). Nostalgia was chosen over awe: (a) awe → ritual would be observationally inert ≤2000 ticks (seeded rituals fire monthly at 4320) and would disturb the carefully-calibrated §13.6 cluster-bonding in long-horizon tests; (b) nostalgia → conservatism was near-saturated dead-signal; (c) nostalgia → collective-memory decay is semantically perfect (producer `narrative_meaning × positive` = the meaningful-past appraisal; consumer = the daily §13.5 collective-memory decay — a nostalgic village keeps its shared past alive) and deterministic (pure mean, no RNG). Fix: (1) `NOSTALGIA_PRESERVATION_RATE` (0.3) + `NOSTALGIA_PRESERVATION_FLOOR` (0.7) + the public pure helper `appraisal::nostalgia_preservation_factor(nostalgia, rate, floor)` = `1 − nostalgia × rate` floored (ONE-SIDED identity-at-zero, mirrors the Iter-122 despair pacifier's never-zero floor design); (2) collective_memory.rs — `decay_preserved` / `tick_decay_preserved` / `tick_all_preserved` scale the linear 0.001/day fade by the factor, legacy methods delegate with `Fixed::ONE` (public-path consumer); (3) sim.rs — the daily collective-memory pass computes the population mean nostalgia and passes the preservation factor into `tick_all_preserved`. **FLOOR-PINNING FINDING (the iteration's key discovery)**: the daily pass reads the emotion field at a point where nostalgia SATURATES at 1.0 (mid-tick, after appraisal accumulation) — the factor pins at exactly the floor 0.7. This is a **UNIFORM 30% slowdown in every calibrated window — a floor-pinned, NOT differential consumer** (unlike gratitude/relief): the near-saturation dead-signal risk flagged in planning applies; the honest value is the baseline memory-preservation effect (long-horizon memories fade measurably slower), and stress/famine worlds where nostalgia drops would show the differential. ZERO baseline blast by construction: collective-memory salience is NOT in the golden metric hash nor any snapshot (verified: golden_replay.rs hashes metrics + agent data; snapshot_tests.rs has no collective-memory fields) — **golden + all 14 snapshots byte-identical, NO regeneration**. Tests: appraisal unit (`nostalgia_preservation_factor_is_identity_at_zero_and_floored` — identity at zero, exact 0.85 at 0.5, exact floor 0.7 at full, floor-binds-at-high-rate); collective_memory unit (`decay_preserved_scales_the_linear_daily_fade` — identity 0.499 vs floor 0.4993); sim-level wiring test `nostalgia_preserves_collective_memory_salience` — runs past two daily boundaries (5040/5184, no ritual in window, famine episode-guarded, `refresh_derived_views` verified read-only on salience), asserts decayed < un-scaled 2×0.001×count (fold applied) AND `|decayed − floor-scaled| ≤ 0.0002` (production factor pinned AT the floor — a drift to 0.85 would fail), plus seed-determinism; 3-leg integration test `nostalgia_preserves_collective_memory_is_live_and_deterministic` (Leg A producer live mean > 0.5; Leg B shipped fold contract + public-path `decay_preserved` consumer; Leg C seed-determinism). **Row 2: TEN §8.1.4 channels live; the unwired remainder is ONE live producer (awe) — the LAST §8.1.4 emotion.** **Blast radius — ZERO baseline drift**: **847 sim + 225 integration green (+1 ignored); golden + 14 snapshots byte-identical with NO regeneration; clippy clean on touched files; benches build.**

- **Iteration 128 (AP2 §8.1.4 — the second CALIBRATED consumer: relief → escalation amplifier; the emboldened-survivor channel)**: the Iter-127 audit left three live-producer emotions unwired (awe, relief, nostalgia). Relief was chosen — the appraisal producer `positive × (1 − controllability)` (the uncontrollable-outcome-turns-positive lucky-escape appraisal) is unambiguous psychology, and its natural consumer is the §19.5.H failed-threat escalation decision as the emotional COUNTERPOINT to the Iter-122 despair pacifier (despair = hopelessness suppresses escalation; relief = post-danger risk-acceptance emboldens it). Fix: (1) `RELIEF_ESCALATION_RATE` (0.1) + the public pure helper `appraisal::relief_escalation_factor(relief, rate)` = `1 + relief × rate` (ONE-SIDED identity-at-zero, NO clamp per the Iter-112 lesson); (2) the `should_escalate` chance chain now multiplies `× relief_factor` AFTER the Iter-125 outrage factor and BEFORE the despair pacifier; the RNG draw stays unconditional (same stream position → replay determinism at every relief value, only the comparison threshold changes). **TUNING LESSON (the iteration's key discovery)**: the FIRST shipped rate was 0.2 (factor 1.20 at full relief), but the probe revealed a DISPROPORTIONATE blast — the panic fire moved 17713→1408 (12.5× earlier), kin seed-42 inverted, famine early-mortality +7 — because the escalation chain has strong feedback (violence→stress→more conflicts), so a LIVE emotion (unlike the zero-blast humiliation/contempt/outrage producers) multiplies far beyond its face value. **Tuned to 0.1 (factor 1.10 at full relief)**: the re-probe showed the famine curve BYTE-IDENTICAL to the Iter-127 pins (1,5,2,2,5,4), all 4 kin seeds strictly buffered (0.458<0.976, 0.848<0.998, 0.838<0.959, 0.698<0.998), the belief gaps intact (0.524 vs 0.063, fear byte-identical), and the conception birth ≥2000 intact — ONLY the seed-42 panic fire moved (17713→18577, re-anchored to [18400,18700]) and the stress-conflict statistical band widened 0.05→0.07 (probe-pinned 0.357 vs 0.417 = 0.060, documented re-pin per that test's established protocol — Iter-118 set the 0.05 band the same way). **GOLDEN + ALL 14 SNAPSHOTS PASS WITHOUT REGENERATION** — the 0.1 rate keeps the deterministic baselines byte-identical while producing live emergent change. **SELECTIVITY MECHANISM (why a live calibrated fold has zero baseline drift)**: relief's producer needs POSITIVE outcomes, which are rare in famine/stress windows — relief ≈ 0 there → factor ≈ 1.0 → zero drift; relief is live only in RECOVERY windows (0.1-rate probe: mean > 0.5 at 5000 on seed 42; the pre-tune 0.2-rate probe measured 0.65–0.79), so the fold surfaces as emergent change in long-horizon runs (the panic shift). Despair is anti-correlated (live in stress windows, zero in recovery) — the two factors never silently cancel in the same window. Tests: appraisal unit (`relief_escalation_factor_is_identity_at_zero_and_amplifies` — identity at zero, exact 1.05 at 0.5, exact 1.10 at full); sim identical-RNG wiring test `relief_amplifies_escalation_outcomes` (two same-seed worlds differ only in the aggressor's relief 1.0 vs 0.0, 500 alternating `should_escalate` draws at the same stream position — the relieved aggressor escalates strictly more); 3-leg integration test `relief_escalation_amplifier_is_live_and_deterministic` (Leg A producer reach: relief mean > 0.5; Leg B the shipped fold contract via the public helper; Leg C seed-determinism). **Row 2: NINE §8.1.4 channels live; the unwired remainder is TWO live producers (awe, nostalgia).** **Blast radius — ZERO baseline drift, two intent-preserving re-anchors**: **844 sim + 224 integration green (+1 ignored); golden + 14 snapshots byte-identical with NO regeneration; clippy clean; benches build.**

- **Iteration 125 (AP2 §8.1.4 — the last zero-blast emotion channel: moral outrage → failed-threat escalation amplifier; the ARMED-not-absent channel)**: the audit confirmed `emotions.moral_outrage` was the last producer-only emotion in the §8.1.4 family — producers live (appraisal.rs:316 `sacredness_violation × goal_relevance`, accumulated sim.rs:3939, Iter-116-decayed sim.rs:4019) but ZERO consumers (sim.rs:9141 reads the DIFFERENT `moral_values.moral_outrage()` trait method, not the emotion). Since the producer is `witnessed_unfairness × max_sacredness × goal_relevance` — which fires at ANY positive unfairness (there is NO 0.05 gate on this channel; that threshold only flips the sign of the separate `fairness` appraisal field) — the exact-zero pin rigorously proves `witnessed_unfairness == 0` exactly: **calm worlds witness no injustice, the channel is ARMED and waiting** (reviewer-caught mechanism correction: the initial doc claim of a 0.05 threshold on this channel was wrong — 0.05 gates only the fairness-field sign). - **Iteration 124 (AP2 §8.1.5 — fixing the audit-found motivation-context dead channel: the §8.1.5 emotional pressure amplifications now receive the live emotion state — the iteration that ended up ZERO-blast despite being queued as calibrated)**: the gap was audit-found in Iter-123 — sim.rs:2510-2513 read `self.agents[i].emotions.fear/anger/joy/sadness` (the field EMPTIED by the tick's `std::mem::take` parallel-array setup at sim.rs:2337, written back only at step-8 sim.rs:4105) into `MotivationState::set_context`, so the §8.1.5 pressure amplifications (`1 + fear×0.4` Safety/Certainty, `1 + anger×0.4` Justice, `1 + joy×0.3` Play/Belonging/Romance, `1 − sadness×0.3` Meaning floored 0.5) ran with an emotionless context since the parallel-array refactor — the dominant-need machinery has been emotionally blind. Probe BEFORE: `motivation.fear` mean 0.0000 across seeds 1/42/99 at 1000–10000 ticks. Probe AFTER: `motivation.fear` mean 0.831–0.999 (the agent emotion field genuinely saturates — fear max 1.0) and joy 0.65–0.89. Fix: the 4 reads now use the LIVE LOCAL `emotions[i]` vec (in scope at the site, the same parallel-array pattern as the Iter-123 attraction fix) — semantic: the local vec holds the value taken at tick start, i.e. the PREVIOUS tick's accumulated writeback, "yesterday's emotion drives today's motivation", matching how the emotion machinery itself treats the vec. **SURPRISING OUTCOME — zero-blast despite the massive data change**: the full gate is green with ZERO regeneration (839 sim + 220 integration + golden byte-identical + 14 snapshots byte-identical + clippy clean + benches; G7 confirms zero golden/snapshot diff lines). The claim is scoped honestly: a world where Safety is NOT maxed (or a threshold-based decision reading raw pressure magnitudes) could flip — that remains the future calibration surface. Test `motivation_emotional_context_is_live` — Leg A pins mean `motivation.fear > 0.5` && mean joy > 0.3 at seed 42 @5000 (context live; pre-fix 0.0000); Leg B pins summed `pressure_full(Safety)` > summed raw safety pressure (net amplification present); Leg B2 zeroes the live fear on a cloned `MotivationState` and asserts `pressure_full(Safety)` strictly drops (direct amplification proof); Leg C pins seed-determinism of the amplified sum. **QUEUED CALIBRATION CONCERN (flagged, not shipped)**: the probe's fear mean 0.83–1.0 / max 1.0 in CALM worlds suggests the 8 base emotions lack the Iter-116 proportional decay (which covers the expanded families only) — the amplification factor is pinned near its 1.4 ceiling for everyone, erasing differentiation (the familiar dead-signal pattern). A core-emotion decay iteration is queued. **Blast radius — ZERO despite the "calibrated" queue**: **839 sim + 220 integration green (+1 ignored); golden byte-identical with NO regeneration; 14 snapshots green with NO regeneration; clippy clean on changed regions; benches build.**

- **Iteration 123 (AP2 §8.1.4 — envy → courtship-interest aversion: the last zero-blast producer-only emotion channel closes, plus a LATENT-DEAD-CHANNEL fix to the Iter-78 disgust channel discovered during debugging)**: the gap was audit-confirmed — envy was the only remaining §8.1.4 emotion that is both producer-only (disgust already feeds attraction, jealousy feeds bond load, humiliation/contempt/despair feed escalation) and provably identically-zero in calibrated windows (Iter-122 Leg C pinned every seed-42 golden agent at envy == 0). Design: (1) attraction.rs — new `envy_cost` field (`#[serde(default)]`, old saves restore 0) + `update_envy_cost` (clamp_01 mirror of `update_moral_disgust`) + `+ envy_cost × 0.2` in `total_attraction()`'s negative channel (same weight tier as moral_disgust — coveting poisons courtship interest like purity-aversion; at full envy the 0.2 removal can flip a borderline profile under the 0.4 D4 gate — documented as the intended effect) + 2 unit tests (exact 0.2 removal at full cost; mirror/clamp/identity-at-zero); (2) sim.rs — the per-tick attraction pass writes the channel from the LIVE LOCAL emotions vec (`emotions[i].envy`). **CRITICAL DEBUGGING FINDING**: the tick empties every agent's emotions via `std::mem::take` at the step-0b parallel-array setup (sim.rs:2337) and writes back only at step-8 (sim.rs:4105) — so the pre-existing Iter-78 line `update_moral_disgust(self.agents[i].emotions.disgust)` was reading the EMPTIED agent field: `moral_disgust` was pinned at 0 in production despite live disgust producers — a latent dead channel. Fixed to read `emotions[i].disgust` (behaviorally identical in calm windows — disgust is 0 there — so still zero-blast, but the channel becomes genuinely live when purity violations fire); (3) integration test `envy_poisons_courtship_interest_zero_blast_in_golden_window` — Leg A pins every seed-42 golden agent at `attraction.envy_cost == 0` at 5000 ticks (byte-identical); Leg B injects envy=1.0 at tick 100 and runs exactly ONE tick (deliberately short: the Iter-116 per-tick decay 0.12/tick erodes the injection to ~0 within 50 ticks — an earlier 100-tick horizon failed with min 0.0000, a test-design bug, not a sim bug) and asserts min cost > 0.9 (the fold reads the live local vec at the injected pre-decay value); Leg C same-horizon differential (control at tick 101) — the envious world courts strictly less (> 0.1 gap = the 1.0 cost × 0.2 weight on every profile). **AUDIT FINDING (queued for a dedicated calibrated iteration, not shipped here)**: the same mem::take audit surfaced a SECOND latent dead channel — sim.rs:2510-2513 read `self.agents[i].emotions.fear/anger/joy/sadness` (the emptied field) into `MotivationState::set_context`, so the §8.1.5 pressure boosts (`1 + fear×0.4` Safety, `1 + anger×0.4` Justice, `1 + joy×0.3` Romance, `1 − sadness×0.3` Meaning) have been operating with an emotionless context since the parallel-array refactor. Those four emotions ARE live in calibrated windows, so the fix is a calibrated change (golden + snapshot regeneration) — queued as a dedicated iteration. **ZERO-blast iteration** (envy probe-pinned identically zero in every calibrated window; disgust likewise — both channels write exactly 0 in the golden/snapshot horizons): **839 sim + 219 integration green (+1 ignored); golden byte-identical with NO regeneration; 14 snapshots green with NO regeneration; clippy clean on changed regions; benches build.**

- **Iteration 122 (AP2 §8.1.4 — contempt amplifier + despair pacifier close two producer-only secondary-emotion channels on the failed-threat escalation decision)**: the gap was audit-confirmed — all 6 §8.1.4 secondary emotions (contempt/awe/envy/relief/despair/nostalgia) were write-only observational state (the `DiscreteEmotions` field doc literally says so): computed as appraisal deltas, accumulated, decayed, and never consumed by any decision. Probe: contempt/envy/despair are identically ZERO in every calibrated window (their appraisal inputs — `(1 − status_threat) × unfair`, `status_threat × incongruent`, `future_negative × (1 − coping)` — never fire in calm worlds), while awe/relief/nostalgia hover high (0.51–0.88). Design: (1) appraisal.rs — `CONTEMPT_ESCALATION_RATE` 0.3 + pure `contempt_escalation_factor` (1 + contempt×rate, NO clamp — the Iter-112 lesson) mirroring the Iter-116 humiliation helper exactly; `DESPAIR_PACIFY_RATE` 0.5 + `DESPAIR_PACIFY_FLOOR` 0.5 + pure `despair_pacify_factor` ((1 − despair×rate).max(floor)) with the never-zero floor design of the Iter-110 trust pacifier (hopelessness demobilizes but never erases escalation) + 2 unit tests pinning exact decimal math (identity at zero exactly 1.0; 1.15@0.5 / 1.30@1.0 for contempt; 0.75@0.5 / floor 0.5@1.0 and floor-binds for despair); (2) sim.rs — both factors multiply the `should_escalate` chance chain (contempt as the SECOND amplifier alongside humiliation, despair as the pacifier counterpoint; the RNG draw stays unconditional — same stream position — so replay determinism holds at every value, only the comparison threshold changes; multipliers compose multiplicatively — a contemptuous+despairing aggressor nets ×0.65, despair dominates, documented with the violent-despair counter-hypothesis noted as a future calibration knob; the combined-amplifier ceiling is clamped at 1.0 by `escalation_chance`); (3) sim unit tests — `contempt_amplifies_escalation_outcomes` + `despair_pacifies_escalation_outcomes` (two same-seed worlds differing only in the aggressor's `emotions.contempt`/`emotions.despair` 1.0 vs 0.0, 500 draws each, deterministic gap — mirrors the Iter-110 trust test, with the non-vacuous control>0 guard); (4) integration test `secondary_emotions_fold_is_zero_blast_in_golden_window` (Leg A pins every seed-42 golden agent at contempt==0 && despair==0 at 5000 ticks — the factors are exactly 1.0 so the chain is byte-identical; Leg B proves awe/relief/nostalgia are LIVE producers (mean > 0.3) — documenting exactly why they were NOT folded (a consumer on them would be a calibrated change, not zero-blast); Leg C pins envy==0 too and notes its natural consumer is the relationship/patronage layer, not failed-threat escalation). **ZERO-blast iteration** (both emotions are probe-pinned identically zero in every calibrated window, so both factors are exactly 1.0 throughout the golden/snapshot horizons by construction): **837 sim + 218 integration green (+1 ignored); golden byte-identical with NO regeneration; 14 snapshots green with NO regeneration; clippy clean on changed regions; benches build.**

- **Iteration 119 (AP2 §10.7 — household food pooling: division of labor + childcare/elder care now decisional — remaining-work row 13 CLOSED)**: the §10.7 consumer gap was audit-confirmed — `pool_food`/`distribute_food` had ZERO production call sites and `HouseholdRole` had ZERO consumers anywhere, so the plan's resource-pooling / division-of-labor / childcare / elder-care dimensions were producer-side only (`derive_roles`/`collect_traditions`/`tick_update` wired since Iter 53, never read). Reviewer fixes folded in: `HouseholdRole::Dependent` included in the dependent predicate (with a doc note that `derive_roles` never assigns it), a roles/members length guard on the pub method (malformed/deserialized households can't panic), and the fold's doc now states the savings-only famine semantics explicitly (contributions are inverse to need — the fold is an emergency buffer, not subsistence). **Blast radius — ZERO**: **826 sim + 215 integration green (+1 ignored); golden byte-identical with NO regeneration; 14 snapshots green with NO regeneration; clippy clean on changed regions; benches build.**

- **Iteration 116 (AP2 §8.1.4 — secondary emotions: the write-only expanded emotion families get a decay and a genuine decisional consumer)**: the 14 expanded emotion families (contempt/awe/envy/humiliation/relief/despair/nostalgia/gratitude/jealousy/loneliness/tenderness/moral_outrage/disgust/hope) were **produced live but write-only** — the base 8 emotions decay subtractively (0.002/tick, §22.1) but the expanded families had **NO decay at all**: the produced families (awe/relief/hope/gratitude/nostalgia) were pinned at exactly 1.0 in every calibrated run (probe-pinned: they climb ~0.08/tick and saturate by tick 20), and the unproduced families (humiliation/envy/contempt/despair/moral_outrage/disgust/jealousy) sat at exactly 0. Design: (1) appraisal.rs — `SECONDARY_EMOTION_DECAY_RATE` (0.12/tick, probe-calibrated: the appraisal runs EVERY tick — the per-tick emotion path, not the daily phase — so a daily decay was probed ~40× too weak and iterated to per-tick; probe-pinned post-wiring steady states across seeds 1/7/42/99 @ 5000: awe 0.51–0.61, relief 0.65–0.79, hope 0.82–0.88, gratitude/nostalgia 0.88), `HUMILIATION_ESCALATION_RATE` (0.3 → factor `1 + humiliation×0.3`, cap 1.30), pure `secondary_emotion_decay(value, rate)` (value × (1−rate), no RNG) and `humiliation_escalation_factor(humiliation, rate)` (**no clamp — the Iter-112 lesson**: the factor provably exceeds 1.0, `clamp_01` would silently erase the amplification). (2) sim.rs — the proportional decay applies **every tick** to the **12 NON-consumed** families; `loneliness` (Iter-98 social-seeking) and `tenderness` (Iter-99 helping) are **exempt** — their consumers were calibrated against the saturated state (the Iter-115 birth-pipeline lesson); the stale "write-only observational state" comment updated. The **decisional consumer**: the humiliation factor folds into `should_escalate` right after the Iter-110 trust / Iter-114 obligation pacifiers — a humiliated agent escalates a failed threat to violence more readily (status-defeat → aggression, the amplifier counterpoint on the same §19.5.H decision). **ONE-SIDED: identity at zero** — the probe shows humiliation is NEVER produced in calibrated windows (mean/max 0.0000 across seeds and scenarios; its appraisal inputs — status threat + identity relevance — don't fire in calm worlds), so the factor is exactly 1.0 throughout the golden/snapshot horizons (provably zero-blast). The RNG draw in `should_escalate` stays unconditional → replay determinism at every humiliation value. (3) Unit tests: `secondary_emotion_decay_is_proportional_and_exact` (explicit rates, exact 0.4/0.2/0, const band check 1–15%), `humiliation_escalation_factor_is_identity_at_zero_and_amplifies` (identity at zero, exact 1.15@0.5 / 1.30@1.0), and the sim.rs identical-RNG wiring proof `humiliation_amplifies_failed_threat_escalation` (500 `should_escalate` calls per world; humiliation 1.0 → factor 1.30 must escalate **strictly more** than control@0 — **fails if the fold line is ever deleted**; the Iter-114 pattern). (4) Integration test `secondary_emotion_decay_and_humiliation_amplifier_end_to_end`: **Leg A** the decay breaks saturation (awe/relief/hope/gratitude/nostalgia mean < 0.95 and > 0.2 @ 5000 — meaningful producer-driven levels), **Leg B** the never-produced channels stay exactly 0.0 (the amplifier's zero-blast precondition), pure-factor pin (1.30 @ humiliation 1.0). **Blast radius — ZERO**: the decay touches only un-consumed observational state (golden `agent_hash` covers 4 metrics/agent, snapshots serialize only fear/anger/joy — nothing serialized drifts) and the amplifier is identity at the never-produced humiliation. **823 sim + 212 integration green (+1 ignored); golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, no regeneration; clippy clean on changed regions (the 7944 `redundant_comparisons` deny is pre-existing); benches build.**

- **Iteration 115 (AP2 §13.5 per the module's own labeling — the moral-panic lifecycle revival; the dead-machinery opportunity flagged at Iter-112)**: the `MoralPanicRegistry` (register/active/daily_update) and `MoralPanic` (new/escalation_pressure/should_resolve/escalate/deescalate/daily_update) were **never called by the simulation** — the registry stayed empty, `daily_update` was a no-op, and the §7.2 trigger (`tick_moral_panic_and_revolution`) pushed events and applied one-shot legitimacy damage but **never registered a panic object**. Design: (1) moral_panic.rs — `TICKS_PER_DAY` (144), `PANIC_ESCALATION_THRESHOLD` (0.5), `PANIC_FATIGUE_RATE` (0.02/day → fatigue crosses the 0.7 resolve threshold after 35 days), `PANIC_LEGITIMACY_DRAIN_RATE` (0.001 — calibrated, see blast below), pure `panic_fatigue(elapsed_days, rate)` and `panic_legitimacy_drain(intensity, rate)`; the superseded registry-level `daily_update()` deleted. (2) sim.rs — the §7.2 trigger now **registers** a `MoralPanic` (council proposition → `InstitutionalCorruption`, market → `MoralViolation`); the daily site replaces the registry no-op with `tick_moral_panic_lifecycle(tick_u64)`: resolve-first (`should_resolve` — institutional response is never set by any system, so **fatigue is the resolution driver**) → escalate (pressure > 0.5, self-reinforcing: as the panic erodes legitimacy the legitimacy-vacuum term keeps pressure high) → natural decay; then the **decisional consumer**: active intensity × rate erodes the target institution's legitimacy each day ("panic → distrust → more panic") until fatigue resolves the crisis; a same-tick skip guard (start_tick == tick_u64) prevents doubling the §7.2 trigger-day damage. Deterministic — no RNG, replay determinism holds. (3) Unit tests: `panic_fatigue_accrues_with_elapsed_days` (exact 0.2/0.7/1.0), `panic_legitimacy_drain_scales_with_intensity` (exact 0.0003/0.001/0), `panic_lifecycle_escalates_then_resolves_via_fatigue`. (4) Integration test `moral_panic_lifecycle_registers_and_drains_legitimacy_end_to_end`: **Leg A** producer reach (seed-42 panic fires at **3034** — probe-pinned, NOT the ~4320 of the Iter-112 differential scenario; intensity escalates 0.3 → 0.95 by 5000; trigger mapping pinned), **Leg B** public-path exactness, **Leg C** replay determinism, **Leg D the wiring differential** (inject a council panic at tick 2000, run to the 2016 daily tick, the control-vs-injected council legitimacy gap equals the drain of the post-update intensity EXACTLY — fails if the drain line is ever deleted). **Blast radius — BIG-BLAST in panic-frequent worlds (documented, intent-preserving)**: the drain at 0.005 was probed to KILL the courtship→pregnancy→birth pipeline liveness (seed-1 80K: 0 births vs 2 pre-Iter-115 — a liveness regression, not re-pinnable); **0.001 is the calibrated rate** — it preserves the 2-birth chain (re-paced [21860, 45710] → [28230, 35960], a classic intent-preserving recalibration like Iter-99/103/108) and the pestilence world (fires the §7.2 trigger every cooldown — 20 panics by 12K; the cumulative drain collapses legitimacy and the epidemic settles to a low 2-carrier endemic PLATEAU instead of exhausting: the deep-tail pin re-pinned from "deep < mid" to "mid ≥ deep ≥ 1" with the decline-from-onset-peak claim (2 < 6) intact). **The golden (1,000 ticks) and snapshots (≤2,000) never fire a panic → golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, no regeneration.** 820 sim + 211 integration green (+1 ignored); clippy clean on changed regions (the remaining sim.rs:9008 `faction_members.clone()` is pre-existing Iter-89 code); benches build.

- **Iteration 114 (AP2 §10.1.2 — the social field's `social_obligation` feeding a second failed-threat escalation pacifier, COMPLETING row #10: EVERY §10.1 relational-field layer now has a decisional consumer)**: `social_obligation` (mean `rv2.obligation` over the agent's relationship graph, refreshed daily at sim.rs:9139/9178; genuinely live — the producer at sim.rs:3625 writes `effect.obligation_delta` into `rv2.obligation` from interactions, so the field accrues as the relationship web deepens) had **zero decisional consumers**. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `OBLIGATION_RESTRAINT_ANCHOR = 0.5` (the semantic midpoint) + `OBLIGATION_RESTRAINT_RATE = 0.3` (per unit above anchor; full obligation 1.0 → exactly 0.15 reduction) + `OBLIGATION_RESTRAINT_CAP = 0.3` (defensive — never binds with shipped constants; the factor is provably ∈ [0.7, 1.0]) consts, pure `obligation_restraint_factor = (1 − min(max(obligation − anchor, 0) × rate, cap)).clamp_01()` — the clamp is a harmless no-op on a provably-≤1.0 dampener (correctly distinguished from Iter-112's amplifier-clamp bug); module doc updated. (2) sim.rs `should_escalate` — `obligation_factor` multiplies the escalation chance chain right after Iter-110's trust factor: trust = relational confidence ("I believe they won't harm me"), obligation = moral constraint ("I have duties I must honor"); the RNG draw stays unconditional (same stream position), so replay determinism holds at every obligation value — only the comparison threshold changes. (3) Unit test `obligation_restraint_factor_is_identity_below_anchor_and_capped` (identity at anchor + 0.456, exact 0.03 at 0.6, monotone, full obligation 1.0 → 0.85, oversized-rate cap saturation) + the identical-RNG wiring proof `social_obligation_restrains_escalation_outcomes` (500 `should_escalate` calls per world, obligated at 0.9 must escalate strictly less than control at 0 — FAILS if the fold line is ever deleted, proving the wiring end-to-end). (4) Integration test `social_obligation_restrains_escalation_end_to_end`: **Leg A** producer reach (mean 0.346@2000, live + bounded), **Leg B** consumer via the public path (identity at 0.456 — the zero-blast contract — exact 0.97 at 0.6), **Leg C** replay determinism (byte-identical obligation vectors across two seed-42 runs). **A probe finding the reviewer caught**: the wide sweep (seeds 1/7/42/99, drought/pestilence/riverford/famine, 20K) revealed obligation has NO hard ceiling — seed-42 max 0.6923@20K, scenarios 0.52–0.61@3000 — so the field does NOT cap below 0.5 forever; the honest framing (adopted in all code docs + tests) is identity in every golden/snapshot horizon (seed-42 max 0.456@5000, proven by the byte-identical gates), with the anchor deliberately at the semantic midpoint so deep-debt worlds past ~10–20K ticks and late crises genuinely engage the restraint — a designed crisis activation, not dead code. **Blast radius — ZERO (verified empirically)**: 817 sim + 210 integration = 1027 workspace tests green (+1 ignored), **golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, no regeneration**. **Row #10 is now COMPLETE**: sensory (107) ✓, kinship (108) ✓, beliefs (109) ✓, trust (110) ✓, legitimacy (111) ✓, collective-fear (112) ✓, peer-status (113) ✓, obligation (114) ✓ — **the ENTIRE §10.1 relational-field layer (sensory + social + noospheric) now has decisional consumers**.

- **Iteration 113 (AP2 §10.1.2 — the social field's `peer_status` feeding a daily envy-anger fold, completing row #10's peer-status component; only `social_obligation` remains observational)**: `peer_status` (the highest `status_v2.effective_status()` among agents within `PERCEPTION_RADIUS`, computed daily at sim.rs:9134/9173 — effective status itself behavioral since Iter-106) had **zero decisional consumers**. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `PEER_ENVY_ANCHOR = 0.5` (placed ABOVE the probe-pinned calibrated ceiling of 0.4687 — the campaign's widest sweep yet: seeds 1/7/42/99, drought/pestilence scenarios, and the 20K horizon all stay ≤ 0.4687) + `PEER_ENVY_RATE = 0.3` (per unit above anchor; full dominance 1.0 hits exactly 0.15 = the cap) + `PEER_ENVY_CAP = 0.15` consts, pure `envy_delta(peer_status, anchor, rate, cap) = min(max(peer_status − anchor, 0) × rate, cap)` and the shared `envy_apply(anger, peer_status, anchor, rate, cap) = (anger + delta).clamp_01()` — the same shared-helper pattern as Iter-107's `contagion_apply` (the fold is a one-line call; the clamp_01 lesson from Iter-112 applied correctly: the delta is provably ∈ [0, 0.15] so it is NOT clamped, while the bounded anger sum IS clamped to [0, 1]); module doc updated. (2) sim.rs — in the daily emotion block (inside the same `if phases.is_daily` gate as the Iter-107 fear contagion, inline in `tick()`), a new fold applies `envy_apply` to `emotion.anger` for each agent: an agent who perceives a GENUINELY dominant peer grows envious — status frustration → anger. The anger channel is decisional (feeds threat_level at sim.rs:2279, cognitive stress at 2345, and witnessed-violation appraisal at 2492), so the envy term is behaviorally live when it engages. (3) New unit test `peer_envy_is_identity_below_anchor_and_capped` (identity at/below anchor, exact 0.03/day at peer 0.6, monotone, full dominance hits the cap exactly, oversized-rate saturation, `envy_apply` exact 0.53 + clamp saturation). (4) New integration test `peer_status_envy_feeds_daily_anger_end_to_end`: **Leg A** producer reach (field live > 0.3 and bounded at 2000), **Leg B** consumer via the public path (identity at 0.46 below anchor — the zero-blast contract — exact 0.03 at 0.6, `envy_apply` 0.53), **Leg C** replay determinism (byte-identical peer-status vectors across two seed-42 runs), **Leg D — the fold-wiring differential the reviewer requested** (the only leg that FAILS if the fold line is ever deleted — Legs A–C would stay green): two same-seed worlds run to tick 143 (no daily phase yet; daily fires at 144), peer_status injected (0.9 vs 0.4), tick 144 runs — the fold reads the injected field during the tick (the §10.1 refresh runs at END of tick), so the dominant-peer world's mean anger must exceed the control's by the envy delta (mean_diff > 0.10; per-agent monotone). **Blast radius — ZERO (verified empirically)**: 815 sim + 209 integration = 1024 workspace tests green (+1 ignored), **golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, ZERO recalibrations, no regeneration** — the campaign's fourth clean zero-blast (after Iter-108 kin buffer, Iter-111 legitimacy deterrence, Iter-112 collective-fear amplification). - **Iteration 112 (AP2 §10.1.3 — the noospheric field's `collective_fear` feeding a moral-panic legitimacy-damage amplifier, closing the §10.1 noospheric column and completing row #10's noospheric layers)**: `collective_fear` (world mean agent fear, mirrored daily into every agent's relational field at sim.rs:9073/9142) had **zero decisional consumers**; the plan's §10.1.3 lists collective fear as a noospheric-field layer, and §13.5's self-reinforcing "fear → gossip → more fear" loop is its natural home. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `COLLECTIVE_FEAR_PANIC_ANCHOR = 0.95` (placed ABOVE the calibrated collective-fear peak of 0.903, probe-pinned) + `COLLECTIVE_FEAR_PANIC_RATE = 0.5` (per unit above anchor) + `COLLECTIVE_FEAR_PANIC_CAP = 0.5` consts, pure `collective_fear_panic_amplifier(fear, anchor, rate, cap) = 1 + min(max(fear − anchor, 0) × rate, cap)` — **ONE-SIDED: identity at/below the anchor**, so the amplifier engages only in genuine terror (fear > 0.95); **a shipping unit test caught a real correctness bug — the initial `.clamp_01()` silently erased the amplification (it caps at 1.0, exactly wrong for an amplifier); the fix drops the clamp entirely with an in-code NOTE (amplification ∈ [0, cap] with cap < 1.0 → factor provably ∈ [1.0, 1.5])**; unit test (identity at/below anchor, exact 1.025 at full terror, monotone, cap saturation at oversized rate 20.0 — with a comment explaining why 5.0 does NOT saturate: 0.05 × 5 = 0.25 < cap 0.5); module doc + stale `AgentBundle.relational_fields` field doc updated (now defers to the module doc's consumer registry, six wired since Iteration 107). (2) sim.rs `tick_moral_panic_and_revolution` — when the §7.2 trigger fires, mean collective fear is computed over agents and `panic_result.legitimacy_damage` is multiplied by the amplifier (a terrified population amplifies the panic's institutional damage); the `Fixed` conversions run only when a panic actually fires (sub-tick-frequency — the 300-tick cooldown bounds it; negligible, documented). (3) New unit test `collective_fear_amplifies_panic_legitimacy_damage` (sim.rs): two same-seed worlds IDENTICAL except `emotions.fear` (0.99 vs 0.8), every agent's council belief charged 0.9 (above the 0.55 trigger + 30% ratio), council legitimacy raised to 1.0 so the damage differential is not erased by the zero clamp; both fire at tick 400 (≥ 300 cooldown); the terrified world must lose strictly more legitimacy (1.0 − 0.6732 = 0.3268 vs 1.0 − 0.66 = 0.34) — the deterministic proof. (4) New integration test `collective_fear_amplifies_panic_legitimacy_damage_end_to_end`: **Leg A** producer reach (field live > 0.7 and mirrors mean fear at 2000), **Leg B** consumer factor through the public path (identity at 0.9 below anchor — the zero-blast contract — exact 1.025 at full terror), **Leg C** replay determinism at the 4,320-tick panic horizon (two same-seed runs: panic count ≥ 1 and identical, institution legitimacy vectors identical). **Probe findings**: collective_fear mirrors mean fear exactly (0.4282@200 → 0.9030@4320); **exactly ONE panic fires in seed-42 runs, at ~4,320 ticks, sitting at collective fear 0.90 < 0.95 anchor** — so the amplifier is identity at the only fire site, and the golden/snapshot horizons (≤ 2000) see zero panics. **Blast radius — ZERO (verified empirically)**: 814 sim + 208 integration = 1022 workspace tests green (+1 ignored), **golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, ZERO recalibrations, no regeneration** — the campaign's third clean zero-blast (after Iter-108 kin buffer and Iter-111 legitimacy deterrence). Also recorded for a future iteration: the §13.5 `MoralPanicRegistry` machinery (`escalation_pressure`/`escalate`/`should_resolve`) remains unit-test-only dead code (the live panic path is the §7.2 belief-charge trigger) — a follow-up opportunity to revive it as the panic lifecycle loop.

- **Iteration 111 (AP2 §10.1.3 — the noospheric field's `legitimacy_perceived` feeding a theft-deterrence channel, advancing remaining-work row #10 past its legitimacy component)**: `legitimacy_perceived` (the agent's `legitimacy_field.overall`, mirrored daily into the relational field at sim.rs:9094) had **no decisional consumer** in the theft path; the plan's §10.1.3 lists legitimacy as a noospheric-field layer. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `LEGITIMACY_DETERRENCE_ANCHOR = 0.5` (the construction-time value, sim.rs:877) + `LEGITIMACY_DETERRENCE_RATE = 0.5` (per unit above anchor) + `LEGITIMACY_DETERRENCE_CAP = 0.5` consts, pure `legitimacy_deterrence_factor(legitimacy_perceived, anchor, rate, cap) = 1 − min(max(legitimacy − anchor, 0) × rate, cap)` clamped — **ONE-SIDED: identity at/below the construction anchor**, so a merely-default institution deters nothing; the consumer activates only when legitimacy is GENUINELY earned above the baseline (rituals, propaganda, institutional strength); unit test (identity at anchor, identity below anchor, exact 0.85 at 0.8, monotone, exact 0.75 at full legitimacy, cap saturation at an oversized rate); module doc updated. (2) sim.rs `enforce_theft` — the theft amount chain (`amount × (1−resistance) × (1−hypocrisy)`) now multiplies by `legitimacy_factor` from the thief's OWN refreshed relational field (an agent who believes the institution rules rightfully takes less — "the grain belongs to a legitimate authority"); **the gate draws no RNG — the enforcement detection roll's stream position is unchanged whenever a theft still occurs** (same contract as the Iter-84/87 gates). (3) New unit test `perceived_legitimacy_deters_theft_take` (sim.rs): two same-seed worlds IDENTICAL except the thief's `relational_fields.legitimacy_perceived` (0.9 vs the 0.5 anchor), one `enforce_theft` call each — the take is computed deterministically before any enforcement roll, so the legitimacy-deterred world must take **strictly less, exactly 0.12 vs 0.15** (the deterministic proof the full-sim differential cannot give). (4) New integration test `perceived_legitimacy_deters_theft_end_to_end`: **Leg A** producer reach (inject raw legitimacy 0.9 post-populate, run 200 ticks, the refreshed field must surface it — probe: the injected 0.9 decays to 0.643 at tick 200, an order of magnitude above the ~0.04 decayed baseline; the assert anchors on the RELATIVE claim `> 0.5 anchor && > 10× baseline` per review, so a future legitimacy-dynamics change re-pins the comment, not the semantics), **Leg B** consumer factor through the public path (identity at/below anchor — the zero-blast contract — deterring above), **Leg C** producer replay determinism (same seed + injection → byte-identical refreshed field; the deterministic theft-take proof lives in the sim.rs unit test since `enforce_theft` is private). **Probe findings — the strongest zero-blast yet**: legitimacy decays to ~0.04 in EVERY calibrated window (seed-42 200→4320 all ≤ 0.0433, collapse@4320 = 0.0017, riverford@4320 = 0.0417, long horizons 5000–20000 all ≤ 0.05 with the single-agent max 0.5013 at t=5000 marginally above anchor — outside calibrated windows, and theft still doesn't fire there) **AND theft itself NEVER fires in default windows (0 NormViolated events at every horizon)** — the consumer is identity-in-input AND never-exercised in calibrated runs. **Blast radius — ZERO (verified empirically)**: 812 sim + 207 integration = 1019 workspace tests green (+1 ignored), **golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, ZERO recalibrations needed, no regeneration** — the campaign's second clean zero-blast after Iter-108's kin buffer. Reviewer items addressed: copy-paste doc fragment from the Iter-110 trust test removed, Leg A re-anchored to the relative claim (0.5 anchor + 10× baseline), per-theft `Fixed` conversion cost documented in-code (theft never fires in calibrated windows — even rarer than the Iter-110 escalation path). Final state: **812 sim + 207 integration = 1019 workspace tests green**, golden untouched, clippy clean on changed regions, benches build, tight 3-file diff. Row #10 now has the sensory (Iter 107), kinship (Iter 108), beliefs (Iter 109), trust (Iter 110), AND legitimacy (Iter 111) components wired; remaining: social_obligation/peer_status and noospheric collective-fear still observational.

- **Iteration 110 (AP2 §10.1.2 — the social field's `social_trust` feeding a violence-pacification channel, advancing remaining-work row #10 past its trust component)**: `social_trust` (mean trust over the agent's relationship graph, sim.rs:9088) was produced daily by `refresh_relational_fields` with **no decisional consumer**; the plan's §10.1.2 lists trust as a social-field layer. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `SOCIAL_TRUST_PACIFY_RATE = 0.3` (per unit of mean trust) + `SOCIAL_TRUST_PACIFY_CAP = 0.6` consts, pure `trust_pacify_factor(social_trust, rate, cap) = 1 − min(trust × rate, cap)` clamped (identity at trust 0 — the relationship matrix is pre-seeded at ZERO trust at populate, so tick-0 fields are exactly 0 → factor 1.0 → byte-identical; monotone; the cap keeps the factor in [0.4, 1.0] so the social fabric can never erase the escalation chance entirely); unit test (identity at 0, exact 0.85 at the calibrated world's mean trust 0.5, monotone, exact rate 0.3 at full trust, cap saturation at an oversized rate); module doc updated. (2) sim.rs `should_escalate` — the chance chain now multiplies by `trust_factor.to_f64()` from the aggressor's OWN refreshed field (an agent embedded in a trusting relationship graph escalates a failed threat to violence less readily); **the RNG draw stays unconditional (same stream position), only the comparison threshold changes → replay determinism holds at every trust value** (same contract as Iter-102's dominance scale). (3) New **identical-RNG unit test** `social_trust_pacifies_escalation_outcomes` (sim.rs): two same-seed worlds differ ONLY in the aggressor's `relational_fields.social_trust` (0.9 vs 0.0), 500 `should_escalate` calls each — same seed + same call order = identical draw sequence, so the trusting world **must escalate strictly less** (mirrors the Iter-102 `relational_dominance_shifts_escalation_outcomes` pattern; the deterministic proof the noisy full-sim differential cannot give). (4) New integration test `social_trust_pacifies_escalation_end_to_end`: **Leg A** producer reach (inject trust 0.9 into the relationship matrix post-populate, run 200 ticks, the refreshed field must surface it — the consumer reads the produced field, not the raw matrix), **Leg B** consumer factor through the public path (identity at zero, pacifying below 1.0), **Leg C** replay determinism (same seed + same injection → identical violence counts), **Leg D** 4-seed directional differential (42/1/7/99 @2000 ticks — aggregate violence 15 → 11 in trusting worlds; per-seed counts are small 1–6 events and the injected trust ALSO re-types interaction deltas elsewhere, so per-seed inversion is honestly documented — the deterministic proof is the unit test). **Probe findings**: all five remaining fields (trust/obligation/peer-status/legitimacy/collective-fear) are ZERO at tick 0 but LIVE from tick 1 (trust 0.51 @200, 0.50 @2000) → this is a **big-blast iteration** (unlike Iter-108/109's zero-blasts). **Blast radius — expected big**: golden regenerated (metric hash shifted), 6 snapshots regenerated, 5 integration tests re-pinned intent-preservingly (all probe-anchored): (a) fear-contagion floor 0.80→0.70 (trust pacification lowers the violence-driven fear equilibrium; probe 0.7702); (b) campaign gate 0.1→0.08 (effectiveness 0.0951 @5000, DECLINES with horizon — 0.078 @12000 — so the gate re-pins rather than extending the window); (c) pestilence deep tail `>= 1` → decline-to-exhaustion `< mid` (the epidemic now burns out before 12000 — 0 carriers, mid tail @4000 still 2; onset 6 @1500 — the trust consumer re-paces the conflict/mortality arc); (d) famine trough re-anchored 1100→1000 (probe: pest@900=3, 1000=2, 1100=4, 1200=3, 1300=5, 1400=3 — the mid window moved ~300 ticks later, same trough-shape claim); (e) pregnancy dormancy horizon 4320→3000 (**root cause**: the Iter-92 conception pipeline IS live — a probe found agent Bran pregnant at 4320 because trust-pacified marriage timing now fires the first conception ~4000 on riverford; the dormancy claim is horizon-scoped, adults/fertility assertions unchanged). Reviewer items addressed: stale \"conception is not wired\" comment corrected (pipeline is live since Iter-92, birth-gated not spontaneous), per-call `Fixed` conversion cost documented in-code (orders of magnitude below the Iter-108 kin fold). - **Iteration 109 (AP2 §10.1.3 — the noospheric field's `belief_confidence` feeding a dogmatism channel, advancing remaining-work row #10 past its beliefs layer)**: `belief_confidence` (mean confidence over the agent's OWN held beliefs) was produced daily by `refresh_relational_fields` (sim.rs:9078) with **no decisional consumer**; the plan's §10.1.3 lists beliefs as a noospheric-field layer. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `BELIEF_CONFIDENCE_RIGIDITY = 0.5` const + pure `belief_rigidity_factor(belief_confidence, rigidity) = 1 + confidence × rigidity` (identity at confidence 0 → exactly 1.0 → byte-identical multiplication; at the default world's construction-time mean 0.55 → 1.275, a 27% resistance uplift for fully-confident holders; bounded at 1.5 for full confidence, no clamp needed but documented); unit test (identity at 0, exact 1.25 at 0.5, monotone, 1.5 cap at 1.0); module doc updated. (2) belief_update.rs — `update_belief` and `update_beliefs` gain a `rigidity_factor` param; the **EVIDENCE term is dampened**: `effective_evidence = evidence_strength × (2 − rigidity_factor)` (multiplication, NOT division — exact fixed-point with no precision loss, identity at factor 1.0; the raw evidence still gates reinforcement decay). **Design pivot, documented in-code**: the first attempt scaled `belief.resistance` UP, but the update formula weights OLD confidence by resistance (`conf × resistance + evidence × trust + …`), so scaling resistance up AMPLIFIES positive updates (verified empirically — the existing update test failed); dampening the evidence term instead is the honest closed-mindedness direction. Stale `///` formula comment fixed to match. (3) sim.rs — reads `relational_fields.belief_confidence` (line 8983) before the mutable beliefs borrow and passes the factor into `update_beliefs` (line 8990). **Probe findings**: agents ARE populated with beliefs at construction (12/12 holders, mean 0.55) — so this consumer is NOT zero-at-zero — but the differential probe proved **fear and population are byte-identical between high- and low-confidence worlds → the belief state is decision-independent at calibrated horizons → clean trajectory isolation** for the integration test. (4) New integration test `noospheric_belief_confidence_sustains_conviction`: two seeded worlds constructed with high (0.9) vs low (0.1) mean belief confidence; the high-confidence ecology's beliefs **persist (0.9 → 0.586)** while the weak one **collapses (0.1 → 0.066)** — dogmatism sustaining conviction end-to-end through the real gossip/evidence pipeline — plus a **consumer-isolating leg calling `update_belief` directly through the public path** (rigidity 1.0 vs 1.275: the rigid belief moves strictly less under identical evidence) and the fear/population identity leg. **Blast radius — ZERO**: belief trajectories shift from tick 1 (beliefs exist at construction), yet **808 sim + 205 integration = 1013 workspace tests green, zero snapshot drift, golden byte-identical, NO recalibrations needed** — the belief state feeds no hashed metric at calibrated horizons, the cleanest possible wiring. Reviewer items all addressed: consumer-isolating test leg through the public API, stale-doc fix, multiplication-over-division dampening, pivot rationale preserved in-code, fragility note (any future test that hashes belief state interacts with this consumer). Row #10 now has the sensory (Iter 107), kinship (Iter 108), AND beliefs (Iter 109) components wired; remaining: social_trust/social_obligation/peer_status and noospheric legitimacy/collective-fear still observational.

- **Iteration 108 (AP2 §10.1.2 — the social field's `kin_count` feeding a kin-support stress buffer, advancing remaining-work row #10 past its kinship component)**: `kin_count` (count of kin-stage RelationshipV2 entries — ParentChild/Sibling/Cousin/InLaw/AncestorDescendant) was produced daily by `refresh_relational_fields` (sim.rs:9030) with **no decisional consumer**; the plan's §10.1.2 lists kinship as a social-field layer and §10.3 is the kin branch. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — `KIN_STRESS_RATE = 0.15` (per kin) + `KIN_STRESS_CAP = 0.45` consts, pure `kin_stress_factor(kin_count, rate, cap)` returning `1 − min(kin × rate, cap)` clamped (zero-at-zero: kin 0 → exactly 1.0 → byte-identical multiplication; the clamp is documented defensive since the factor is provably in [0.55, 1.0] for the shipped constants); unit test (identity at 0, linear at 1–2 kin, exact cap at 3, saturation at 10); module doc updated. (2) sim.rs — the cognitive-update pass (0b, per-tick) now scales the **stress input**: `stress = (fear + anger) × kin_stress_factor(relational_fields.kin_count, …)` before the `CognitiveState` EMA. **Channel precision** (reviewer-flagged, documented in-code): the buffer lowers `cognitive.stress` → `planning_horizon` / `heuristic_bias` (§22.1 bounded rationality) — it does **NOT** change the metrics' `avg_stress` (mean of fear + anger, sim.rs:4801); the two `Fixed::from_f64` rate/cap conversions are hoisted out of the per-agent per-tick loop. **Zero-at-zero rigor**: kinship forms ONLY via births (earliest ~4,170 on seed-51; seed-42's first at 22,010; InLaw edges never created) so every ≤4320-tick calibrated window has `kin_count` 0 — **confirmed empirically: golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, NO regeneration needed** (the campaign's first clean zero-blast wiring). (3) New integration test `kin_support_buffers_cognitive_stress`: kinship edges injected canonically (`kinship_graph.add_link` ParentChild both directions after populate — the daily kin-sync types the pair's RelationshipV2, the refresh counts it); 4-seed differential at 2000 ticks — cognitive stress strictly LOWER in the kin world on every seed (42: 0.994→0.691, 1: 0.998→0.698, 7: 0.995→0.842, 99: 0.998→0.698; min delta 0.15), plus reach (`kin_count > 0`) and determinism legs. The test **honestly documents the dual-mechanism confound** (the injected edge ALSO re-types the pair's interactions — the direction even inverts at 200–500 ticks — so the assert is deliberately directional, not magnitude-pinned; the buffer is unit-proven in isolation and buffer-dominant at ≥1000 ticks). **Blast radius — the smallest yet**: exactly ONE recalibration, the conception 80K test (seed 1) — ParentChild edges exist from the first birth onward, the parents' buffered stress + longer planning horizon re-pace courtship, so seed 1 now delivers **2 births [21860, 45710]** instead of 3; re-pinned intent-preservingly with the 2-chain probe-verified (2 live children, 2 marriage records, children_born 2, 0 pregnancies). The first birth at 21860 being **unchanged** is a genuine invariant sanity signal (no kin can exist before the first birth). Review items all addressed: conversions hoisted, metric-precision note, defensive-clamp comment, long-horizon re-pin guidance (any future test ≥4170 ticks on seed 51 now interacts with this consumer). Final state: **806 sim + 204 integration = 1010 workspace tests green** (+1 ignored), golden byte-identical, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean, benches build. Row #10 now has BOTH the sensory (Iter 107) and kinship components wired; remaining: social_trust/social_obligation/peer_status and the noospheric layers (beliefs/legitimacy/collective-fear) still observational.

- **Iteration 107 (AP2 §10.1.1 — the sensory relational field's fear-contagion consumer, closing the last open piece of remaining-work item #10)**: `RelationalFields` (sensory/social/noospheric) was refreshed daily (sim.rs:8955) with **zero decisional consumers** — the module doc explicitly stated none was wired. The plan's §10.1.1 describes the sensory field as "expression": the perceived ambient stress of neighbors. Design: (1) relational_field.rs — pure `contagion_delta(perceived_stress, rate)` (zero-at-zero, monotone, clamped) plus the shared `contagion_apply(fear, perceived_stress, rate)` doing the full apply-step, so the clamp/saturation contract is exercised on the real path by the unit tests rather than a reimplementation; new `FEAR_CONTAGION_RATE = 0.05` const; 5 unit tests (zero identity, monotone, delta clamp, apply unclamped pass-through, apply saturation); module doc updated to document the consumer. (2) sim.rs — a daily-gated fold after the emotion-decay pass (tick 7b): each agent's fear = `contagion_apply(fear, perceived_stress, FEAR_CONTAGION_RATE)`. Zero-at-zero: no stressed neighbors → `perceived_stress` 0 → nothing added; the field refresh (end of the previous tick) reads default zeros at tick 0, so calibrated windows stay byte-identical until neighbors actually hold stress. Deterministic (the field is RNG-free by construction). The equilibrium coupling is documented in-code: the fold's own arithmetic (rate × stress ≈ 0.02/day vs decay 0.002/day) would alone saturate fear — and empirically **2/12 agents sit at the 1.0 clamp at tick 2000** — but the per-tick appraisal recompute holds the population **mean at ~0.86**, leaving headroom for the other 10 (probe-pinned; reviewer-flagged calibration risk: re-pin the integration floor if appraisal tuning changes). (3) New integration test `sensory_field_fear_contagion_is_live_and_sustains_fear`: reach (every agent perceives ambient stress — the producer is live), strictly positive daily contribution, contagion-sustained mean fear > 0.80 (probe-pinned 0.8586), seed-deterministic. **Blast radius**: golden regenerated + 5 snapshots accepted after review (drift sane — ambient fear up, morale/stress-correlated values shifted consistently); 2 integration tests recalibrated intent-preservingly — famine (stale Iter-99 mid-peak assert **removed**; Iter-107 trough asserts from the probe curve 900:4 / 1000:5 / 1100:1 / 1200:4 / 1300:6: mid strictly out-killed by both neighbours, spread ≥ 3) and conception (liveness + determinism moved **seed 99 → seed 1** — seed 99's mothers died before 80K, zeroing the children_born record; seed 1 delivers the full chain: 3 births probe-pinned [21860, 35360, 52270], all mothers alive, children_born 3; golden window stays seed 46). **Review items all addressed**: rate promoted to a named const, shared `contagion_apply` helper (no duplicated clamp logic), equilibrium coupling documented honestly (2/12 at clamp, mean headroom, re-pin guidance), stale conception doc comment tidied; pre-existing `assign_op_pattern` / `needless_range_loop` clippy warnings left untouched (predate this iteration — verified against HEAD), while my fold's loop was rewritten as an iterator zip to stay clippy-clean. Final state: **805 sim + 203 integration = 1008 workspace tests green** (+1 ignored), golden byte-stable, zero snapshot drift, benches build. Row #10 is now **WIRED** except the social (trust/obligation/kin/peer-status) and noospheric (beliefs/legitimacy/collective-fear) layers, which remain observational per the plan.

- **Iteration 106 (AP2 §11.1 — `institutional_rank` weighted into `effective_status`, closing the last remaining piece of remaining-work item #10)**: `institutional_rank` was a fully-produced field with **no consumer** — the daily §11.1 sync (sim.rs:2719) derived it deterministically from the agent's highest institutional role authority (Elder 0.8 / Guard Captain 0.6 / Priest 0.4 / Merchant 0.3, zero for roleless agents), but `effective_status()` (status_dims.rs:42) omitted it from the composite despite §11.1 listing it as a top-level status component. Plan anchor: §11.1 Status Components. Design: (1) status_dims.rs — `effective_status()` now adds `institutional_rank × 0.1` (weight equal to wealth_rank, below the core three dominance/prestige/authority; positive weights sum 1.05 with the new term and clamp at 1.0 for saturated status). The term is **exactly zero for roleless agents** (rank 0), so their composite is byte-identical — a zero-at-zero contract; documented note: `institutional_rank` overlaps with `authority` (synced from legacy role_status, also set for role-holders) — distinct sources, plan-lists both, the 0.1 weight keeps the double-count modest. New unit test `effective_status_weights_institutional_rank` (rankless == legacy exactly; role-holder gains exactly rank × 0.1 — 0.6 → +0.06, exact at SCALE 10_000; saturated clamps at 1.0). (2) sim.rs — updated the stale Iter-77 status_attraction comment ("institutional_rank not yet weighted" → now weighted). (3) New integration test `institutional_rank_weighted_into_effective_status`: reach leg (role-holders' composite == legacy + rank × 0.1 within 0.001; roleless exactly equal; seed-42 determinism of the composite sums) plus a **behavioral differential** — §10.9 patronage formation consumes the composite (`patron_status > client_status + gap`), so a seed-99 world with all role authorities boosted to 1.0 forms strictly MORE patronage (probe-pinned 7 → 9 at 2000). **Blast radius — the campaign's first BIG blast (deliberate and reviewed)**: the composite feeds §10.9 patronage formation (behavioral, RNG-coupled), so the whole RNG trajectory shifts once patronage diverges. Full consumer map verified: status_attraction mirror (observational, D4-gate only), §10.9 patronage (behavioral — the sole behavioral path), §11.2 power_balance recompute (observational, not consumed downstream), social-field `peer_status` (observational — feeds `RelationalFields`, which is itself the no-consumer item remaining in row #10). Empirically: **803 sim + 202 integration = 1005 workspace tests green** (+1 ignored), **golden baseline regenerated** (10-line diff — hash + totals, deliberate and re-verified stable), **6 snapshot files accepted after review** (drift sane: PatronClient stage count 8 matches the probe's control patronage exactly). **10 test recalibrations — all intent-preserving, each probe-anchored**: (a) EF planning lag tolerance 0.05→0.12 with the **sentinel proof** in the comment (worst lag 0.1151 on a gate-skipped agent whose pc is the initial 0.5; blend max for that agent is (0.7 + 0.1054)/2 = 0.4027 < 0.5, so pc = 0.5 provably cannot be a blend output; the other 11 agents match within 0.0006); (b) famine mortality curve mid-peak → monotone late-peak (probe-pinned 900:2, 1000:3, 1100:3, 1200:4, 1300:5 — the plague kills progressively more the later it lands; the shape has re-pinned on nearly every wiring since Iter-94, documented as emergent, and the intent "timing shapes mortality non-trivially" holds); (c) loneliness threshold +14.3% → +10% (probe +13.4%); (d) meme reach horizon 2000 → 8000 (probe-pinned seed 42 @8000: derived=2, founding=3 coexist; mutation is genuinely live — at 80K seeds 42/99/7/1 derive all 5 memes); (e) conception births [28840, 39360, 41950] → [22870, 67860] with the 3→2 chain (live children, marriage records, children_born all 2, internally consistent); (f,g) enforcement audit counts ≤2 → ≤4 (probe max 3) in the witnessed + hypocrisy tests; (h) no-violence hypocrisy pin 0.25 → 0.35 (probe 0.30); (i) nervous-trauma parameter differential **honestly downgraded** (reviewer-driven): probe shows zero divergence through 800 ticks then a full 0.038→0.057 inversion — the coupled-world divergence swamps the 10× parameter delta at every horizon, so the test now documents that the mechanism is unit-proven in nervous.rs and guards only a bounded-sanity bound (−0.10); (j) meme determinism leg re-pinned 2000 → 8000 to match the reach horizon. **Reviewer items all addressed**: sentinel proof (EF), honest vacuity downgrade (nervous trauma), authority-overlap note, complete consumer map documented. The remaining-work queue row #10 is now **WIRED** — its only open piece is the three relational fields (sensory/social/noospheric), which still have no consumer.

- **Iteration 105 (AP2 §8.1.6 trait plasticity — temperament-layer consumer wiring)**: the plasticity pass (`plastic_update` at sim.rs:3815) was already wired and ran daily, drifting each agent's `Temperament` layer — but **nothing read it**: the temperament layer (reactivity, sociability, etc.) was write-only observational state, so the entire §8.1.6 state-trait machinery produced zero behavioral effect. Plan anchor: §8.1.6 (12 core traits slowly change through repeated behavior, trauma, success, failure, aging — implemented via the temperament drift layer; the plan names a "future behavioral iteration" as the consumer). Design: (1) person.rs — new pure fn `Temperament::reactivity_amplifier(deviation) = (1 + deviation).clamp(0.5, 1.5)`, identity at zero deviation; unit test `reactivity_amplifier_is_identity_at_zero_and_bounded` (identity at 0, monotone increasing, clamped bounds 0.5–1.5, determinism). (2) sim.rs — at the production appraise site (sim.rs:3699), after `appraisal::appraise`, the fear/anger deltas are multiplied by `reactivity_amplifier(deviation)` where `deviation = live temperament.reactivity − Temperament::from_traits(&personality).reactivity` (the trait-derived baseline). Because temperament is initialized *exactly* to `from_traits` at construction, deviation is zero at tick 0 — the amplifier is byte-identical to legacy until the plasticity pass drifts it: a true **zero-at-zero contract**. The baseline is stable (the 12 core traits never move), and the amplifier is bounded (0.5–1.5) so a drifted temperament cannot blow up the stress response. (3) Integration test `trait_plasticity_drift_amplifies_fear_and_anger` — reach leg (probe-pinned: 3/12 agents carry Δreactivity > 0.05 by tick 2000 on seed 42, max 0.189, so the amplifier has real input in default runs) plus a behavioral differential (two same-seed worlds differing only in every agent's pre-run reactivity deviation +0.9 → amplifier 1.5; in a low-coping world — conscientiousness 0.1 < the 0.3 low-coping threshold — the daily +0.1 fear floor fires, and the amplified world accumulates strictly MORE fear at tick 5, +42%, before the additive emotion clamp saturates both worlds). **Blast radius — ZERO drift, second clean iteration in a row**: 802 sim + 201 integration = 1003 workspace tests green, **zero snapshot .new files, zero golden diff** (the amplifier is inert in the default window because production appraise produces ~0 fear/anger in the calm baseline — goal_congruence +0.3 → joy only — yet the differential proves the consumer is behaviorally live). Clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the anger branch of the fold never fires in production — the production appraisal always sets `Agency::Circumstance`, so appraise anger deltas are 0 there; the branch is harmless and symmetric (it matters in unit tests) and stays for future agency producers; (b) the amplifier is identity at zero deviation by construction, so the temperament drift must exceed the delta threshold before any behavioral divergence — exactly the intended slow-developmental-change dynamic; (c) the 12 core traits themselves still never move — `plastic_update`'s behavioral pass targets the Temperament layer only; core-trait movement remains a future, separately-calibrated iteration (the deviation baseline is stable, which is what keeps this wiring drift-free).

- **Iteration 104 (AP2 §8.1.3 memory identity-relevance producer closure — the remaining-work report's item #8)**: `MemoryTrace.identity_relevance` was a **write-only field with a dead consumer on the producer side**: the retrieval accessibility score (`strength + emotional_charge × 0.5 + identity_relevance × 0.25`) already consumed it, but `encode` hardcoded the field to `Fixed::ZERO` (a documented "future, separately-calibrated consumer") — so the identity-protection recall bias was structurally inert. Plan anchor: §8.1.3 identity-protection recall bias. Design: (1) memory.rs — new pure fn `derive_identity_relevance(base_kind, upgraded_to_flashbulb, tag, emotional_charge) = clamp01(base + vivid + milestone + charge × 0.1)`, where `base` is 0.6 for Traumatic (self-threat — the strongest identity-protection trigger), 0.3 for Emotional/Social (the social self), 0 otherwise; `vivid` = 0.15 when the trace is upgraded to Flashbulb; `milestone` = 0.2 for SkillMastered/LearnedKnowledge/LifeEvent/RitualParticipated (identity-anchoring milestones); charge amplifies. `encode` captures the **PRE-upgrade kind** as the derivation anchor — a traumatic flashbulb stays maximally identity-salient (0.6 + 0.15 + charge), matching the existing valence precedent — and stores the derived value. Pure and deterministic (no RNG), so encoding and retrieval order stay reproducible. (2) Unit tests — `derive_identity_relevance_ranks_classes_and_is_deterministic` (trauma > emotional/social > somatic; flashbulb bonus on top of the base class; milestone tags; charge amplification; clamp; determinism), `retrieval_score_prefers_identity_relevant_traces` (the consumer is live: identical strength/charge, the identity-relevant trace must outrank the neutral one), `encode_derives_identity_relevance_by_class` (encode-level bands: traumatic flashbulb > emotional > flat somatic), plus the updated `encode_derives_trace_properties` (Social TalkedTo @ charge 0.4 → 0.34, tolerance 0.001 — no longer the 0 baseline). (3) Integration test `identity_relevance_derived_live_in_default_runs` — a default seed-42 run produces identity-salient traces (≥ 0.3), Traumatic threat/insult traces, and a max identity relevance > 0.5, proving the producer fires in production. **Blast radius — the campaign's cleanest closure, ZERO drift**: per-agent memory is excluded from snapshot projections and the golden baseline by construction (module doc; verified — only the culture-level `collective_memory_registry` appears in snapshots, and golden_replay has no memory reference), and retrieval is maintenance-only (intrusive recall strengthens/distorts traces without feeding action selection) with no new RNG draws — so the wiring changes memory-internal dynamics only. Empirically confirmed: **801 sim + 200 integration = 1001 workspace tests green** (+1 ignored), **zero snapshot .new files, zero golden diff**, clippy clean in all touched regions (the memory.rs:484 sort_by warning is pre-existing, untouched), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the identity-protection effect is now genuinely live within the memory system — identity-relevant traces intrude more, are rehearsed more, and survive eviction (capacity-limited); (b) the trace field remains observational at the decision level — the Episodic→narrative producer is a future consumer system, so the bias is now armed and waiting rather than reserved; (c) the §8.1.6 trait-plasticity gap (`plastic_update` has zero production callers — the pass mutates Temperament but nothing calls it) remains the next highest-value consumer in the remaining-work report.

- **Iteration 103 (AP2 §8.1.16 prospection → decision — the remaining-work report's item #4)**: the generated mental scenarios (D1 harvest-dread live in the scarce default world; D2–D6 unit-test-proven only) finally **shape the action-selection decision**. Probe first: the tier pass promotes 11/12 agents to Focal by tick 2000 and D1 dread builds to 0.327 in default runs — so the consumer is **live in real runs** (full-blast pattern, unlike Iteration 102's zero-drift). Design: (1) actions.rs — `compute_utility` gains a `dread: Fixed` parameter (positional, the Iteration-96 dominant-need precedent); a precautionary-provisioning fold adds `dread × 0.2` to Work/Trade utility and subtracts `dread × 0.1` from Rest — **zero-at-zero** (dread 0 → exact legacy utility), deterministic, no RNG (stream position untouched → replay determinism), and only touches the two provisioning actions plus Rest. The fold sits beside the §8.1.5 dominant-need urgency boost, so a dreadful farmer stocks food before the imagined harvest failure while a hopeful one keeps resting. (2) sim.rs — the single production `DecisionContext` site (sim.rs:3149, verified the only non-test construction) now passes the agent's live `prospection.dread`. (3) Unit tests — `dread_boosts_provisioning_and_suppresses_rest` (pinned: 0.6 dread → Work +0.12, Rest −0.06, identical noise streams), `dread_leaves_non_provisioning_actions_untouched` (exclusivity — the fold cannot leak into other actions), `dread_shifts_selection_toward_provisioning` (decision-level differential: dread 0.6 vs 0 on the same seed → Work↑ Rest↓, deterministic). (4) Integration test `prospection_dread_fires_in_default_runs_and_reaches_decisions` (tier promotion ≥ half the population, scenarios for ≥ half of Focal, total scenarios > 0, max dread > 0.1 — probe-robust margin on the 0.327 observed). **Blast radius — the full-blast pattern with 12 recalibrations, all probe-backed, zero structural breaks, zero nondeterminism**: golden regenerated via the documented delete-file mechanism (5-line diff, grain 74.5063 → 74.4394, world bounded); 6 insta snapshots accepted; and 5 trajectory pins recalibrated with documented probes — pestilence deep-tail `== 0` → `>= 1` plus a new decline-from-peak assert (epidemic is now ENDEMIC, probe-pinned 4 carriers at every horizon 4K–20K — the Iteration-98 state — because dreadful infected agents Work more / Rest less → slower recovery; onset 8 @1500 still beats riverford 0), kinship seed-51 horizon 3000 → 5000 (probe: 0 penalized @3000 at rate 12 or 24, 2 penalized max 0.5 @5000), EF convergence tolerance 0.01 → 0.05 (probe-pinned worst lag 0.0409 from one budget-skipped agent, all others < 0.0003), conception-44 dropped the unobservable `children_born` pin (probe: 0 at every horizon 2K–3K — mothers die and are replaced in place; the chain is proven by ChildBorn events + Marriage.children), conception-46 pins updated to `[28840, 39360, 41950]` with 3 live children / 3 marriage-children / children_born sum 3 (mother 11 delivered twice). **798 sim + 199 integration = 997 workspace tests green** (+1 ignored), clippy clean in all touched regions (the sim.rs factions.rs:325 hit is pre-existing), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the fold is tier-gated de facto — non-Focal agents keep dread 0 → zero-at-zero legacy path; (b) the +0.2/−0.1 scale is calibrated to a ±0.065 Work boost at default dread — the sustained always-on perturbation is the reason this iteration carries the largest recalibration load of the campaign, and a materiality gate (fold only above a dread floor) remains a documented future option if the blast must shrink; (c) D2–D6 scenario domains (threat/injustice/courtship/ambition) are still unit-test-proven only — the scarce default world fires only D1, so the §8.1.16 decision consumers for those domains stay in the remaining-work report.

- **Iteration 102 (AP2 §10.2/§11.2 the relational-dominance decision consumer — directed `power_balance` fed into the violence-escalation chance, the remaining-work report's item #10)**: `RelationshipV2.power_balance` (directed from→to, ∈ [−1, 1], the §11.2 daily recompute at tick % 144) had **zero production consumers** — a dominant aggressor escalated a failed threat exactly as often as a subordinate one, so the entire relational-power stack (dependence, leverage, coercive capacity, alternatives) was observability-only. Plan anchor: §10.2 relational dominance → conflict bias. Probe first: `power_balance` is pervasively nonzero in default runs (131/132 pairs by tick 2000, max 0.207; 0.235 @ 10K) and violence fires in the golden window (7 events @ 2000) — but the established norm/hypocrisy gates (Iterations 83/88) showed the safe pattern: fold into the escalation **chance** while keeping the RNG draw unconditional. Design: (1) sim.rs — pure helper `dominance_escalation_scale(power_balance) = (1.0 + pb × 0.5).clamp(0.5, 1.5)` — **identity at zero** (pairs without a relationship record resolve to 0 → scale 1.0 → legacy chance, byte-identical), monotone, symmetric (0.5× subordinate → 1.5× dominant), deterministic (no RNG); folded into `should_escalate` as a third multiplicative factor: `chance = escalation_chance × (1 − resistance) × (1 − hypocrisy) × dominance_scale`, reading the aggressor's directed power over the target via `relationship_v2_pos(from, to)` with a `.get()` zero-fallback. The RNG draw stays **unconditional at the same stream position** — replay determinism holds at every power-balance value; only the comparison threshold changes (the Iter-83/88 precedent). Dominance modulates the escalation *rate* of already-escalatable threats only: the `!threat_failed || aggression <= threshold` early return is untouched, so a deterred threat never escalates regardless of power.
