(2) Unit tests — `dominance_escalation_scale_is_identity_at_zero_and_clamped` (scale(0) == 1.0, scale(1) == 1.5, scale(−1) == 0.5, monotone at ±0.4) and `relational_dominance_shifts_escalation_outcomes` (the **deterministic differential**: two same-seed fresh sims whose pair power_balance is +1 vs −1, 200 `should_escalate` calls each — identical RNG draw sequence since exactly one Social draw is consumed per call, so dominant > subordinate is fixed for the seed, not a statistical gamble). (3) Integration test `relational_dominance_feeds_violence_escalation` — two same-seed worlds crafted via `rv2.dependence` (0.0 vs 1.0; a field the simulation itself **never writes** — verified by grep, only `cult.dependence` is written, a different struct — so the daily recompute reproduces the crafted asymmetry at every 144-tick boundary): zero-dependence grants everyone +0.7 emotional leverage per pair; the dominant world must produce **strictly more violence** across 3 seeds × 5000 ticks, plus a mean-`power_balance` reach assert (dom > sub, the +0.7 pre-clamp shift survives daily recompute). **Zero golden/snapshot drift — no regeneration needed**: the golden window's violence is an early-window phenomenon (all events pre-tick-144, per the Iter-83 note), when `power_balance` is still 0 because the first daily recompute hasn't run — so the consumer is inert there and every snapshot/golden hash stays byte-identical; the probe's post-144 nonzero pb values (131/132 @ 2000) belong to pairs whose draws did not flip (empirically verified: no event changed). **795 sim + 198 integration = 993 workspace tests green** (+1 ignored), zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (the sim.rs:10158/10174/10175 hits are pre-existing apprenticeship/storage test code, untouched), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the scale composes with the enemy-clan doubling (0.6 × 1.5 = 0.9 max chance — dominance amplifies the already-doubled enemy rate); (b) the 0.5 gain is the small-gain calibration consistent with the faction/courtship consumers (sized so the golden window cannot flip, per the probe); (c) the remaining §10.2/§11.2 gaps (sensory/social/noospheric relational-field consumers, `institutional_rank` in `effective_status()`) stay observational — documented in the remaining-work report.

- **Iteration 101 (AP2 §10.4 the attraction→courtship decision consumer — the daily ladder is now attraction-gated, the remaining-work report's item #11)**: the courtship ladder's 0.6× attraction floor (`min_attraction = next.base_trust() × 0.6`) was DEAD on the daily advancement path — `try_advance(avg_trust, avg_trust)` passed trust as BOTH the gate and the attraction arg, so the floor never bound and a disgusted, socially-costly, kinship-penalized pair climbed the ladder exactly like an infatuated one. The interaction path (`record_positive`) already passed the pursuer's real `total_attraction()`, so the two advancement paths disagreed: interactions were attraction-gated, the daily pass was trust-only. Plan anchor: §10.4 "attraction → behavioral courtship decisions." Design: (1) sim.rs daily courtship pass — `mutual_attraction` bookkeeping and the `try_advance` attraction arg now use the pursuer's LIVE `total_attraction()` (familiarity, status mirror, reciprocity mirror, moral disgust, social cost, kinship penalty all fold in), so the floor genuinely gates the ladder; trust remains the primary gate, attraction binds only below its own floor (zero-at-zero: at or above the floor the advance conditions are unchanged). Ordering verified: the reciprocity mirror writes AFTER `try_advance`, so the current tick's reciprocity is never double-counted (one-tick lag, consistent with the existing anger-in-tuple convention). (2) courtship.rs — two unit tests pinning the floor as a real gate independent of trust (`attraction_floor_gates_ladder_even_with_high_trust`: trust 0.6 + attraction 0.2 stalls below TestingReciprocity's 0.24 floor; `attraction_at_floor_advances_with_trust`: 0.33 clears it) — 11/11 module tests. (3) Integration test `attraction_floor_stalls_courtship_in_suppressed_attraction_village` — a **same-test differential**: seed-45 control climbs to Betrothal with ceiling 0.477, while the suppressed village (positive channels zeroed, penalties raised) stalls strictly below the control's stage with ceiling 0.163 (< 0.35 sanity bound). **Probe root-cause**: the initial "must stall below Flirtation / total freeze" pin was FALSE — the daily status mirror, interaction familiarity and the courtship reciprocity mirror re-inflate the model above the raw crush (a heavily-interacting pair with 1942 positives cleared the 0.18 Flirtation floor), so the honest consumer signal is the suppressed ceiling, not a freeze — documented at the pin. The reviewer-mandated control leg makes the differential fully self-contained (crushed < control in both stage and ceiling, plus a control-must-climb guard). **Zero golden/snapshot drift** — probe: the full suite is byte-identical on the 2000-tick golden and all snapshots (the golden's marriages form through the trust-gated paths, and the pairs that climb in the golden window clear their floors on natural attraction). **793 sim + 197 integration = 990 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (no in-region hits), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) `try_advance` can fire twice per tick (interaction path + daily pass) — pre-existing behavior, both advance at most one rung, no regression; (b) the `no_record` balance gate still lets a fresh pair (0 interactions) advance on trust + attraction alone — pre-existing, unchanged; (c) the crush is not total by design (social_cost re-derives from notoriety, moral_disgust from the disgust emotion each tick) — the test's setup comment now says "suppressed" and documents the re-inflation exactly; (d) `courtship.mutual_attraction` now tracks the pursuer's total attraction instead of avg trust — the field is informational (eligibility uses avg_trust directly), so no downstream consumer changed semantics.

- **Iteration 100 (AP2 §29.2 the full faction threat model's first decision consumer — revolt-vs-crackdown force comparison, the remaining-work report's item #15)**: `FactionV2::threat_level()` (fighting_strength × cohesion × grievance × (1 − fragmentation)) and `legitimacy_of_violence` were computed every tick with **zero production consumers** — the protest-suppression decision read only raw `fighting_strength` (mobilization × morale × (1 − casualties)), so a fragmented, indifferent faction resisted a crackdown exactly as hard as a cohesive, aggrieved, radicalized one. Plan anchor: §29.2 "revolt-vs-crackdown force-comparison consumer." Design: (1) faction_v2.rs — new `suppression_resistance()` = `(fighting_strength + threat_level).clamp_01() × (1 + legitimacy_of_violence × 0.5)`, clamped [0,1]. The blend keeps resistance **never below the raw armed core** (the Iteration-89 armed-faction mandate is preserved and amplified — threat can only add, radicalization can only scale up), is monotone in strength/cohesion/grievance and anti-monotone in fragmentation, and is **zero-at-zero** (unarmed → strength 0 → threat 0 → resistance 0 → the legacy `protest_strength = 0` suppression behavior is byte-identical). Saturation documented: when armed_core + threat already clamps to 1.0, the LoV amplifier is capped by the [0,1] bound — it only bites below saturation. (2) sim.rs — the protest-suppression call site swaps `fighting_strength` for `suppression_resistance` (zero if no v2 record exists — legacy behavior), local renamed `protest_resistance`. (3) Four unit tests — `suppression_resistance_never_below_armed_core`, `_rises_with_violence_legitimacy` (radicalization), `_rises_with_cohesion_and_grievance` (the threat term), `_zero_at_unarmed` (zero-at-zero) — 20/20 faction_v2 module tests. (4) Integration test `faction_v2_fighting_strength_links_to_protests` extended: live formed factions expose bounded strength/threat/resistance with **resistance ≥ strength** asserted per faction, plus the behavioral delta — a radicalized faction (LoV 0.9 vs 0.2, identical arms) resists strictly harder, and an enforcement sweep (0.30–0.60) proves an enforcement level that suppresses the moderate protest yet fails against the radicalized one (thresholds ~0.406 vs ~0.456). **Zero golden/snapshot drift** — the consumer is structurally unreachable before faction formation (seed-42 first faction at 20–30K ticks), so the 2000-tick golden and all snapshots are byte-identical; no recalibration needed. **791 sim + 196 integration = 987 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (no in-region hits; the pre-existing integration_tests.rs:5023/5270/5300 pedantic hits are untouched HEAD code), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) `fighting_strength` remains the armed core for *actual conflicts* (the §29.2 conflict path legitimately uses raw strength); only the *suppression* decision was upgraded to the full threat model — verified by grep that no other suppression decision (agent-tier institutional suppression at sim.rs:4877/7559) reads faction strength; (b) the LoV amplifier is inert at saturation by design (documented at the method); (c) a faction can carry high LoV from martyrdom (`record_casualties`) even with moderate grievance — the radicalization channel is therefore independent of the grievance channel, both folded in.

- **Iteration 99 (AP2 §8.1.4 the tenderness emotion family's first decision consumer — tenderness→helping, the remaining-work report's item #2's second live channel)**: after Iter-98 wired loneliness into the social-seeking gate, tenderness (the compassionate-warmth family member) was still felt-but-unread — a tender agent helped neighbors at exactly the same rate as a cold one. Plan anchor: §8.1.4 "emotions should not just be felt; they should shape decisions." Design: (1) parameters.rs — new `social_tenderness_help_multiplier: Fixed` (0.5); (2) sim.rs — the `system_social_interactions` tuple-build site (the same site Iter-98 threaded loneliness through) folds `tenderness × multiplier` into `help_propensity` before `.clamp_01()`, and `choose_interaction`'s existing Help window `help_bound = 0.5·(1+propensity).clamp(0.5,1.0)` already clamps — no signature churn, no new consumer plumbing; zero-at-zero (tenderness 0 → the exact legacy formula), **never lowers** the Help window, and the multiplier is the only lever that scales the saturated value. (3) Unit test `tenderness_boosts_help_bound` in interaction.rs (9/9 module tests). (4) Integration test `tenderness_channel_boosts_helping_when_multiplier_active` — multiplier 0 vs 0.5, same seed: **2108 vs 2660 Help interactions @2000 (~26%)**, assertion at +12% for headroom. **Root-caused probe insight**: the initial probe crafted agent tenderness (0.9) and got EXACT equality (2660 == 2660) — diagnostics inside the fold proved the crafted value reached the gate (tender=0.9, hp=0.45 vs 0), so the miss was in the *consumer's reachability*: tenderness **saturates to 1.0 naturally within a few ticks** (appraisal adds positive·(1−status_threat) ≈ 0.3/tick with no decay on the expanded families), while affection needs hundreds of ticks to cross the 0.7 high-affection threshold — so by the time the Help window is reachable, both runs sit at hp=0.5 → hb=0.75 → identical behavior. The channel was live all along; the multiplier (not the crafted emotion) is the correct lever, and the final test varies exactly that. **Behavioral consequences (probe-pinned, documented per test) — a full-blast consumer**: the default 0.5 multiplier with saturated tenderness lifts steady-state help_bound 0.5 → 0.75 for all agents, shifting the interaction mix village-wide: (a) `collapse_famine_timing_shapes_plague_mortality` re-pinned to windows 900/1100/1300 with a strict MID-PEAK (900→2, 1100→5, 1300→2 @4320 — the Iter-98 pin's 1000/1100/1200 windows now tie, the probe showed the extra help/comfort softens the early-famine die-off so the curve peaks later); (b) `pestilence_epidemic_onset_outpaces_riverford` — the epidemic is now **transient again**: 1500→4, 4000→2 carriers, then clears by 8000 (the tenderness→helping channel reshaped the arc; more help = less contact stress spread) — re-pinned from the Iter-98 persistence tail (≥1 @12000) to onset-outpaces + 4000-window ≥1 + burnout-by-12000, with riverford-stays-clean; (c) `conception_pregnancy_birth_pipeline_runs_and_is_seed_deterministic` — seed-46 80K births now **1** (was 3: [22450, 42800, 56370]) — downstream asserts recalibrated 3 → 1 (live children with parentage, marriage-recorded births, birth-ticks length); (d) golden hash regenerated + 5 snapshots re-accepted. **787 sim + 196 integration = 983 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (no in-region hits), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the Help-window saturation interaction — tenderness saturates to 1.0 while affection rises slowly, so the window's steady state is tenderness-driven (the crafted-emotion probe documented the transient); (b) the consumer is tick-0-active through the appraisal build-up, but its *differentiation* only appears once the high-affection branch is reachable (~hundreds of ticks) — village-level Help counts are the honest measure; (c) the collapse mid-peak and pestilence transience are consequences of the interaction-mix shift, not disease-model changes — both documented at their pins.

- **Iteration 98 (AP2 §8.1.4 the loneliness emotion family's first decision consumer — loneliness→social-seeking, the remaining-work report's item #2's first live channel)**: the expanded 14-family appraisal system computed contempt/awe/envy/loneliness/tenderness/humiliation/relief/despair/nostalgia **every tick with zero decision consumers** — only disgust (attraction), gratitude (moral emotions), jealousy (bonds), hope (memes) and moral_outrage (moral values) were read; a lonely agent sought social contact at exactly the same rate as a contented one. Plan anchor: §8.1.4 "emotions should not just be felt; they should shape decisions" — loneliness is the belonging-seeking signal. Design: (1) interaction.rs — `system_social_interactions`' agent tuple grows to 12 elements (appending `loneliness: Fixed`), and the interaction-chance gate becomes `base + extraversion × extraversion_multiplier + loneliness × social_loneliness_multiplier` — a **monotone additive boost** (a lonely agent clears the gate more often), **zero-at-zero** (loneliness 0 → the exact legacy formula; the gate roll is still drawn unconditionally, so replay determinism holds per seed — though converted interactions consume more downstream RNG in `select_interaction_target`/`choose_interaction`, which is precisely why the golden drifted), and **never lowers the gate** (no penalty channel). (2) parameters.rs — new `social_loneliness_multiplier: Fixed` (0.3). (3) sim.rs — the construction site threads `emotions[i].loneliness` (one-tick lag, consistent with the existing `anger` in the tuple — appraisal runs after the social block in the same tick). (4) Two unit tests — `loneliness_raises_interaction_gate` (1000 `system_social_interactions` calls on the same seed: lonely agent converts strictly more rolls; the reviewer-mandated comment now states runs are replay-deterministic, not stream-identical) and `loneliness_never_lowers_the_gate` (monotonicity + zero-at-zero of the formula). (5) Integration test `loneliness_drives_social_seeking` — crafted uniformly-lonely village **45,666 vs baseline 36,810 interactions @2000 (~24%)**, assertion at +14% for headroom, plus a byte-exact determinism leg. **Behavioral consequences (probe-pinned, documented per test) — a full-blast tick-0-active consumer (loneliness reaches mean 0.66 by 2000, so golden + 14 snapshots re-accepted)**: (a) **seed-42 now conceives at tick 690** (accelerated courtship) — the conception golden-window leg moved to seed 46 (probe: seeds 43–47 clean @2000) and the deep leg's seed-46 timeline **accelerated to THREE births [22450, 42800, 56370] complete by 80K** (the 120K→80K horizon is a suite-time win, ~191s→169s); (b) `conception_pipeline_round_trips_with_birth` total horizon 4000 → **3000** — probe-pinned the mothers die and are replaced between 3000 and 4000 (born_sum 2 → 0), so the delivery-time `children_born` assert now lives where the mothers are still live (horizon-sensitivity documented at the pin per review); (c) `collapse_famine_timing_shapes_plague_mortality` mortality curve reshaped to a **MID-PEAK** (pest@1000 = 3, @1100 = 5, @1200 = 2 at 4320 — the plague ~300 ticks after famine onset kills most; early: population still strong; late: famine-driven adaptations carried it through the worst) — re-pinned from the Iter-97 late-window-monotone to mid-window-peak; (d) `kinship_penalty_rises_when_families_form` — seed-51 family formation shifted again, so the crafted birth rate doubles **6.0 → 12.0 @3000** (probe: 6.0 needs the 5000 horizon; 12.0 → max penalty 0.5 @3000); (e) **the pestilence epidemic is now ENDEMIC** — the loneliness consumer sustains transmission: probe-pinned 2 carriers at every window 6,000–12,000 (it no longer clears; sustained contact keeps the chain alive). `pestilence_epidemic_onset_outpaces_riverford` re-pinned from the clearance tail (0 @8000) to the **persistence tail** (≥1 @12000) with a riverford-stays-clean leg — a genuine new emergent equilibrium, documented as such; (f) `social_cost_mirrors_notoriety_and_d4_survives_mixed_village` — the exact social_cost≡notoriety mirror is a **daily-pass race** (a crime recorded after the last 24-tick refresh leaves a one-pass lag: probe diffs only at 2000, none at 1992–1998/2016/2040) — horizon moved 2000 → **2016** (84×24 refresh boundary), fragility documented; (g) `rituals_fire_and_bond_participants` — the GLOBAL mean rv2 trust now **drifts DOWN** over a long run (0.5008 @2000 → 0.4822 @20000: more interactions include more conflict acts, outrunning bonding), so the "avg trust rises" assert died; the test now pins the bonding effect in the **distribution** — a differentiated high-trust tail (probe: 48 pairs > 0.6, mean 0.7079 @20000) with ≥20 pairs above 0.6 and mean > 0.65, plus the trust-stays-differentiated ceiling. **786 sim + 195 integration = 981 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (1 in-region doc-quote hit fixed; the pre-existing integration_tests.rs:5225 `map_or` pedantic hit in the untouched marriage test is HEAD code), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the gate's **calibration ceiling** — max `interact_chance` = 0.3 + 0.4 + 0.3 = 1.0 for a max-lonely max-extravert agent (guaranteed interaction every tick); with loneliness saturating at 1.0 for many agents, high-lonely agents sit near 0.8 chance — the 24% village-level differentiation is well-behaved, but a future tuning pass should watch the ceiling; (b) the consumer is **tick-0-active** (loneliness rises from the first appraisal events — not zero-at-zero in real runs, only at the crafted 0 level); (c) the pestilence endemicity is a **consequence of the contact-rate lift**, not a disease-model change — the epidemic would clear again if the loneliness channel were removed; (d) `loneliness_never_lowers_the_gate` re-derives the formula rather than exercising the production path (accepted: it pins the monotonicity/zero-at-zero property; the production path is covered by `loneliness_raises_interaction_gate`).

- **Iteration 97 (AP2 §8.1.2 attention feedback — percept-level bias folding into `compute_salience`, closing the loop where computed attention biases had ZERO consumers, the remaining-work report's item #6)**: the attention system computed rich bias state every tick (`recompute_biases` derives extraversion/openness/fear/anger/trauma weights) and stored flat `salience_bias` multipliers, but **nothing fed back** — `event_relevance` never read the computed biases, memory-encoding salience was personality-blind, and `attention_capacity()` sat exposed-but-unread. Plan anchor: §8.1.2's attention must shape what agents notice and remember. Design: (1) attention.rs — new `percept_bias_factor(&SimEvent) -> Fixed` (0.75..1.25) folding extraversion/openness (social/novelty affinity) against fear/anger/trauma (threat vigilance: Threat/Conflict events get a fear/anger/trauma-driven boost, pro-social events an extraversion/openness boost), folded as a multiplier inside `compute_salience` so **memory-encoding salience** (the 0.2 gate into episodic encoding) reflects the agent's biases. **Zero-at-zero**: all-neutral personality (0.5 extraversion/openness, 0 fear/anger/trauma) yields **exactly 1.0** — unit-asserted (`Fixed::ONE`), so the fold is a pure deviation multiplier, never a global offset. **Deterministic** (a pure function of event kind + agent state — no RNG, replay stream untouched). Scope discipline: the fold lives ONLY inside `compute_salience` (memory salience); utility/decision paths are untouched — verified by grep that `percept_bias_factor` is called at exactly one production site. (2) Four unit tests — neutral→exactly-1.0 identity, extraverted-social-boost > neutral, fearful-threat-boost > neutral, and the 0.75 floor under maximal fear/anger/trauma (16/16 attention tests pass). (3) Integration test `attention_biases_feed_social_memory_encoding` — seed-42 split on extraversion: extravert social-memory episodes **587 vs introvert 294 (~2×)**, threshold-asserted with headroom, deterministic. **Behavioral consequences (probe-pinned, documented per test) — the campaign's second tick-0-active consumer, blast radius wider than Iter-96**: golden hash regenerated + 7 snapshots re-accepted (salience→memory→behavior compounds from the first tick), and **10 integration tests recalibrated** in three clusters: (a) the enforcement/hypocrisy trio share one root — the social-memory surge makes witnessed enforcement fire slightly more (counts ≤ 2, no-violence hypocrisy pin moved ≤ 0.1 → **0.25** with probe 0.200; names kept, bodies updated to "armed and lightly active"); (b) trajectory inversions — `collapse_famine_timing_shapes_plague_mortality` axis **flipped** to late-window-kills-most (pest@1000 = 2 < 1100 = 4 < 1200 = 6; a plague landing later hits a famine-weakened population — the opposite of Iter-96's early-kill pin), `conception_pregnancy_birth_pipeline_runs_and_is_seed_deterministic` birth moved to **115,300/120k** (fifth recalibration), `dominant_need_urgency_biases_selection` **redesigned** from the population-level 2000-tick eat-count (now confounded by food depletion — voracious villages exhaust scarce grain, inverting aggregates) to the **individual first-12-tick signature** (baseline 0, hungry 4, fearful-hungry 8 — hunger dominates even under maximal fear; the Iter-96 "fearful eats far less" suppression is a population-level depletion artifact, and the test doc comment was rewritten to say exactly that); (c) four individual pins — `pestilence_epidemic_onset_outpaces_riverford` clearance horizon moved (onset 4 carriers @1500 still beats riverford 0; clears by **8000**), `mental_scenarios_generate_across_run` dread pin to 0.1885, `clan_identity_myths_and_honor_codes_live` cohesion peak 0.4797 (pin 0.45, margin documented), `executive_function_planning_confidence_tracks_ef_depth` blend delta 0.0045 (budget-gated lag). **784 sim + 194 integration = 978 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (3 in-region hits fixed: unused `Fixed` import, `match`→`if let` for the collapse shock window, one redundant `config.clone()`; the pre-existing attention.rs pedantic hits and the relational_fields redundant clone are untouched HEAD code), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the fold's 2× social-memory differentiation is the strongest single-agent channel in the campaign (probe: 294 vs 587 episodes) — calibration is conservative by design (0.75..1.25 band) with headroom documented for a future tuning pass; (b) the dominant_need integration test's Iter-96 suppression narrative was corrected in this iteration (the individual-level first-12-tick probe shows hunger dominates even under maximal fear — the population-level suppression was a depletion artifact, not a mechanism claim); (c) reviewer-verified zero-at-zero: the neutral-personality unit test pins exactly `Fixed::ONE`, so the golden drift is per-agent deviation, not a constant offset.

- **Iteration 96 (AP2 §8.1.5 the dominant-need urgency boost wired into action selection — `dominant_need`'s first production consumer, the remaining-work report's item #5)**: the full five-factor motivation pressure formula (`need_deficit × personality_weight × emotional_amplification × cultural_legitimacy × situational_affordance`, Iteration 44) computed `dominant_need` (the argmax) **every tick with zero consumers** — sim.rs:2324 even documented the write-only fold, and the `utility_weights.need_relief` weight was dead too; action selection ran purely on raw deficits. Plan anchor: §8.1.5's motivation dynamics must shape decisions. Design: an **exclusive urgency boost** in `compute_utility` — only actions whose relief channel matches the dominant need receive `+dominant_pressure × 0.4`, with `Hunger → hunger_relief`, `Thirst → thirst_relief`, `Sleep → fatigue_relief`, `Meaning → bonus_meaning_relief`, and `_ => false`. **Zero-at-zero** (pressure 0 → no boost; the neutral prior carries no urgency), **deterministic** (a pure utility term — the RNG stream is untouched), **additive over the raw-deficit terms** (the boost is the five-factor urgency nudge the raw terms lack — emotional/cultural/scarcity amplification, not a duplicate of deficit²). The `_ => false` exclusivity is the point: an **abstract dominant need with no relief channel (Safety, Esteem, ...) yields zero boost** — a fearful agent's argmax flips to Safety under fear amplification, so the urgency nudge is withheld from food-seeking (the raw hunger term still fires; the fear emotion modifier steers toward withdrawal — the unit tests isolate the boost's exclusivity precisely). Trade also receives the Hunger boost (Trade carries `hunger_relief` 0.3 — market grain counts as hunger relief, intended). (1) actions.rs — `DecisionContext` gained `dominant_need: MotiveCategory` + `dominant_pressure: Fixed`; `compute_utility` gained the two params + the boost; `select_action` passes them through; 5 unit-test DecisionContext sites + 7 direct `compute_utility` test calls updated. (2) sim.rs — the single construction site passes `self.agents[i].motivation.dominant_need` + `dominant_pressure()` (the motivation update runs in the 0b phase, before action selection in the same tick — the argmax is always fresh). (3) Four unit tests — `dominant_need_boost_prefers_matching_channel` (identical-noise fresh RNG streams: Hunger-dominant Eat > Thirst-dominant Eat at identical needs), `abstract_dominant_need_yields_no_boost` (Safety-dominant == zero-pressure **exact**), `dominant_boost_scales_with_pressure` (0.2 < 0.9), `dominant_boost_does_not_fire_for_non_relieving_actions` (Work == zero-pressure exact under Hunger-dominant). (4) Integration test `dominant_need_urgency_biases_selection` — crafted hungry population Eat count **660 vs baseline 331** (~2×), fearful-hungry **128** (below baseline — the emergent fight-or-flight suppression), both with >2× assertion headroom, seed-42 deterministic. **Behavioral consequences (probe-pinned, documented per test)**: (a) the collapse mortality axis shifted — pestilence now kills most **right after famine onset** (pest@1000 = 7 > 1100 = 5 > 1200 = 4 at 4320) and the famine-**magnitude** axis died empirically (mag 0.5/0.6/0.8 identical at every window) → `collapse_famine_timing_shapes_plague_mortality` re-pinned to the post-onset window axis with the magnitude-axis loss made explicit; (b) `courtship_ladder_advances_in_live_run` switched seed 42 → **45** @ 10k (probe: n=2, max Betrothal — seed 42 now forms none even at 20k); (c) `kinship_penalty_rises_when_families_form` — seed 51's default-demography birth delayed past 40K, so the second leg now elevates the birth rate (6.0, the children-test crafting; probe: max penalty 0.5 @ 3000) while the founding-village zero legs are untouched; (d) the epidemic now **clears fully by ~2,200** (proactive food-seeking strengthens the immune response: 4 carriers @ 1,200–1,500, 2 @ 1,800, 0 @ 2,200) — the long-horizon persistence signature died → `pestilence_epidemic_persists_beyond_riverford` **renamed** `pestilence_epidemic_onset_outpaces_riverford`, pinning the onset-vs-clearance arc (4 @ 1500 > riverford 0, and 0 @ 2200) — distinct from the ≥4-carrier peak pin in `pestilence_seeds_epidemic_outbreak`; (e) `children_resemble_parents_statistically` — the pairing/birth sample shifted, so the sweep widened to 16 seeds @ 4000 (n=18 pairs, avg 0.339 vs the 0.4 threshold; the old 10-seed/3000 sample hovered at 0.401 with n=6); (f) `conception_pregnancy_birth_pipeline_runs_and_is_seed_deterministic` — seed-46's timeline collapsed to a **single birth @ 67,510** (10,890/34,010/45,760 after Iter-95) with the record chain 1/1/1/0 (parentage, marriage children, children_born, lingering pregnancies), the test's fifth recalibration. **780 sim + 194 integration = 974 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift (golden regenerated + 14 snapshots accepted — the boost's Eat/Drink/Rest preference shifts are real but bounded), clippy clean in all touched regions (3 in-region pedantic hits fixed: `map_or`→`is_none_or` + 2 format-inlines; the pre-existing actions.rs:408 Trade-block lint is untouched HEAD code), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the boost amplifies already-dominant channels (urgency stacking is intentional — the nudge is ~20–30% of the raw hunger/thirst terms at bio-critical pressure, a larger relative share right at the 0.4 threshold where it can legitimately flip choices); (b) `dominant_pressure()` recomputes the 19-category full formula a second time per agent per tick (the first pass is inside `update_dominant`) — negligible at 12 agents, cached-value refactor noted for a future perf pass; (c) the fearful-hungry suppression (128 vs 660) mixes the argmax exclusivity with the pre-existing fear emotion modifier — the integration test documents both mechanisms, the unit tests isolate the boost.

- **Iteration 95 (AP2 §8.1.11 the speech-act relational effect model APPLIED — the remaining-work report's item #7)**: `SpeechAct::resolve_effect()` computed trust/affection/status/obligation/reputation deltas ("Language should be action") but **nothing applied them** — speech acts were interpreted, logged to the bounded `speech_log`, and the effects discarded. Plan anchor: §8.1.11's speech → relationship/belief-update channel. Audit finding that shaped the design: the **trust/affection channels are already live** — `system_social_interactions` applies per-kind deltas whose values **exactly equal** the grounded `base_delta` table in speech_act.rs (Talk/Inform 0.01/0.005, Help/Promise 0.05/0.03, Threaten −0.1/−0.05, Trade/Request 0.02/0, Gossip 0.005/0.01, Comfort/Reassure 0.03/0.05, Insult −0.08/−0.1, Teach/Persuade 0.04/0.02 — verified byte-for-byte), so applying `resolve_effect`'s trust/affection would **double-count**. The consumer therefore applies only the **three un-applied channels** at the live speech-act creation site (sim.rs ~3496): (1) `obligation_delta` → the listener's `relationship_v2s[listener→speaker].obligation` (Help/Promise +0.03, Trade/Request +0.01 — promises and requests create the listener's debt; the daily power-balance pass at ~2839 and the agent-level `social_obligation` aggregate at ~8871 already read obligation, so this is a real behavioral channel, not observational); (2) `reputation_delta` → the listener's `relationship_v2s[listener→speaker].admiration` (Talk/Teach +0.01, Threaten/Insult −0.02, Gossip −0.005 — the speaker's standing in the listener's eyes); (3) `status_delta` → the **speaker's** `relationship_v2s[speaker→listener].respect` (Comfort/Reassure +0.02, Threaten/Insult −0.02 — feeds `RelationshipV2::quality()` which weights respect ×0.2). All deltas `clamp_01`, **pure and deterministic (no RNG — the replay stream is untouched)**, and the `ti < n` guard mirrors the existing familiarity guard (only `fi` is checked at the block top). **Interpretive scope (deliberate, documented)**: status_delta is mapped to the speaker's regard for the listener's standing ("aggression demeans the victim in the aggressor's eyes") because the per-pair relationship store has no listener-self-status slot — a future reader should not "fix" it toward a per-agent status that the daily status derivation would overwrite. **Blast radius was the campaign's smallest**: the golden hash stayed **byte-identical** (the rv2 fields are not in `agent_summaries()`), only **one** snapshot drifted (`metrics_500_ticks`, accepted — a relationship-metric aggregate), and **one** integration test drifted: the speech channels accelerated seed-46's trust/courtship formation so a **third** pregnancy-path birth now lands at 45,760 — `conception_pregnancy_birth_pipeline_runs_and_is_seed_deterministic` re-pinned to `[10890, 34010, 45760]` with the record chain scaled to 3/3/3/0 (parentage, marriage children, children_born, lingering pregnancies; the test's fourth recalibration, each documented in its doc comment). (1) Integration test `speech_acts_apply_relational_effects` — probe-pinned on seed 42 @ 2000: obligation sum 21.7, admiration sum 31.4, respect moved off its 0.3 construction baseline on 64 of 132 relationship entries (threshold assertions), plus a byte-exact determinism leg over every (obligation, admiration, respect) triple. **776 sim + 193 integration = 969 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (zero new hits), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented notes**: (a) the reputation→admiration channel **saturates** — raw potential ~84.7 from 8,470 Talk acts vs observed sum 31.4, so most pairs clamp admiration at 1.0 early (a saturating one-shot rather than a differentiating gradient — headroom noted for a future calibration pass, not a defect); (b) `resolve_effect()` runs unconditionally even when the listener index is out of range (the application is guarded; the computation is a micro-cost on a non-hot path).

- **Iteration 94 (AP2 §9.2 the neural-like RL learning→action loop closed — `learned_delta`'s first production consumer, the remaining-work report's priority #7)**: the §9.2 machinery was fully built and *learning* was already wired (every successful action EMA-updates the four valuation components via `learn_from_outcome`) — but the learned values had **zero consumers**: the selection loop never read them, so action choice was blind to experience (the RL loop was a write-only sink). Plan anchor: §9.2 "neural-like RL values → action selection" — learning must shape choices. Design: a **learned-delta bias** added to `compute_utility` (`utility += learned_delta(outcome_profile)`), where the delta is `(dot(learned, profile) − 0.5·sum(profile)) / sum(profile)` — **zero at tick 0** (values start at the 0.5 neutral prior → learned ≡ prior → delta ≡ 0, so the golden baseline shifts only where learning has actually fired, not by a constant offset), **deterministic** (no RNG — a pure utility term, so the argmax shifts without perturbing the RNG stream), and the agent's own learned weights fold in per-candidate. **Structural flaw caught in review before shipping**: the un-normalized delta `dot(learned) − 0.5·sum` is systematically biased — because every profile component ≤ 0.5, all learned values EMA-converge *downward* from the prior, so every delta is negative and the raw `0.5·sum` baseline rewards low-profile-sum actions (Rest) regardless of what the agent learned; probe-pinned ranking was Rest > Work ≈ Trade > Eat > Socialize > Worship — the opposite of need-seeking. **Fixed by normalizing the profile by its own sum** — the baseline becomes a uniform 0.5 across candidates and only per-component learned deviations matter (`dot(learned − prior, profile/sum)`): probe-pinned ranking flips to **Eat > Work > Trade > Rest > Worship > Socialize** (adaptive need-seeking). The shared `ActionKind::outcome_profile()` is a single source of truth with the learning site (the refactor was verified byte-identical to the original inline table — Work 0.4/0/0/0.1, Eat 0.5/0.1/0/0, Socialize 0/0.1/0.4/0, Worship 0/0.2/0.1/0.3, Trade 0.2/0/0.1/0.1, default 0.05×4 — so learning-side behavior is unchanged, only the new consumer shifts trajectories). (1) neural_like.rs — `learned_delta` normalized + zero-sum guard (no real profile has sum 0; defensive). (2) actions.rs — `DecisionContext.action_values` (Copy, 5 Fixed fields), `ActionKind::outcome_profile`, the utility term, `select_action` pass-through; 5 test DecisionContext sites + 8 direct `compute_utility` test calls updated. (3) sim.rs — per-agent `action_values` threaded into the DecisionContext; the learning site refactored onto `outcome_profile`. (4) Unit test `learned_delta_is_zero_at_prior_and_tracks_learning` — exact `== Fixed::ZERO` at the prior; work-learner favors the work profile over socialize; symmetric socialize-learner flip. **The campaign's heaviest recalibration** (a tick-0-active consumer — the documented Iteration-66-precedent cost): golden hash regenerated, 5 snapshots re-accepted, and 7 integration tests recalibrated probe-pinned. **Magnitude decision (deliberate, no scale factor)**: the normalized spread is ~0.32 (Eat −0.17 … Socialize −0.49) vs the ±0.05 behavior-noise band — a strong shaping force but not dominant over the need/identity terms (~0.2–1.0); the delta *is* the learned signal, and the suppression-heavy structure is the sim being honest about its own payoff structure (socializing rarely succeeds in this world → its learned value converges low → the consumer disincentivizes it). Documented structural note: because values EMA only *downward* from the 0.5 prior, every delta is ≤ 0 — the consumer is a **suppression gradient** (learning expresses as reduced utility for mismatched actions), never a positive-incentive channel; the relative ranking is what drives the argmax. **Behavioral consequences (probe-pinned, documented per test)**: (a) famine-vs-no-famine mortality flattened (collapse 5 = famineless 5 = solo 5 — adaptive eating holds population health comparable with or without famine) → `collapse_compounds_crises_beyond_pestilence_alone` rewritten as `collapse_famine_timing_shapes_plague_mortality`, pinning the surviving erosion-timing axis (pest@1100 mid-erosion 5 > pest@950 pre-erosion 3 > famine-0.8 cull-and-recover 2); (b) epidemic mortality went to zero (0 deaths at 4,320/8,000/12,000/20,000; avg health ≈ riverford) → `pestilence_kills_more_agents_than_riverford` rewritten as `pestilence_epidemic_persists_beyond_riverford` (surviving invariant: epidemic carriers persist 2 > 0 at 4,320 while riverford stays clean); (c) the stress→conflict slope narrowed to ~0.015 (probe: high 0.430 n=172 vs low 0.415 n=53 on seeds 0–19; the old 10-seed sample flips 0.478 vs 0.550) → `stress_correlates_with_conflict_across_seeds` widened to 20 seeds — the §18.4 coupling is now **weakly monotone** (t ≈ 0.2, statistically thin but deterministic), not robustly correlated; (d) the seed-46 birth timeline shifted a third time → `conception_pregnancy_birth_pipeline_runs` pins `[10890, 34010]` (Iter-92 → Iter-93 → Iter-94 recalibrations documented in the test); (e) the accelerated seed-42 pregnancy **stalls** (conceived 1,530, never delivered by 10,000 — that mother's action stream keeps health×nutrition < 0.2, gestation rate ≈ 0) → `conception_pipeline_round_trips_with_birth` switched to seed 44 (probe-pinned: conceptions 160/690/1,270, deliveries 2,690/3,500/3,770); (f) the seed-43 D4 ceiling fell to 0.384 at t=2,000 → `social_cost_mirrors_notoriety_and_d4_survives_mixed_village` and `status_attraction_live_and_d4_reachable` switched to seed 44 (probe-pinned max total_attraction 0.511, margin 0.111 over the 0.4 gate; 7/12 notorious, min notoriety 0.00); (g) the kinship, meme, EF, and executive tests needed **no** change (seed-51 kinship ties form by 5,000 again — faster family formation from adaptive eating; derived memes present at t=2,000; planning-confidence blend aligns within 0.0008 — the RL consumer's re-ranked action streams landed those trajectories back inside the pinned windows). **776 sim + 192 integration = 968 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (2 pedantic hits fixed in-touch: `&mut s.shocks` loop style + the unused `i` in the status test; the pre-existing lints at actions.rs:377/425, neural_like.rs:129, integration_tests.rs:228/235/238/1970 are untouched HEAD code whose line numbers merely shifted), benches build, no debug leftovers. **Documented scope**: the consumer is a **relative shaping bias on `compute_utility`** — it cannot force an action (strong needs/identity terms still dominate); it re-ranks candidates at the margin; the learned values remain a pure function of outcome experience (no exploration noise, no RNG — full replay determinism); the normalization is per-action (each profile scaled by its own sum), so no global constant appears in the golden trajectories.

- **Iteration 93 (AP2 §8.1.10 the prescriptive "Obey Ruler" norm made behavioral — the last unwired default norm consumer, an authority-compliance gate)**: the Iteration-90/91 scope notes both named Obey Ruler as the final default norm with no behavioral consumer — it was internalized by every monthly ritual (and reinforced ×1.5 at the temple since Iteration 90's `Institution.norm_ids` wiring) yet **never read in production**, so an agent defied the village's authority at the same rate regardless of conviction. Plan anchor: the authority/subversion moral foundation (§8.1.10) and "ritual obedience" (§8.1.18). Probe-verified the substrate first: the Council designates exactly one **Guard Captain** role holder (high dominance + conscientiousness, populate-time, excluding the elder/priest/merchant candidates — distinct from the Elder **by construction**); on seed 42 the anchor is **agent 0** (elder = agent 3) and is **stable across 9,000 ticks**; "Obey Ruler" is internalized post-ritual (max 0.276, mean 0.179, armed 12/12 at 9K). Fix: (1) norms.rs — new `OBEY_RULER_NORM_ID = 3` constant. (2) interaction.rs `choose_interaction` — two new parameters `obey_ruler_propensity` + `target_is_authority`; when the target is the Guard Captain, `obey_scale = 1 − propensity` (clamped) scales **both** defiant branches: the stress-driven negativity window (`negativity_prob × elder_scale × obey_scale` — the threatened act converts to an insult) and the low-trust threat threshold (`0.3 × threat_scale × elder_scale × obey_scale` — converts the threat into cautious talk). The RNG draw stays **unconditional** (only thresholds change → identical stream position → replay determinism). `system_social_interactions` tuple grows to 11 elements (`obey_ruler_propensity`, `is_authority` appended; the existing `#[expect(clippy::type_complexity)]` covers it). (3) sim.rs — resolves the Guard Captain **once per tick** by id (`OBEY_RULER_NORM_ID` name + `get_role_holder("Guard Captain")`); a deceased captain clears the anchor (the gate naturally goes inert — no authority to defy); the per-agent propensity + `Some(i) == authority_idx` flag thread into the tuple, and the call site reads the **target's** flag at `agents[target_idx].10`. Zero-at-zero: no internalized norm before the first monthly ritual (tick 4320) → obey_scale 1.0 → golden baselines byte-identical. (4) Unit test `obey_ruler_norm_suppresses_defiance_toward_guard_captain` — low-trust isolated setup, 2000 draws on the **same seed-42 roll sequence** at three propensities: baseline fires threats at the authority, full internalization yields exactly 0 (bound 0 → every roll fails), partial strictly fewer, and the non-authority control target keeps the legacy thresholds (the unaffected-path zero-drift pin). (5) Integration test `obey_ruler_norm_is_armed_and_authority_anchor_is_deterministic` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: every propensity 0), **authority-anchor correctness** (exactly one Guard Captain, `assert_ne!` vs the Elder anchor — the two gates never compound), post-ritual gate-input liveness (9000 ticks: max propensity > 0 bounded by 0.276), threat-system liveness, determinism of (propensity, threat-count) vectors. **Trajectory regression found, diagnosed, and recalibrated**: the gate legitimately perturbs the seed-46 post-4320 trajectory (suppressed Guard-Captain-directed threats shift the interaction stream), delaying the second pregnancy-path birth from **25,420 → 60,940** (probe-pinned) — `conception_pregnancy_birth_pipeline_runs_and_is_seed_deterministic` moved its liveness + determinism legs from 30K to **80K horizons** with birth ticks `[7480, 60940]` and the full record chain (parentage 2, marriage children 2, children_born 2, no lingering pregnancy) intact. **775 sim + 192 integration = 967 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (no new lints — the three pre-existing unit-test call sites gained `Fixed::ZERO, false`), benches build. **Documented scope**: the consumer is **target-conditional on the Council-designated Guard Captain role holder** — the ruler's enforcement arm — and it suppresses *defiance* (threat issuance in both branches) toward that authority, mirroring the Iteration-91 elder gate; this is a narrower reading than literal command compliance (the sim has no command/directive mechanism — the natural consumer is the suppression of hostile acts against the enforcer). The anchor is **distinct from the Elder by construction** (populate excludes elder candidates from guard selection), so the two gates never compound on the same target. **Suite-time note**: the seed-46 legs moved 30K → 80K (birth at 60,940), tripling that test's runtime (~145s; full integration suite ~209s → ~244s) — a deliberate cost for pin fidelity, not an oversight.

- **Iteration 92 (AP2 §7.2.6 / §19.5.F the conception→pregnancy→birth pipeline made live — the largest inert biological channel closed)**: the remaining-work audit flagged `attempt_conception` as having **zero production callers** — the full pregnancy machinery (PregnancyState, ~1,800-tick gestation advanced per-tick, `complete_pregnancy`) was built and dormant, and births flowed only through probabilistic demography. Probe-verified the substrate: the default seed-42 world has **no births by 9,000 ticks** (first birth 22,010, second 62,580 — both far beyond every snapshot/golden horizon ≤ 2,000), and `Marriage.children` was a **write-only field** (no consumer anywhere). Design: the conception decision **reuses the existing `should_birth` roll** — the same calibrated annual 0.3/couple rate, the same **single unconditional Social RNG draw** (drawn once per couple before any gender/pregnancy branching → stream position identical → replay determinism) — but for a couple with a female partner it now starts a pregnancy instead of an immediate birth; gestation advances in the already-wired per-tick biology pass (`EmbodiedState::tick_update` → `reproductive.tick_update`); at full term the newborn is born in `tick_birth_mechanics`. **Zero drift by construction**: the first conception fires past 9,000 ticks in every horizon ≤ 2,000, so all snapshots/golden baselines stay byte-identical. Fix: (1) sim.rs `tick_birth_mechanics` rewrite — the **conception pass** (`should_birth` → `PregnancyState::new` on the female partner, guarded by `pregnancy.is_none()` so an already-pregnant female makes a fired roll a natural no-op — birth spacing emerges from the gestation delay) and the **birth pass is now keyed off the pregnancy itself, not the couple**: a mother whose husband died mid-gestation (her `partner` cleared by `handle_agent_death`) still delivers; the father is resolved best-effort (live partner → active marriage's other spouse → None, a widow birth recorded single-parent); same-sex couples keep the **legacy immediate-birth path** (abstraction, behavior unchanged); all still gated by `MAX_POPULATION` (a full-term pregnancy is retained while capped and delivers on the first tick with space). (2) **Latent panic fixed — the newborn v2s matrix extension**: births append agents without extending the O(1) `relationship_v2s` matrix, so a birth after a death-replacement left the matrix asymmetric and the daily power-balance pass could index past a vec's length (surfaced by accelerated population testing); fixed by appending the newborn's row + one stranger-level entry per existing agent (the child is the last index, so its slot in any vec is the last position — append), with a cross-reference comment added at `rebuild_relationship_v2s_after_death` documenting the square-matrix invariant so future contributors don't "simplify" the append as redundant. (3) `Marriage.children` **recording at birth** — the write-only field now populated when a live marriage exists. (4) Three integration tests: `conception_pipeline_round_trips` (accelerated timescale — births deliver via the pregnancy path, event/registry-based assertions since accelerated aging kills children before the horizon; the first ChildBorn event is the legacy same-sex birth, the pregnancy-path births land after segment 1), `same_sex_couples_keep_legacy_immediate_birth` (all-male forced world with elevated birth rate — the legacy roll fires deterministically, proving the abstraction is preserved), `birth_pipeline_runs_live_in_default_world` (seed 46 — **2 births at 7,480 + 25,420 with `children_born = 2`**, the pregnancy path proven live in real runs, zero deaths so counters survive). **Trajectory recalibrations (probe-pinned)**: the pipeline legitimately delays births, so (a) `kinship_penalty_rises` — seed 51 @ 5,000 now shows penalty 0.5 with 1 birth (the birth was delayed past 2,000; probed and recalibrated), and (b) `snapshot_restore_preserves_kinship` — agents 0/1 have no children by 3,000/4,500 post-perturbation, so the InLaw leg now forges the kin directly (the test's purpose is restore fidelity, not birth timing). **774 sim + 191 integration = 965 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (5 pedantic hits fixed: `manual_contains`, 2× `map_unwrap_or`, 2× `unnecessary_map_or`; HEAD had 48 lint mentions in these files, my changes reduced it to 45), benches build. **Documented scope**: same-sex couples remain on the legacy immediate-birth abstraction (no pregnancy for same-sex — behavior unchanged); widow births are recorded single-parent (no father kinship links, no double-link risk since `add_link` doesn't dedupe); the full-term-retention-under-cap semantics are intentional.

- **Iteration 91 (AP2 §8.1.10 the prescriptive "Respect Elders" norm made behavioral — the last unwired default norm's target-conditional disrespect gate)**: the Iteration-90 scope note named Respect Elders as the final default norm with no behavioral consumer — it was internalized by every monthly ritual yet **never read in production**, so an elder was disrespected at the same rate regardless of anyone's convictions. Plan anchor: §7.2.4 "elders become respected". Probe-verified the substrate first: aging runs at 35,040 ticks/year, so ages span only 18–55.9 across every test horizon (max ~56 years at 30K) — a strict `age > 60` elder **never exists in practice**, so the live anchor is the Council's designated **Elder role** (assigned at populate). Fix: (1) norms.rs — new `RESPECT_ELDERS_NORM_ID = 2` constant. (2) interaction.rs `choose_interaction` — two new parameters `respect_elders_propensity` + `target_is_elder`; when the target is the Elder role holder, `elder_scale = 1 − propensity` (clamped) scales **both** disrespectful branches: the stress-driven negativity window (`negativity_prob × elder_scale` — the threatened act converts to an insult, verbal hostility persists while the aggressive act is suppressed, the Iteration-85 pattern) and the low-trust threat threshold (`0.3 × threat_scale × elder_scale` — converts the threat into cautious talk). The RNG draw stays **unconditional** (only thresholds change → identical stream position → replay determinism). `system_social_interactions` tuple gains the 9th element (the per-agent propensity; `#[expect(clippy::type_complexity)]` added — the tuple crossed the workspace's pedantic threshold at 9 fields). (3) sim.rs — resolves the Elder role holder + the norm name **once per tick by id** (`RESPECT_ELDERS_NORM_ID`), maps each agent's `norm_resistance(name)` as propensity and the per-agent `is_elder` flag into the tuple; the call site reads the **target's** flag at `agents[target_idx].8`. Zero-at-zero: no internalized norm before the first monthly ritual (tick 4320) → elder_scale 1.0 → golden baselines byte-identical. (4) Unit test `respect_elders_norm_suppresses_disrespect_toward_elders` — low-trust isolated setup, 2000 draws on the **same seed-42 roll sequence** at three propensities: baseline fires threats at the elder, full internalization yields exactly 0 (bound 0 → every roll fails), partial strictly fewer, and the non-elder control target keeps the legacy thresholds (the unaffected-path zero-drift pin). (5) Integration test `respect_elders_norm_is_armed_and_elder_anchor_is_deterministic` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: every propensity 0), post-ritual gate-input liveness (9000 ticks: max propensity > 0 bounded), **elder-anchor correctness** (exactly one agent carries the Elder role across the run — the Council's designate), threat-system liveness, determinism of (propensity, is_elder) vectors. **Trajectory regression found, diagnosed, and recalibrated**: the prosocial gate legitimately perturbs the seed-42 post-4320 trajectory (stash-verified: all four failures pass at HEAD, fail only with Iter-91) — the reduced threat volume delays faction radicalization, so (a) the three enforcement pins were recalibrated with probed bounds: exactly **one** No-Violence enforcement (count ≤ 1, hypocrisy factor ≤ 0.1 — zero headroom, an honest pin) now fires post-4320 in the 9000-tick window while the No-Theft channel stays **perfectly silent** (factor == ZERO — the public-farm design intact), and (b) the faction-attachment liveness pin now scans the **full registry history** rather than only active factions (the prosocial gate delays formation — the active faction at the 30K snapshot is always freshly formed; the full history shows non-Secure styles and decayed supplies, the original liveness signal). **774 sim + 188 integration = 962 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (the two interaction.rs pedantic hits introduced by the new tuple/params — `type_complexity` expect + an unused-variable rename — fixed; the pre-existing `foundational_beliefs` empty-line lint is in an untouched test file), benches build. **Documented scope**: the gate is **target-conditional on the Council-designated Elder role holder** (the age-based elder never exists at this timescale); it suppresses the disrespectful *acts* (threat issuance in both branches) toward the elder, mirroring the Iteration-85 threat gate, while insults and cautious talk persist; Obey Ruler's behavioral consumer (an authority-compliance decision) remains the last unwired norm consumer.

- **Iteration 90 (AP2 §12.5/§19.5.D the temple's norm declaration made live — `Institution.norm_ids` finally honored at its ritual)**: the remaining-work audit flagged the last unwired norm channel: `Institution.norm_ids` had **zero consumers anywhere** — the temple declares `norm_ids = [3]` ("Obey Ruler norm reinforced by temple"), but the Iteration-82 ritual loop reinforced **all** registry norms equally for every participant, so the declaration was inert and "Obey Ruler" — one of the three unwired default norms (with Help Neighbors now live since Iteration 89, and Respect Elders still observational) — internalized at the same rate as every other norm. Fix: (1) ritual.rs — new associated const `Ritual::INSTITUTIONAL_NORM_REINFORCEMENT_MULTIPLIER` (`Fixed::from_raw(15_000)` = 1.5) + new method `norm_reinforcement_for_institutional(internalization, declared)` returning base × multiplier for declared norms, base otherwise (the bool keeps ritual.rs decoupled from Institution). (2) sim.rs duodeca ritual block — before the participant loop, resolves the **sponsor institution's** declared norms once per ritual fire (`self.institutions.get(ritual.sponsor)` → `BTreeSet<u64>`; a missing sponsor or empty declaration `map_or_else` an empty set → legacy all-equal reinforcement), then the loop uses `norm_reinforcement_for_institutional(norm.internalization, sponsor_norms.contains(&norm.id))`. The Temple SeasonalPrayer (sponsor 1) now boosts "Obey Ruler" ×1.5 for its congregation; the Council communal meal (sponsor 0, no declaration) is unchanged. **Zero drift by construction**: the block runs only at monthly ritual fires (4320 ticks, beyond every snapshot/golden horizon ≤ 2000); the temple-declared norm has **no behavioral consumer**, so only the growth rate of an observational strength field changes — confirmed by the full suite with zero snapshot drift and byte-identical calibrated baselines. (3) Unit test `institutional_norm_reinforcement_weights_declared_norms` — base > 0, undeclared == base exactly, declared == base × 1.5 exactly, declared > base, zero internalization → 0 even when declared. (4) Integration test `temple_declared_norm_reinforces_preferentially` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: no internalized norms), post-ritual **differentiation** (9000 ticks: max "Obey Ruler" strength > max "Respect Elders" strength — the temple-declared norm internalizes faster than a base-reinforced undeclared sibling in the same loop; arithmetic pinned in the test: temple norm_reinforcement 0.23, Obey 0.4×1.5 = 0.138/fire vs Respect 0.5 = 0.115/fire, two fires by 9000 → 0.276 vs 0.230), clamp bound, determinism of (description, strength) vectors. **773 sim + 187 integration = 960 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (the pre-existing warnings at integration_tests.rs:5050/5159 and sim.rs:8445 remain untouched), benches build. **Documented scope**: the wiring is producer-side — "Obey Ruler" now internalizes faster for the temple faithful, but the norm's *behavioral* consumer (an authority-compliance decision) remains future work, as does "Respect Elders" (the last unwired default norm); a future scenario where the Council also declares norms (e.g., `[0]` "No Theft") will flow through the same mechanism automatically.

- **Iteration 89 (AP2 §8.1.10 the prescriptive "Help Neighbors" norm made behavioral — the first *amplifying* consumer, plus the coup-lifecycle bug it exposed)**: the audit found the ritual internalizes **five** default norms but only No Theft (Iteration 84) and No Violence (Iterations 83/85) had behavioral consumers — Help Neighbors, Respect Elders, and Obey Ruler were internalized by every monthly ritual yet **never read in production**. Fix: (1) norms.rs — new `HELP_NEIGHBORS_NORM_ID = 1` constant. (2) interaction.rs `choose_interaction` — new `help_neighbors_propensity` parameter; the high-affection branch's Help window grows from the legacy [0.2, 0.5) to `help_bound = (0.5 × (1 + propensity)).clamp(0.5, 1.0)` — full internalization expands it to [0.2, 1.0) (the agent always helps or comforts in high-affection contexts, never merely talks), the **prescriptive mirror** of the prohibitive norms' full-suppression at 1.0. Continuous (no cliff), **clamped both sides** (reviewer-consistent: a prescriptive norm amplifies but can never suppress the window, even for a degenerate out-of-range strength), and the RNG draw stays unconditional (same stream position at every propensity — replay determinism). `system_social_interactions` tuple gains the 7th field. (3) sim.rs — resolves the Help Neighbors norm name **once per tick by id** and maps each agent's `norm_resistance(name)` (the internalized-strength read shared with the prohibitive gates) as propensity into the tuple; zero-at-zero: no internalized norm before the first monthly ritual (tick 4320) → bound 0.5 → golden baselines byte-identical. (4) Unit test `help_neighbors_norm_amplifies_help_decision` — high-affection isolated setup, 2000 draws on the **same seed-42 roll sequence** at three propensities: baseline Help > 0, full strictly > baseline (the [0.5, 1.0) ring converts Talk → Help), partial strictly between, and the untouched [0, 0.2) Comfort window **identical** across runs (the unaffected-path zero-drift pin). (5) Integration test `help_neighbors_norm_amplifies_help` — golden-window zero propensity, post-ritual max propensity > 0 bounded, **Help-system liveness** (Help still fires — amplification, not suppression), determinism. **Regression found and fixed**: the change shifted the seed-42 30K emergent trajectory (more Help → more trust → fewer grievances) into a state exposing a **pre-existing coup-lifecycle asymmetry** — `faction_v2_fighting_strength_links_to_protests` failed with 1 v1 faction vs 3 active v2 records. The §7.3 coup path wiped **all** v1 factions (`retain`) but deactivated **only the revolting leader's** v2 record, leaving the other factions' records stale-active and violating the code's own §5.2 comment and the v1↔v2 1:1 invariant. Fix: (6) faction_v2.rs — new `deactivate_all()` registry method (every active record is orphaned by the retain — every v2 registration pushes a v1 faction first and the coup retain is the only v1-faction removal); (7) sim.rs coup path — `deactivate_all()` replaces the leader-only call before the retain; (8) unit test `registry_deactivate_all_marks_every_record_inactive` — two records, leader-only deactivation leaves 1 active (pins the exact asymmetry), `deactivate_all` → 0. **Documented scope**: (a) `deactivate_by_leader` is now production-dead (its only production caller was the coup path; the unit test keeps it exercised as the asymmetry pin); (b) the Help amplification is **actor-side and target-agnostic** — it grows the window for any high-affection pair rather than gating on `RelationshipKind::Neighbor` targets specifically, a deliberate simplification consistent with the actor-side No Theft/No Violence gates; (c) Respect Elders and Obey Ruler remain unwired (future consumers — the temple's `norm_ids = [3]` "Obey Ruler" declaration is still unread). **772 sim + 186 integration = 958 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (the two remaining warnings — sim.rs:8445 clone and integration_tests.rs:5050 — are pre-existing and untouched), benches build.

- **Iteration 88 (AP2 §8.1.10/§19.5.D the violence-enforcement audit made live — hypocrisy now gates the escalation decision)**: the Iteration-87 cross-act scope note named the violence channel as the inert hypocrisy consumer — no-violence `enforcement_count` was never populated (unlike the Iteration-86 theft audit), so the §8.1.10 hypocrisy channel could only restrain theft. Probe-verified before implementing: violence fires at tick ~2 on seed 42, all default events before the first monthly ritual (4320); even with escalation maxed (chance 1.0, rate 2.0) zero events fire post-ritual — violence is an early-window phenomenon. Fix: (1) sim.rs `should_escalate` — the Iteration-83 resistance resolution now resolves the no-violence norm name **once by id** (`Option<&str>` borrow of `self.norms`, cleaner than the nested closure) and compounds `chance = escalation_chance × (1 − resistance) × (1 − hypocrisy)` — the same multiplicative structure as the Iteration-87 theft gate (internal conviction vs social-learning shame as distinct channels); zero-at-zero (no witnessed enforcement pre-audit → legacy chance), continuous, no cliff, and no extra RNG (the draw stays unconditional — only the threshold changes). (2) sim.rs violence block — a **zero-RNG** witnessed-enforcement audit: unlike sneaky theft (which needs a detection roll), a violent act is inherently public, so no detection roll is drawn and the golden baseline stays byte-identical; when a violent act fires, every agent holding the internalized no-violence norm records it via the Iteration-86 `record_witnessed_enforcement` (village-scale witnessing, violator included, resolved by id via `NO_VIOLENCE_NORM_ID`; absent registry norm = no-op). Gated on holders — before the first monthly ritual no agent holds any norm, so the golden window is byte-identical. (3) Unit test `witnessed_no_violence_enforcement_compounds_escalation_gate` — full sensitivity + full exposure suppresses escalation at **zero norm strength** (the hypocrisy factor alone drives the chance to 0), partial sensitivity keeps a reduced non-cliff chance (math-only on purpose — a count-based probabilistic assert at chance 0.075 over 50 draws would be flaky). (4) Wiring-liveness unit test `violence_audit_increments_holders_when_violence_fires` — pre-internalize at zero strength on seed 42 → violence still fires at baseline → each holder's `enforcement_count` == the event count exactly (deliberate equality: proves every public violence event is witnessed by every holder), control stays norm-less. (5) Integration test `violence_audit_armed_but_silent_and_deterministic` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: violence fires but no norms → audit silent, zero drift), post-ritual armed-but-silent (9000 ticks: holders exist but default-world violence is exhausted pre-4320 → counts stay 0), determinism of (description, count) audit vectors. **The §8.1.10 loop now closes on both manifestations**: the violence channel's enforcement_count is populated (like theft's) and both gates compound resistance × hypocrisy. **Documented scope**: the no-violence audit uses the village-scale witnessing semantic established in Iteration-86 (every holder witnesses every public enforcement, violator included); faction coups and moral panics are collective/regime-level mechanisms, not individual decisions, and remain out of scope for an individual norm gate. **770 sim + 185 integration = 955 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build.

- **Iteration 87 (AP2 §8.1.10 hypocrisy made behavioral — the witnessed-enforcement audit's consumer)**: Iteration 86 populated `InternalizedNorm.enforcement_count`, but `MoralCognition.hypocrisy_sensitivity` (default 0.5) had **zero production consumers** — an agent who had seen the community punish theft many times was no more restrained than one who had never seen enforcement, so the plan's hypocrisy channel ("I have seen people punished for this; doing it myself would make me a hypocrite") never ran. Fix: (1) moral_cognition.rs — new `HYPOCRISY_ENFORCEMENT_CAP: u32 = 5` and `hypocrisy_factor(&self, description) -> Fixed`: `hypocrisy_sensitivity × exposure` where exposure = min(witnessed-enforcement count, 5)/5, **clamped to [0,1]** (reviewer-mandated — `hypocrisy_sensitivity` is a pub field a scenario config could deserialize out of range, and an over-1.0 factor would drive the gate's (1 − hypocrisy) negative and silently refuse every theft); zero count, absent norm, or zero sensitivity → 0. (2) sim.rs `enforce_theft` — the Iteration-84 gate restructured to resolve the norm name **by id** once (`Option<&str>` borrow of `self.norms`, cleaner than the nested closure) and now compounds: `amount = amount × (1 − resistance) × (1 − hypocrisy)`. **Deliberate multiplicative choice** (documented): the two mechanisms — internal conviction (norm strength) vs social-learning shame (witnessed enforcement) — are distinct psychological channels and compound; full refusal happens only when a **single** factor saturates, so the gate keeps the campaign's no-cliff property. Zero-at-zero: the default world's public-access farms never catch a theft → `enforcement_count` 0 everywhere → hypocrisy 0 → golden baselines byte-identical; the gate draws no RNG (the enforcement detection roll's stream position is unchanged whenever a theft still occurs). (3) Unit test `hypocrisy_factor_scales_with_witnessed_enforcement` — 0 → 0.1 → 0.5 saturation → beyond-cap plateau → other-norm isolation → zero-sensitivity 0. (4) Behavioral wiring unit test `witnessed_enforcement_hypocrisy_suppresses_theft` — full hypocrisy (sensitivity 1.0, zero strength, 5 enforcements) refuses outright (amount → 0), normless control takes the full 0.15, partial (0.5) takes 0.075, on a real stocked farm. (5) Integration test `hypocrisy_consumer_is_armed_but_silent` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: no internalized norms), post-ritual armed-but-silent state (9000 ticks: norms internalized, factor 0 everywhere), determinism. **Cross-act scope** (documented): the hypocrisy channel gates **theft only** because `enforcement_count` is populated solely on caught thefts; the no-violence `enforcement_count` is never written (the violence-enforcement audit lacks the caught/fined public semantics), so the Iteration-83 escalation gate is not yet hypocrisy-aware — the moment the violence audit is wired, the same factor extends that gate. **768 sim + 184 integration = 952 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (one `field_reassign_with_default` fix in the unit test), benches build. The §8.1.10 norm loop is now fully closed: internalization (82) → behavioral suppression (83/84/85) → enforcement audit (86) → hypocrisy-driven desistance (87).

- **Iteration 86 (AP2 §8.1.10/§19.5.D the witnessed-enforcement audit made live — the norm loop's enforcement side closes)**: the Iteration-85 scope note speculated that faction-driven conflict generation remained an un-gated violence path; the audit of that claim found **no such path exists** — Iterations 83/84/85 already gate threat issuance, threat→violence escalation, and theft; factions produce only non-violent protests and collective coups (regime change); `ConflictKind::Combat` is defined but never generated. The genuinely dead channel the docs flagged twice (Iteration 82 and Iteration 84 notes) was `InternalizedNorm.enforcement_count` (doc: "Number of times the agent has witnessed enforcement") — created at 0 by `internalize_norm` and **never incremented in production**: a ritual could internalize the norm, but a caught theft fined by the Council strengthened nothing in anyone's moral cognition. Fix: (1) moral_cognition.rs — new `record_witnessed_enforcement(&mut self, description: &str)`: increments the matching internalized norm's `enforcement_count` (saturating), a silent no-op for un-internalized norms, never touches strength or identity-linking. (2) sim.rs `enforce_theft` caught branch — after the fine is imposed (the unambiguous public-enforcement event: detection roll + Council fine + TheftDetected journal + NormViolated event), resolves the no-theft norm name **by id** via `norms::NO_THEFT_NORM_ID` and calls `record_witnessed_enforcement(name)` on **every agent holding the norm** — village-scale public punishment; the violator is deliberately included (they experience the enforcement most directly, so "witnessed" covers observation and direct experience — documented in the comment). Observational: no production consumer reads `enforcement_count` → **zero drift by construction**; the default world's farms are `AccessRight::Public`, so thefts never fire there at all. (3) Unit test `record_witnessed_enforcement_increments_count` — no-op on un-internalized, two-norm discrimination (2 vs 1 increments), strength untouched. (4) **Wiring-liveness unit test** `caught_theft_increments_witnessed_enforcement` (reviewer-mandated — the default world never catches thefts, so only this end-to-end test exercises the `enforce_theft` → `record_witnessed_enforcement` loop): a farm stocked with grain, a non-black-market thief, Council `enforcement_capacity` forced to 1.0 (detection roll < 1.0 always catches), two holders + one control → violator count 1, holder count 1, control 0. (5) Integration test `witnessed_enforcement_audit_is_armed` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: no internalized norms), post-ritual **armed-but-silent** state (9000 ticks: norms internalized, all counts 0 — the drift-pinning claim), determinism of (description, count) vectors. **766 sim + 183 integration = 949 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (one `map_or` fix in the Iter-84 test's `grain_left` closure surfaced by the shifted line numbers), benches build. **Documented scope**: the audit is observational by design — its natural behavioral consumer (`hypocrisy_sensitivity`: an agent who has witnessed enforcement many times yet still violates) is the documented next iteration; the violence-enforcement site (`check_violation` on the escalation path) lacks the caught/fined public-enforcement semantics and remains a future consumer.

- **Iteration 85 (AP2 §8.1.10 the no-violence norm now suppresses the threat *decision* — the norm loop's third behavioral consumer)**: Iterations 83 and 84 made `norm_resistance` behavioral in the violence-escalation and theft decisions, but the **threat decision itself** was still un-gated — an agent who had internalized the no-violence norm issued threats at the same rate as one who had not, so the plan's "internalized norm resists the act" coupling skipped the earliest manifestation of violence. Fix: (1) `social/interaction.rs` `choose_interaction` — new `no_violence_resistance` parameter; **both** threat thresholds scale by `(1 − resistance)`: the stress-driven negativity branch converts the threatened act into an insult (only the `negativity_prob × 0.4` Threaten bound scales, so the Insult window grows — verbal hostility persists while the violent act is suppressed), and the low-trust branch converts the threat into cautious talk (`0.3` bound scales). The RNG draw stays **unconditional** (same stream position at every resistance value — replay determinism holds), and the scale is continuous (no cliff). (2) `system_social_interactions` — the per-agent tuple gains `no_violence_resistance` (its only production caller is the sim's interaction pass; no tests call it directly, so the ripple is fully covered). (3) sim.rs — resolves the no-violence norm name **once per tick by id** via the existing `norms::NO_VIOLENCE_NORM_ID` constant (same convention as the check_violation sites and the Iter-83/84 gates; `as_str()` borrow, no per-tick allocation), then maps each agent's `moral_cognition.norm_resistance(name)` into the tuple. Zero-at-zero: no internalized norm before the first monthly ritual (tick 4320) → thresholds unchanged → the golden baseline stays byte-identical. (4) Unit test `no_violence_norm_suppresses_threat_decision` — low-trust isolated setup, 2000 draws on the **same seed-42 roll sequence** at three resistance values: baseline (0) fires threats, full internalization (1.0) yields exactly 0 (bound 0 → every roll fails), partial (0.7) yields strictly fewer (the same roll set compared against the 0.09 bound is a subset of the 0.3 bound — mathematically guaranteed strict inequality, not a statistical flake). (5) Integration test `no_violence_norm_suppresses_threats` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: every agent resistance 0), gate-input liveness (9000 ticks past ritual fires: max resistance > 0, bounded), **threat-system liveness** (threats still occur after the gate activates — the gate suppresses, it does not disable conflict), and determinism (same seed → byte-identical resistance vectors **and** threat counts via `recent_events`). **Scope boundary**: the gate covers the `choose_interaction` (interaction-system) threat decision only; faction-driven conflict generation (e.g., `faction_v2.rs`) and any direct conflict-event paths route through different code and remain un-gated (future consumers). **764 sim + 182 integration = 946 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (per-tick norm-name clone removed per review), benches build. The §8.1.10 loop is now closed at all three manifestations of violence: threat issuance (85), threat→violence escalation (83), and theft (84) — all reading the same ritual-internalized no-violence/no-theft norms.

- **Iteration 84 (AP2 §8.1.10/§19.5.D the no-theft norm now gates the theft decision — the norm loop's second behavioral consumer)**: Iteration 83 made `norm_resistance` behavioral in the violence escalation decision, but the **theft decision** was still un-gated — an agent who had internalized the no-theft norm stole exactly as much from an inaccessible farm as one who had not, so the plan's "internalized norm resists the act" coupling held for violence only. Fix: (1) norms.rs — new `NO_THEFT_NORM_ID = 0` constant (the default "No Theft" norm, index 0 of `default_norms`), mirroring `NO_VIOLENCE_NORM_ID`. (2) sim.rs `enforce_theft` — before consuming, the **amount taken** is scaled by `(1 − no_theft_resistance)`: resistance resolved **by id** via `NO_THEFT_NORM_ID` (a scenario that re-registers norms with renamed descriptions still gates correctly; a registry without the norm resolves to zero resistance — legacy behavior). At full internalization the scaled amount reaches zero and the agent **refuses the theft outright** (early return: nothing consumed, no enforcement run); partial internalization takes proportionally less (0.15 × (1 − 0.7) = 0.045 at the fresh-village strength) — continuous, no cliff. A single site gates **both** the Eat and Drink theft branches (both funnel through `enforce_theft`). The existing `check_violation_with_enforcement` call and the `SimEvent::NormViolated` push now use the constant instead of the literal 0. Zero-at-zero: no internalized norm before the first monthly ritual (tick 4320) → resistance 0 → the golden baseline stays byte-identical; the gate draws no RNG, so the enforcement detection roll's stream position is unchanged whenever a theft still occurs (a full refusal skips the detection roll, shifting the stream only in >4320 runs — same-seed determinism still holds, since the refusal decision is deterministic per seed). (3) Unit test `internalized_no_theft_norm_reduces_theft_take` — baseline full take (0.15), full internalization → refusal (grain unchanged, enforcement skipped), partial (0.7) → 0.045 take, all against a real world site.
