(4) Integration test `no_theft_norm_resistance_reduces_theft_take` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: every agent resistance 0), liveness (9000 ticks past ritual fires: max resistance > 0, bounded), determinism. **Note on behavioral proof**: default-world farms/wells are `AccessRight::Public`, so `inaccessible_farm_with_grain` never fires and thefts never occur in default runs — the behavioral outcome is therefore proven at the unit level (direct `enforce_theft` against a real stocked site with the gate on/off), not via an integration-level pre/post delta like Iteration 83's conflict probe. **763 sim + 181 integration = 944 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build. **Documented scope**: the gate scales the *amount taken* (the magnitude of the transgression), and full internalization refuses the act — the §8.1.10 pattern applied to the theft act; `enforcement_count` still records only witnessed enforcement (a caught theft runs the existing `check_violation_with_enforcement` path unchanged); other norm-gated acts (threats, direct violence) remain future consumers.

- **Iteration 83 (AP2 §8.1.10 norm_resistance made behavioral — the no-violence norm now suppresses violence escalation)**: Iteration 82 wired rituals to populate `MoralCognition.internalized_norms`, but `norm_resistance` still had **zero production callers** — an agent who had internalized the no-violence norm was indistinguishable from one who had not, so the plan's "internalized norm resists the act" coupling never ran. Fix: (1) sim.rs `should_escalate` — after the existing guards (threat_failed && aggression > threshold), the escalation **chance** is scaled by `(1 − norm_resistance)`: `chance = escalation_chance × (1 − resistance)`, where resistance comes from the agent's internalized no-violence norm, resolved **by id** via the existing `norms::NO_VIOLENCE_NORM_ID` constant (same convention as the `check_violation` sites at 8014/8055 — a scenario that re-registers norms with renamed descriptions still gates correctly; a registry without the norm resolves to zero resistance, legacy behavior). The RNG draw stays **unconditional** (same stream position at every resistance value — replay determinism holds, the Iter-74 pattern). Zero-at-zero: before the first monthly ritual (tick 4320) no agent holds any internalized norm → resistance 0 → the golden 1000-tick baseline stays byte-identical. (2) Unit test `internalized_no_violence_norm_resists_escalation` — zero-resistance read, full internalization (1.0) never escalates over 50 draws (chance 0 → every draw fails regardless of stream), partial (0.7) leaves a reduced non-cliff chance. (3) Integration test `no_violence_norm_resistance_gates_escalation` — golden-window invariance (2000 ticks: every agent resistance 0), liveness (9000 ticks past two ritual fires: max resistance > 0, bounded), determinism (two seed-42 runs byte-identical resistance vectors). **Probe-verified behavioral effect**: a 30K seed-42 run before the change shows 28,878 conflicts / 3 factions; after the change 28,736 conflicts / **4 factions** — the norm measurably suppresses escalation and shifts emergent faction dynamics, while all five faction tests (three at 30K) still pass. **Reviewer notes folded in**: the magic-string "No Violence" replaced with the `NO_VIOLENCE_NORM_ID` id-resolved lookup; the semantic site defended (scaling the escalation **decision** rather than the violence **outcome** is the §8.1.10 reading — an internalized norm prevents the act); the partial-chance assertion documented as a formula boundary-pin. Only tests past 4320 ticks see the change (30K faction tests pass, 20K/10K pass, courtship/jealousy/rumor tests at ≤4K are below the first ritual fire — unchanged): **762 sim + 180 integration = 942 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build. **Documented scope**: the gate affects only the threat-failed escalation path (a fraction of total conflict volume — the 142-conflict delta at 30K); threats, direct violence, and theft decisions remain un-gated by norms (future consumers); and `enforcement_count` still records only witnessed enforcement per its Iter-82 semantics.

- **Iteration 82 (AP2 §12.5 ritual "reinforces norms" — the dead norm-reinforcement channel wired to the dead moral-cognition consumer)**: the audit found the plan's ritual effect "reinforces norms" never ran — `Ritual::norm_reinforcement_for` (the `norm_reinforcement × internalization` scaling) had **zero production callers**, and on the consumer side `MoralCognition::internalize_norm`/`norm_resistance` had **zero production callers** with `internalized_norms` permanently empty — a ritual could bond the village, boost legitimacy, and encode cultural memory, but could not move a single agent's moral convictions. Fix: (1) moral_cognition.rs — new `reinforce_norm(&mut self, description: &str, amount: Fixed)`: strengthens an existing internalized norm (`strength += amount` clamped, `emotional_charge` re-synced to `strength × 0.5` mirroring `internalize_norm`, `identity_linked` flips permanently once strength crosses 0.7) or internalizes on first exposure via the existing `internalize_norm`; **reviewer-mandated semantic fix**: `enforcement_count` is deliberately NOT incremented — its field doc says "times the agent has witnessed enforcement" and ritual exposure is not enforcement, so the counter keeps its documented meaning for any future violation-audit consumer; +3 unit tests (strengthens existing with enforcement_count staying 0, internalizes on first exposure with zero enforcement, clamps at one + identity-linking). (2) sim.rs — inside the duodeca ritual block's participant loop (guarded by `p < agents.len()`, after the memory-encode block), the registry norms (`self.norms.norms()` — the five `default_norms`: No Theft/Help Neighbors/Respect Elders/Obey Ruler/No Violence) are each reinforced through the previously-dead `norm_reinforcement_for(norm.internalization)` → `agents[p].moral_cognition.reinforce_norm(&norm.name, reinforcement)` when > 0 — high-consensus norms (No Violence, internalization 0.7) internalize fastest. (3) Integration test `ritual_reinforces_internalized_norms` — **golden-window invariance**: at 1000 ticks (before the first monthly 4320 fire) every agent's `internalized_norms` is empty, so the calibrated baseline and all ≤2000-tick snapshots stay byte-identical; **liveness**: at 5000 ticks (past the first fire) the total internalized count > 0, every norm name matches the registry and strengths are in (0,1]; **determinism**: two seed-42 runs byte-identical; **per-agent uniqueness** (no agent holds a norm twice — pins `reinforce_norm`'s duplicate-safety against a future refactor of the find); **cross-seed differentiation**: seed 42 vs 43 internalize differently (the pro/anti cluster split on traditionalism + agreeableness > 1.0 responds to village composition). Zero drift by construction — rituals fire only at monthly 4320-tick intervals while every snapshot/golden horizon is ≤ 2000; the wiring consumes no RNG and emits no events; and `norm_resistance` has no production consumer, so even the 30K-tick faction runs only gain observational state. **Documented caveats**: (a) `internalized_norms` IS serialized in the AgentBundle snapshot (no new serialized fields → no SNAPSHOT_VERSION bump), so a *future* snapshot test at >4320 ticks would capture it and require regeneration then — harmless today; (b) `norm_resistance` remains observability-only (no production caller) — a violation-decision consumer reading it is the natural next iteration; (c) reinforcement is per-fire add (strength accumulates across monthly rituals toward 1.0) with no decay — internalized norms are deliberately sticky per the plan's identity-linked semantics. **761 sim + 179 integration = 940 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (1 new-warning fix: `map(|v| v.len())` → `map(Vec::len)` in the test), benches build.

- **Iteration 81 (AP2 §11.2 power_balance → §10.2 private-label divergence — wiring two dead channels at once)**: the audit found the plan's "power asymmetry privately shapes the emotionally honest label" coupling never ran — `RelationshipV2.power_balance` was **written daily** (the Iter-39 pure-function RelationalPower computation: directed dependence, commitment, attachment, status advantage, fear, obligation, moral debt) but had **zero production readers**, and `private_label` was **derived daily** (`update_identity_metadata`) but had **zero production consumers** — a dominated agent's honest view could never diverge from the public stage. Probe-verified the substrate is live-but-muted before designing: power_balance spans **[-0.144, 0.251]** across a default seed-42 run with **zero pairs past the ±0.5 magnitude** — a hard-threshold consumer would be the boundary-cliff class this campaign eliminates, so the wiring uses a **smooth ramp**. Fix: (1) relationship_v2.rs — `derive_private_label` feeds *being dominated* into the existing resentment channel that already drives the downgrade (§10.2: resentment dominating trust downgrades toward the negative branch): `domination = (ZERO − power_balance).max(ZERO)`, `effective_resentment = (resentment + domination × 0.5).clamp_01`, and the downgrade fires when `effective_resentment > trust && effective_resentment > 0.4` — the divergence is **continuous** in the power differential (no cliff), and high-trust bonds need proportionally more domination to flip privately, inheriting the §10.2 trust-gate semantics. +2 unit tests: `private_label_downgrades_under_domination` (balanced power → private == public Friend; pb −0.9 with **zero overt resentment** flips to Disliked; pb −0.5 stays Friend — the no-cliff proof) and `power_balance_fills_directed_leverage_and_drives_label` (heavy dependence + status deficit fills a **negative** balance for the subordinate and a positive one for the mirror dominant relationship). (2) Integration test `power_balance_private_label_coupling_is_seed_deterministic` — two seed-42 runs byte-identical in private-label divergence count, min power_balance, and max |power_balance|; the channel is live (min pb < 0: at least one relationship is dominated from its owner's perspective by tick 2000; max |pb| > 0: power genuinely differentiates). **Reviewer notes folded in**: the 0.5 ramp coefficient and the 0.4 floor documented inline as deliberate simplifications (the ramp is the §11.2 magnitude mapped half-strength into the existing resentment channel; the 0.4 floor matches the pre-existing §10.2 downgrade threshold); the **ordering guarantee** verified (`update_power_balance` runs earlier in the same daily block than `update_identity_metadata`, so private labels always derive from fresh power); the event-site calls to `update_identity_metadata` without a same-tick power recompute are safe because `power_balance` is **stored state** that persists between daily computations — the label reflects the most recent daily power read. **Documented scope**: in default riverford runs the channel stays quiet (strong domination is rare — same documented pattern as Iter-80's D5 gate), so the integration test pins the live signal (non-zero power differentials, seed-deterministic divergence) rather than asserting widespread label flips; the divergence class is unit-proven under strong domination. Zero drift by construction — `power_balance` and `private_label` appear in no golden hash/metric or snapshot projection, and the ramp consumes no RNG and emits no events: **758 sim + 178 integration = 936 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build.

- **Iteration 80 (AP2 §8.1.12 executive function wired to §8.1.16 prospection — the last dead cognitive consumer)**: the audit found `CognitiveRuntime` **live-but-unconsumed**: updated every tick (stress/fatigue/pain/trauma degrade `effective_capacity`, `effective_inhibition`, `effective_flexibility`; `current_impulsivity` runs a decay-to-base + stress-push equilibrium) but with **zero production readers** — all four gate methods (`can_plan_long_term`, `can_inhibit`, `can_switch_strategy`, `effective_planning_depth`) were dead code, so the plan's "high executive function enables long-term planning" coupling (§8.1.12) never ran, and the §22.1 "stress reduces planning horizon" story was observability-only. Probe-verified the substrate is live and differentiating before designing: effective_capacity spans **0.07–0.49** across 12 agents (seed 42), only **2–4/12 agents per seed can plan long-term** (the rest are EF-degraded by the stress/fatigue/pain/trauma load), planning_confidence spans 0.30–0.70. Fix: (1) imagination.rs — `ScenarioInputs` gained `can_plan_long_term: bool`; the **D5 ambition branch** ("If I distinguish myself, I may gain respect") now requires `ambition > 0.5 && can_plan_long_term` — an ambitious agent whose EF is degraded (stressed/sleep-deprived/traumatized) cannot simulate the status-seeking future and falls through to the D6 hopeful default, the plan's categorical "enables long-term planning" made behavioral; +2 unit tests (intact EF fires D5 / degraded falls to D6; non-ambitious never fires D5 even with planning). (2) sim.rs — the daily prospection block passes `cognitive_runtime.can_plan_long_term()` into the inputs, and after `prospection.update()` blends `effective_planning_depth()` into `planning_confidence`: `pc = (pc + ef_depth) × 0.5` — the plan's "confidence in ability to plan and execute" now tracks the agent's actual degraded capacity rather than only fear/ambition. **Probe-verified live**: planning_confidence 0.186–0.408 tracks EF depth 0.015–0.386 per-agent (seed 42), determinism byte-identical. (3) Integration tests: `executive_function_planning_confidence_tracks_ef_depth` (recomputes the emotion formula from final fear/ambition and pins the exact blend within 0.001, plus asserts EF differentiation — some agents can_plan_long_term, some cannot) and `executive_function_coupling_is_seed_deterministic`. **Reviewer notes folded in**: unused `Fixed` imports in the new tests removed (the compiler-warning source); the 0.5/0.5 blend documented as a deliberate simplification (emotion term = motivational confidence, EF depth = cognitive capacity, equal weight revisable via calibration; both operands provably ∈ [0,1] so no re-clamp needed); the **budget-gate staleness window** documented (`cognitive_runtime.update` runs under `can_appraise()`, the gate read sits in `can_prospect()` — mismatched exhaustion reads last-tick EF; harmless at 12 focal agents whose budgets never exhaust); the D5 **hard-gate threshold** acknowledged as deliberate (matches `can_plan_long_term`'s categorical contract; the soft-scale alternative is less legible). **Documented scope**: the D5 gate is unit-proven but never fires in default riverford runs (D1 scarcity dominates — the same documented pattern as Iter-71's D2–D6); the live-observable channel is the `planning_confidence` blend, which now differentiates per-agent in every run. Zero drift by construction — `prospection` and `cognitive_runtime` appear in no golden hash/metric or snapshot projection, and the coupling consumes no RNG and emits no events: **756 sim + 177 integration = 933 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (one pre-existing unused-variable warning in an untouched Iter-77 test region left as-is per convention), benches build.

- **Iteration 79 (AP2 §10.4 AttractionModel complete — kinship_penalty, the last unwired attraction field, wired live)**: the audit confirmed `AttractionModel.kinship_penalty` had **zero production writers** — a declared plan field pinned at 0 (weight 0.3, negative), the final gap after familiarity (Iter-75), reciprocity (Iter-76), status_attraction (Iter-77), and social_cost/moral_disgust (Iter-78). The courtship eligibility gate hard-blocks direct kinship coefficient ≥ 0.25 at the pair level, but the plan's §10.6 kinship model is *transitive* (BFS half-coefficient decay: parent/sibling 0.5, grandparent 0.25, first cousin 0.125) — so cousin ties pass the direct-edge hard gate entirely, leaving a genuine soft-taboo gap. Probe-verified the signal is live-but-situational before designing: seeds 42/43/44 form **zero kin ties** in 2000 ticks (founding villages → penalty 0), seed 51 forms families (max coefficient **0.50**). Fix: (1) attraction.rs — new `update_kinship_penalty(&mut self, max_relatedness: Fixed)` (clamps [0,1], mirrors the `update_social_cost` pattern) + 4 unit tests (mirror, clamp at 1.5/−0.5, and `cousin_relatedness_depresses_attraction_below_gate_math` — a first-cousin tie 0.125 × weight 0.3 = 0.0375 drops a 0.42 profile to 0.3825, under the 0.4 D4 gate: the soft complement to the direct-edge hard gate). (2) sim.rs — in the status pass, computes the max `transitive_coefficient(i, j)` over all other adults (age ≥ 16, `j != i` guard makes the `debug_assert_ne!` safe) and calls `update_kinship_penalty`; **daily cadence** (`phases.is_daily`) per reviewer — kinship edges only change on birth/marriage, so the O(N²) BFS runs once a day (the patronage block below uses the same `is_duodeca` pattern for its O(N²) work). (3) Integration tests: `kinship_penalty_rises_when_families_form` (founding seeds 42/43/44 → **exactly 0**; seed 51 → some agent > 0) and `kinship_penalty_is_seed_deterministic` (seed 51 byte-identical). **Probe-verified**: max_penalty mirrors relatedness (seed 51: 0.500) and the D4 gate stays reachable in every integration seed (seed 43: 0.410, seed 44: 0.465 — founding villages are unaffected by construction). **Reviewer notes folded in**: daily cadence for the O(N²) BFS; the pool-inbreeding semantics documented — the 0.5 tier (parent/sibling, hard-gated out of courtship anyway) raises the cost of ALL matches in a kin-dense village while the actionable taboo tier (cousins 0.125–0.24, which pass the hard gate) is exactly what the soft channel suppresses. Zero drift (attraction unhashed, no RNG, no events): **754 sim + 175 integration = 929 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build. **Documented**: the §10.4 AttractionModel is now **fully wired** — every plan field has a live producer; total_attraction responds to familiarity, reciprocity, status (positive), social_cost, moral_disgust, kinship_penalty (negative); the D4 courtship gate is reachable in mixed/founding villages and suppressed where the negative channels legitimately apply (uniform violence, kin-dense gene pools).

- **Iteration 78 (AP2 §19.5.D norm enforcement revived + §10.4 the two negative attraction channels — social_cost and moral_disgust — wired live)**: the audit's opening claim — that norm enforcement was dead (`record_offense` had zero callers, crime records never written) — was **wrong**, and the probe-driven correction defined the iteration. `record_offense` is called internally by `check_violation`, which IS called from three pre-existing production sites (the theft-fine path, the violence-conflict block, and the norm-evaluation pass). The real defect was **notoriety saturation**: a conflict-prone village produces 57–316 offenses per agent over 2000 ticks, driving `notoriety` to **1.0 for everyone** and erasing all differentiation. Wiring `social_cost ← notoriety` (weight 0.1) then crashed max `total_attraction` from 0.42 to **0.312** — killing the Iter-77 D4 courtship gate (0.4) in seed 42 and exposing the saturation. Fix: (1) norms.rs — `CrimeRecord` gained `episode_count`/`last_episode_tick`; `record_offense` now **episode-gates notoriety** (one increment per `ENFORCEMENT_EPISODE_TICKS`=400 window) while `offense_count` still grows every call — so every **punishment trajectory stays byte-identical** to baseline (fines→wealth_rank and trust-erosion→relationship metrics are snapshot-hashed; gating them would drift the golden baselines, which the first call-site-gating attempt demonstrated: 6 snapshot tests + golden replay failed until the speech-act site was removed). (2) sim.rs — the per-tick status pass wires `attraction.update_social_cost(notoriety)` and `attraction.update_moral_disgust(disgust)` (both clamped, deterministic, no RNG). (3) attraction.rs — `update_social_cost`/`update_moral_disgust` + 3 unit tests. **Probe-verified liveness**: notoriety now differentiates (seed 43: range 0.39–0.92, 6/12 agents notorious) and the D4 gate survives in 9/11 seeds (max_total 0.403–0.562); seed 42 is a uniformly-violent village (all agents 0.86–0.92) where social_cost **legitimately suppresses** courtship — nobody wants to court a notorious criminal's family; `moral_disgust` stays 0 in peaceful runs (the §8.1.4 appraisal computes disgust = purity_violation × goal_relevance, the same situational class as the Iter-65 jealousy channel). (3) Integration tests: `social_cost_mirrors_notoriety_and_d4_survives_mixed_village` (exact mirror + differentiation + D4 reachable in seed 43), `moral_disgust_stays_zero_in_peaceful_default_run`, `social_cost_is_seed_deterministic`. **The Iter-77 D4 test's seed pin moved 42 → 43** (documented in-comment): seed 42's uniform violence now legitimately suppresses the gate, and the test asserts D4 reachability where the channel differentiates. **Reviewer notes folded in**: `SNAPSHOT_VERSION` bumped 10 → 11 (CrimeRecord's wire format changed — postcard has no default-fill), line-number comment references replaced with descriptive ones, and `last_offense_tick` documented as vestigial. Zero drift: the episode gate lives entirely in norms-registry state that no golden metric/hash or snapshot projection captures, and punishments are byte-identical: **751 sim + 173 integration = 924 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build. **Documented**: the §10.4 attraction profile now responds to all five wired factors — familiarity, reciprocity, status (positive) and social_cost, moral_disgust (negative); the D4 gate is reachable in mixed villages and deliberately suppressed in uniformly-violent ones; `kinship_penalty` remains the one unwired channel (pair-specific, live at the courtship formation gate via `coefficient_between`).

- **Iteration 77 (AP2 §10.4 status_attraction wired — the D4 courtship gate is now fully reachable in default runs)**: the audit found `AttractionModel.status_attraction` dead in production: **zero writers** — a declared plan field pinned at 0 forever, and the last of the three attraction factors (familiarity Iter-75, reciprocity Iter-76) that could push `total_attraction()` over the §8.1.16 D4 courtship gate (0.4). Probe-verified the status substrate is live before designing: `status_v2` refreshes daily-ish in the per-tick status pass (authority from `role_status`, `institutional_rank` from the highest role authority, `wealth_rank` from relative coin, `moral_reputation` from pride/gratitude, `network_centrality` from relationship quality, decay on shame/honor), and every agent carries a non-zero, differentiating `effective_status()` (probe: **0.18–0.40 across all 12 agents** — role holders authority 0.6, wealth_rank 0–1.0 discriminating). Fix: (1) attraction.rs — new `update_status_attraction(&mut self, effective_status: Fixed)` (clamps [0,1], mirrors the `update_familiarity` pattern; the re-clamp is deliberately defensive for a public API even though `effective_status()` already clamps); +3 unit tests (status 0.4 raises `total_attraction` by ~0.04 at its 0.1 weight; clamps at 1.5/−0.5; `high_status_plus_familiarity_crosses_d4_gate` — base 0.30 + familiarity at the 0.8 cap (0.08) + status 0.4 (0.04) = 0.42 > 0.4; the **first draft used 300 Talk interactions (familiarity only 0.09 → total 0.349) and failed — a genuine calibration catch**, corrected to 1400 Help so familiarity saturates exactly at the 0.8 cap, modeling the probe-verified live condition). (2) sim.rs — in the per-tick status pass, right after the `network_centrality` refresh: `attraction.update_status_attraction(status_v2.effective_status())` (computed into a local first — borrow-safe; deterministic, no RNG). **Reviewer notes folded in**: the exact mirror (status_attraction == effective_status, pinned by the integration test) is documented as *intentional* — a future relative/scaled re-derivation is a conscious re-design; and status_attraction tracks whatever `effective_status()` captures (the Iter-55 `institutional_rank` field is not yet weighted into the composite — role holders are still captured via `authority = role_status`, and a future weighting flows through automatically). (3) Integration tests: `status_attraction_live_and_d4_reachable` (every agent's `attraction.status_attraction` equals `status_v2.effective_status()` exactly — the idempotent-clamp mirror holds; max `total_attraction` = **0.42 > 0.4, the D4 gate crossed in a default run without reciprocity**) and `status_attraction_is_seed_deterministic` (two seed-42 runs byte-identical in status_attraction and total_attraction). Zero drift by construction — attraction fields appear in no golden metric/hash or snapshot projection, and the D4 scenario gate (prospection hope/dread) has no behavioral consumer, so the newly-reachable courtship scenarios change only observational state: **746 sim + 170 integration = 916 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions, benches build. **Documented**: the attraction profile (total_attraction) now responds to all three plan factors — familiarity (contact), reciprocity (courtship dynamics), and status (social standing); the D4 courtship mental scenario is reachable in peaceful default runs for role-holding/wealthy agents, exactly as the plan's "If I court them, my kin gain standing" intends; the remaining attraction factors (kinship_penalty, moral_disgust, social_cost) and the plan's taboo_penalty (mapped to the code's moral_disgust/social_cost) still sit at 0 — they are penalty/aversion channels, not D4 boosters, and remain future wiring.

- **Iteration 76 (AP2 §10.4 Courtship stage ladder made live — reviving the dead romantic pipeline)**: the audit found the §10.4 courtship ladder dead in production — `Courtship::record_positive`/`record_negative`/`advance_probability`/`regression_probability` had **zero production callers** (self-tests only), so every courtship formed at `Awareness` and rotted there (probe: seed-42 courtships formed at ticks 48/672, **zero pair interactions in 10k ticks** — courting pairs sit beyond each other's perception radius — stages pinned at Awareness, `AttractionModel.reciprocity` = 0 everywhere, `total_attraction` frozen at 0.38), and the per-agent `AttractionModel.reciprocity` (the Iteration-75-documented factor that could push `total_attraction` over the 0.4 D4 gate) had zero writers. Fix: (1) courtship.rs — `try_advance` made public (the plan's trust-gated advancement: trust ≥ next stage's base_trust, attraction ≥ 0.6× that, and — once the pair has an interaction record — positives > negatives; **balance relaxation**: a freshly formed 0/0-record courtship may advance on trust alone, since the strict `pos > neg` gate made the daily path unreachable — the surgical 0/0-only relaxation leaves the (1,0)/(0,1) behavior unchanged); **ladder capped at Betrothal** (`next == Married → return` — courtships must never display the post-marriage stages; probe-verified: the pre-cap ladder advanced a courtship to `Strain`, a label artifact the cap eliminates); `advance_probability`/`regression_probability` documented as **superseded** (a probabilistic daily roll would draw from the Social RNG stream, shift every downstream draw, and force golden recalibration — the deterministic gate keeps replay byte-identical); +4 unit tests (ladder climbs one rung per positive as trust grows 0.3→0.7 reaching Betrothal — the trust gate is the pacing mechanism, Exclusivity needs 0.55/Betrothal 0.65 so flat trust 0.5 stalls at SocialValidation, caught by the test; trust thresholds gate advancement; direct try_advance trust-gated/monotonic — this test caught the balance-gate deadlock; hostile interactions regress and kill a courtship at Awareness). (2) sim.rs — the speech-act block feeds each courting-pair interaction into `record_positive(tick, trust, total_attraction)`/`record_negative(tick)` (trust = the speaker's relationship trust, computed once and reused as the speech-act credibility — reviewer-mandated dedup); the daily courtship pass refreshes each courtship's `mutual_attraction` to the pair's **current average trust** and calls `try_advance(avg_trust, avg_trust)` — trust-gated ladder advancement without same-tick interaction (the trust gate is the real gate; passing trust as the attraction-floor input is a documented simplification), then **mirrors the courtship's reciprocity into the pursuer's `AttractionModel.reciprocity`** — moved to the daily pass per review so the mirror decays in lockstep with the courtship's own 0.98 daily decay instead of snapping stale, and **zeroed on courtship conclusion** (dead-courtship retain and marriage-removal retain both reset the pursuer's mirror, closing the reviewer-flagged stale-mirror wart); marriage formation removes any active courtship between the newlyweds so the record concludes at Betrothal. (3) Integration tests: `courtship_ladder_advances_in_live_run` (seed 42: both courtships climb past Awareness to SocialValidation/TestingReciprocity — the trust path is live — and max ≤ Betrothal, the cap holds) and `courtship_interactions_drive_reciprocity_and_d4_gate` (seed 44: courting pairs accumulate 1153/1904 positive interactions, reciprocity 0.97/1.0, max `total_attraction` = **0.530 — the 0.4 D4 gate finally crossed**, the Iteration-75-documented future wiring closed); plus the marriage-removal invariant appended to `courtship_to_marriage_chain` (no active courtship may contain a married pair). Probe-driven calibration: the interaction path was verified across seeds 42–48 (44/45/47/48 exercise it; 42/46 prove the trust-only path). Zero drift by construction — courtship state (stages, reciprocity, mutual_attraction) appears in no golden metric/hash or snapshot projection, the wiring draws no RNG and emits no events, and the marriage-removal retain only touches `active_courtships`: **743 sim + 168 integration = 911 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (pre-existing tick_pair_bonds warnings in untouched regions unchanged), benches build. **Documented scope**: the ladder climbs to the pair's trust ceiling (stalling at e.g. TestingReciprocity for seed-42's 0.4-trust pair) — sustained ladder growth requires pair trust to grow, which requires interaction (the plan's "seek proximity" dynamic remains future work); `status_attraction` remains 0 (the one other D4 factor); the §10.4 probabilistic daily roll remains superseded-by-design for golden stability.

- **Iteration 75 (AP2 §10.4 Attraction familiarity made live — the plan's "familiarity grows with repeated interaction" wired)**: the audit found §10.4's `AttractionModel::update_familiarity` dead in production: **zero callers** — the familiarity term was pinned at 0 forever, so `total_attraction()` (which gates the §8.1.16 D4 courtship scenario at 0.4) never responded to repeated contact, and the plan's familiarity mechanic never ran. Fix: (1) attraction.rs — new `familiarity_quality_for(kind: InteractionKind)` pure table (Help 0.06, Comfort 0.05, Teach 0.05, Trade 0.04, Talk 0.03, Gossip 0.03, Threaten 0.005, Insult 0.005 — deep prosocial acts build familiarity most, hostile acts almost none); the qualities sit **an order of magnitude below the initial 0.3–0.6 draft because the probe showed the raw scale saturated EVERY agent at the 0.8 cap by tick 1000 in both seeds (total 9.6 = 12 × 0.8), erasing all differentiation** — the same dead-signal class this wiring exists to fix; the 0.005–0.06 scale keeps familiarity a slow, discriminating, seed-differentiating signal (probe: seed 42 vs 43 diverge at 1000 ticks, max 0.515, no saturation); +5 unit tests (accumulation from repeated Help, 0.8 cap, hostile-vs-prosocial ordering, familiarity feeds total_attraction) alongside the 2 pre-existing. (2) sim.rs — in the daily social block's speech-act pass (which already iterates every `InteractionOccurred` event deterministically — no RNG), **both participants** now receive `update_familiarity(quality-for-kind)` before speech-act recording; the update is a pure function of the interaction kind applied in the deterministic event window. (3) Integration tests: `familiarity_grows_with_interaction_across_run` (4000 ticks: all 12/12 agents engaged, max respects the 0.8 cap) and `familiarity_growth_is_seed_deterministic` (seed 42 == seed 42 byte-identical at 3000; seed 43 diverges at 1000 — the short horizon is because by 3000 the majority (8/12) have saturated identically at the cap, collapsing the cross-seed signal). **Reviewer-mandated doc correction**: with default other factors, even familiarity at the 0.8 cap only reaches `total_attraction ≈ 0.38` — still below the 0.4 D4 gate — so the wiring makes the signal *live and responsive* while crossing the gate requires additional attraction factors (reciprocity/status) to be non-zero; the doc comments now state this invariant accurately. Zero drift by construction — `attraction.familiarity` appears in no golden metric/hash (golden hashes hunger/thirst/fatigue/valence + grain/water + event count), no snapshot projection (AgentSnapshot fields), and feeds only the observational D4 scenario gate (no behavioral consumer per Iteration 71): **739 sim + 166 integration = 905 workspace tests green**, zero drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (2 test-region warning fixes: empty-doc-lines after probe removal, unused `Fixed` import), benches build. **Documented caveats**: (a) both participants receive identical quality while the relationship model applies `social_reciprocal_factor` to the reverse direction — a deliberate symmetry choice (both experienced the same interaction), documented so a future reader doesn't "fix" it into an unintended asymmetry; (b) `reciprocity` and `status_attraction` remain 0 at default — the two other attraction factors that could push `total_attraction` over the 0.4 D4 gate are future wiring work.

- **Iteration 74 (AP2 §29.2 Faction-v2 threat surface wired to protest suppression — the last fully dead §29.2 surface)**: the audit found the faction-v2 combat layer dead in production: `FactionV2::fighting_strength()`, `threat_level()`, `mobilization_capacity`, and `legitimacy_of_violence` were computed and ticked (since the Iter-72 faction attachment wiring), but had **zero production consumers** — an armed, mobilized faction had no more effect on conflict outcomes than a crowd of peasants. The plan's §29.2 mandate ("mobilization capacity, morale, supplies, leadership, cohesion, casualties, legitimacy of violence") was observability-only. Fix: (1) factions.rs — `council_response` (the v1 protest-suppression decision) gained a `protest_strength` parameter: the effective protest size ratio scales **continuously** with fighting strength (`size_ratio × (1 + strength.clamp_01())`), so armed factions are harder to suppress at *every* strength level — deliberately no threshold, avoiding the dead-branch/boundary-cliff class this iteration exists to eliminate (a strength-0 faction is byte-identical to legacy, pinned by `zero_protest_strength_is_legacy_identical`); suppression remains enforcement-vs-effective-size, and the legitimacy effect is unchanged structurally (gain on suppression, loss on failure). 4 new unit tests: armed-resistance flip at the borderline, monotonicity (a stronger faction is **never** suppressed while a weaker one is free — the violation condition was briefly inverted during development and corrected), legacy identity at strength 0, and the `higher_strength_is_never_easier_to_suppress` sweep. (2) sim.rs — the protest block looks up the protesting v1 faction's "Leader" role holder, finds the 1:1-registered active `FactionV2` record by leader index, and passes `fighting_strength()` into `council_response`; the lookup is a panic-free `map(...).and_then(...).map_or(Fixed::ZERO, ...)` chain — a missing leader or missing v2 record safely falls back to the unarmed legacy path. (3) Integration test `faction_v2_fighting_strength_links_to_protests` — the 30K-tick seed-42 grievance horizon (factions form 20-30K): asserts v1/v2 **1:1 registration**, the leader linkage resolves for every active v2 record, live fighting strength/threat in [0,1] and non-zero for formed factions, and the armed-aware outcome through the exact public API the consumer calls. Zero drift by construction — factions do not form in the golden 2000-tick run, so the protest block (and its new lookup) is never exercised there; the lookup consumes no RNG and the v2 registry is not snapshot-serialized: **735 sim + 164 integration = 899 workspace tests green**, zero drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (3 new-warning fixes: redundant closure in the leader lookup, `panic!`-in-if → `assert!` in a unit test, `flat_map` → `filter_map` in the integration test; 3 pre-existing warnings in untouched regions left as-is), benches build. **Documented caveats**: (a) the v2 leader is fixed at registration while the v1 "Leader" role could theoretically change — after a leadership change the lookup falls back to unarmed (safe, legacy-identical), and in practice v1 faction leadership is not reassigned in the current dynamics; (b) `threat_level()`/`mobilization_capacity`/`legitimacy_of_violence` remain observability-only — the suppression consumer reads only `fighting_strength()`; a broader conflict-resolution consumer (revolt-vs-crackdown force comparison) is future work.

- **Iteration 73 (AP2 §13.3 Rumor transmission made live + §12.3 "anxious groups escalate rumors" wired)**: the audit found §13.3's core mechanic dead in production: `record_transmission`/`transmission_chance` had **zero production callers** — rumors were created from emotionally charged gossip and decayed daily, but never spread, so `prevalence` could only decay (never grow), stored `evidence_quality` never degraded per hop (violating the plan's `evidence_quality × fidelity^hops`), and the §18.4 statistical test `gossip_accuracy_declines_with_hops` was vacuous (long chains never formed, so its core assertion never had both buckets populated). Fix: (1) rumor_v2.rs — `transmission_chance` now reads stored evidence directly (the old on-the-fly `hops_fidelity` was removed because `record_transmission` now degrades the **stored** `evidence_quality` by 0.85 per hop — one degradation per hop, single source of truth for the plan's fidelity law); new `RumorRegistry::transmission_pass(trust_matrix, susceptibility, skepticism, escalation, population, tick)` — for each active rumor, scans listeners not already in the source chain, computes `transmission_chance × source-escalation`, transmits to the **argmax listener (tie → lowest index, fully deterministic, consumes no RNG — the golden baseline stays byte-identical)** when the chance clears the 0.02 spread floor, then `record_transmission` (chain grows, evidence degrades, charge +0.02) + `believer_count += 1` + `prevalence = believers/population × (1 + emotional_charge)` (the plan's `host_count / population × emotional_contagion`); returns the hop count; **reviewer-mandated fix**: creation now uses a new `record_source(agent, tick)` that records the originator in the chain *without* degrading evidence — previously the creation-site `record_transmission` call silently added a phantom hop penalty, leaving stored evidence one degradation ahead of the counted `source_chain.len() - 1`; +5 unit tests (stored-evidence degradation, spread-to-most-receptive, determinism + no-chain-repeat, anxious 1.5× escalation fires where secure 1.0× does not, source-recording). (2) sim.rs — in the daily block of `tick_kinship_household_daily`: builds the n×n trust matrix from `relationship_v2s`, per-agent susceptibility from openness (0.4 + 0.6×openness), skepticism from `epistemic.trust_network.skepticism`, and a §12.3 escalation vector = max over active factions and peer groups of the group's attachment-style scale (Secure 1.0, **Anxious 1.5 — the plan's "escalate rumors"**, Avoidant 0.5 — "suppress emotion", Disorganized 1.25 — documented interpretation of "volatile leadership" since §12.3 lists escalation under Anxious only), then calls `transmission_pass`; the creation site switched to `record_source`. (3) Integration tests: `rumors_transmit_through_population` (probe-verified **fully live**: 1223 rumors, 10,765 hops, max chain 12 = full village saturation, 97% of rumors gained hops across 6 seeds × 4000 ticks), `rumor_evidence_degrades_with_transmission_hops` (single-hop avg evidence > multi-hop avg per seed), `rumor_transmission_is_seed_deterministic` (same seed → byte-identical chain/evidence/prevalence/believer/charge). Zero drift by construction: the rumor registry is **not snapshot-serialized**, no production consumer reads rumor state, and the pass consumes no RNG — **732 sim + 163 integration = 895 workspace tests green**, zero drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (3 new-warning fixes: `is_none_or`, block-return, index-loop→enumerate), benches build. **Documented**: the n×n trust matrix is rebuilt daily (O(n²) — trivial at 12 agents, a future large-population pass should make it lazy/sparse per the §17.4 story); the `gossip_accuracy_declines_with_hops` §18.4 test is now genuinely exercised (long chains form and stored evidence degrades).

- **Iteration 72 (AP2 §12.3 Attachment styles scale upward to factions — the largest group type)**: the §12.3 "attachment styles scale upward" mechanic was live for peer groups only (formation-pass modal derivation + style-dependent cohesion decay, Iteration-60-era) — **factions, the largest and most consequential group type, had no attachment dimension**: `FactionV2`'s rich dynamics surface (supplies, morale, cohesion, fragmentation, trust-in-leadership) ran its daily decay (the registry's `daily_update()` was already called in the kinship daily pass — an audit grep initially missed it due to a path error), but the decay was style-blind. Fix: (1) faction_v2.rs — `FactionV2` gained `attachment_style: GroupAttachmentStyle` (`#[serde(default)]` — pre-§12.3 serialized factions restore Secure, mirroring the PeerGroup precedent; the v2 registry is not snapshot-serialized anyway), a `derive_attachment_style(&[AttachmentStyle])` helper (modal aggregation via the existing `derive_group_attachment_style` — one deterministic scaling rule for both group types), and a **`StyleDynamics` per-style tuning table** (reviewer-mandated consolidation of four match arms into one four-field constant table for one-point recalibration): scarcity morale penalty (anxious 1.5× — plan "panic under uncertainty", avoidant 0.5× — plan "suppress emotion", disorganized 1.25×, secure 1.0× baseline), low-cohesion stress fragmentation growth (secure 0.0005 — "tolerate dissent" < anxious 0.00075 — "cling" < disorganized 0.0015 — "prone to purges" < avoidant 0.002 — "fragment under stress"), cohesion retention (secure 0.9995 > anxious 0.999 > avoidant 0.9985 > disorganized 0.998 — "volatile leadership"), trust-in-leadership decay under an incompetent leader (anxious 0.001 — "cling to leaders" < secure 0.002 < disorganized 0.003 < avoidant 0.004 — "distrust dependence"); plus 6 unit tests (modal derivation, old-snapshot serde default, secure-holds-cohesion ordering, anxious-cling vs avoidant-distrust trust decay, avoidant stress fragmentation, anxious scarcity panic — the last test originally never reached the scarcity floor because supplies start at 0.7 and only decay 0.001/day; fixed by forcing supplies to 0.2 so the penalty fires). (2) sim.rs — at `FactionV2` registration in `tick_faction_dynamics`, the modal member style is derived from live member attachment styles before `register()` (bounds-safe: `member_indices` was already filtered to `idx < agents.len()`). (3) Integration test `faction_attachment_styles_scale_upward_and_dynamics_run` — the same 30K-tick seed-42 grievance horizon as `factions_emerge_from_grievance` (factions form between 20-30K ticks): active FactionV2s exist, every faction's stored style equals the modal style of its live members (recomputed in the test), cohesion ≤ 0.9 formation bound, and style-aware dynamics are observable (non-Secure faction exists or supplies decayed below the 0.7 formation value). Zero drift by construction — factions do not form in the golden 2000-tick run (the v2 registry stays empty there) and the registry is not snapshot-serialized; the only production consumer (`agent_faction` at sim.rs 2735) sees an empty registry in golden runs: **728 sim + 160 integration = 888 workspace tests green**, zero drift, clippy clean in touched regions, benches build. **Documented**: the plan's "anxious groups escalate rumors" behavior is a rumor-system connection deliberately out of scope for this wiring iteration; faction attachment styles are derived at registration only (member styles are static after `initialize`) — a re-derivation pass would matter only if the sim adds attachment-style plasticity.

- **Iteration 71 (AP2 §8.1.16 Mental-scenario generation — the plan's "agents simulate possible futures" made live)**: the audit found §8.1.16's core mechanic dead in production: `ProspectionState` and the emotion-biased `update()` (fear→catastrophic bias, ambition→optimism, trauma→catastrophe, depression→hope drain) were already wired in the daily per-agent block (Iteration 40-era), but **`generate_scenario`/`evaluate_scenarios` had zero production callers** — the scenario set stayed empty, so agents never actually simulated futures and hope/dread were never scenario-grounded. Fix: (1) imagination.rs — `ScenarioInputs` (10-field Copy struct: hunger/thirst/safety deficits, fear/anger, ambition, world food/water scarcity, partner status, total attraction) + `generate_daily_scenario` with a strict-priority 6-domain roster derived deterministically from live state (no RNG): D1 scarcity on personal deprivation (hunger>0.6‖thirst>0.6) or world scarcity>0.5 → "If the harvest fails, my household goes hungry."; D2 threat on safety>0.5‖fear>0.4 → "If I travel alone, I may come to harm."; D3 injustice on anger>0.4 → "If I complain, the council may punish me."; D4 courtship on !partner∧attraction>0.4 → "If I court them, my kin gain standing."; D5 ambition>0.5 → "If I distinguish myself, I may gain respect."; D6 hopeful default → "If the rains come, the village thrives." — each scenario's probability/valence/need-relief/social-consequence/risk/vividness derived from state, bounded by the module's capacity (oldest evicted); plus 8 unit tests (all six domains under their exact triggers, courtship gating on partner/attraction, capacity bound, hope/dread regrounding). (2) sim.rs — hoisted the expected-food/water + scarcity computation out of the motivation `set_context` block into the agent-loop scope (pure scope change, byte-identical — verified no later shadowing), and wired daily generation + `evaluate_scenarios()` into the existing budget-gated prospection block. **Reviewer-mandated ordering fix**: generation runs first (uses the standing emotion biases from the previous tick), then evaluation grounds hope/dread in the scenario set, and only then does `update()` apply today's emotions — so the plan's "depression reduces hope" drain operates on the scenario-grounded hope instead of being clobbered by the overwrite (the first draft ran update→generate→evaluate, which discarded the drain). (3) Integration test `mental_scenarios_generate_across_run` — scenarios populate across a real 2000-tick run, capacity-bounded, the flagship harvest-dread scenario fires (probe-verified: riverford is genuinely scarce — world grain ~11.6 vs the ~120 reference → food_scarcity ≈ 0.9, and fear saturates near 1.0 — so the strict priority correctly fixates the whole frightened, hungry village on the harvest dread; hope→0, dread 0.38 above the 0.2 baseline, proving evaluate is live), seed-deterministic. Observational state — prospection is absent from the AgentBundle snapshot/metrics projection and has no behavioral consumer, so calibrated runs stay byte-identical: **722 sim + 159 integration = 881 workspace tests green**, zero drift, clippy clean in touched regions (also fixed a test bug where `.dedup()` mutated the determinism-comparison operand), benches build. **Documented**: the plan's four canonical scenarios are all wired, but in the default scarce world only the harvest-dread domain fires through the live wiring (D2–D6 are proven by unit tests under their trigger conditions) — a per-agent differentiation tiebreak is future consumer work; hope/dread have two writers (scenario-grounded daily, bias-drained in between) — semantically coherent now, and no consumer reads them yet.

- **Iteration 70 (AP2 §10.3 Authority branch — making the six reserved authority stages live)**: the §10.3 authority branch was the last reserved relationship taxonomy: all six labels (`PatronClient`, `LordVassal`, `MasterApprentice`, `PriestLayperson`, `ElderJunior`, `GuardCitizen`) and their `RoleExpectation` variants existed in enums, but `RelationshipStage` had **zero authority variants, no label/role arms, and no derivation** — the pair-bond equivalent of the Iter-58 dead-stage class. Fix: (1) relationship_v2.rs — the six stage variants with base_trust (0.6/0.6/0.55/0.55/0.5/0.5), `derive_public_label` arms, and `derive_role_expectation` arms; (2) relationship_stages.rs — `min_interactions` (0 — assigned, not advanced), trust/affection/obligation threshold tables, `is_authority_stage()`, and a unit test pinning the assigned-not-advanced contract; (3) sim.rs daily authority-assignment pass (`tick_kinship_household_daily`) — labels **structural** bonds from the live producers (patronage registry → PatronClient, apprenticeship `learning_events` → MasterApprentice, cult charismatic leader ↔ members → PriestLayperson, household head ↔ members → ElderJunior), written symmetrically (both directions carry the authority label), with a **baseline gate** (only pairs still at Unnoticed/Noticed receive the label). The pass runs **after** the transition pass so pairs that can socially progress do so first — the ordering was the second-debugged regression (the first draft ran before the transition pass and *froze* patronage pairs at the terminal authority stage, erasing proximity-driven friendship signals and breaking the `friendships_correlate_with_proximity_across_seeds` invariant at 0.503 vs 0.513; the reorder restored it). The transition pass now skips authority stages outright (`is_authority_stage` guard beside `is_kin_stage`). (4) **Orphan reset** (reviewer-mandated, mirroring the kin-stage orphan reset): any authority label whose producer has disappeared without a death-path rv2 rebuild (registry cleanup, cult disbanding — death itself fully rebuilds v2s in `rebuild_relationship_v2s_after_death`) returns to Unnoticed, since the transition pass would otherwise never reset the terminal label. (5) friendships-test proxy corrected: authority stages are **excluded** from the social-progression count (`>= Acquaintance && !is_authority_stage`) — the structural overlay rescues exactly the far pairs that interact least, counteracting the proximity signal; a *discriminant-ordering audit* found only one other `stage as u32` comparison (a lower-bound `> 0` assertion in `courtship_to_marriage_chain` — authority stages can only inflate it, never break it). **Intentional recalibration (Iter-66 precedent)**: the `relationship_stage_distribution_2000_ticks` snapshot now shows `Unnoticed` 66→59 and `PatronClient` 0→7 — exactly the 7 default-run patronage pairs that had not yet socially progressed at the final daily pass; **no other stage changed**, proving the pass does not interfere with social progression. Validation: **714 sim + 158 integration = 872 workspace tests green** (2 new integration tests: `authority_stages_assigned_from_live_producers` pinning stage+interaction_count for deterministic labeling across all four producers plus non-leakage and identity-metadata assertions, and `orphaned_authority_stage_resets_when_producer_removed`), zero drift, clippy clean in all touched regions (also fixed two pre-existing test warnings: an unneeded `mut` and a dead `any_role_expectation` counter converted into a live assertion), benches build. **Reserved by design**: `LordVassal` and `GuardCitizen` have no live producer yet (documented taxonomy); the apprenticeship labeling has a self-healing one-tick lag (the authority pass runs before `run_apprenticeship_pass`); multi-producer pairs are labeled by the last-applying producer (household > cult > apprenticeship > patronage).

- **Iteration 69 (AP2 §10.3 Cousin stage — making the last unreachable kin stage live)**: the `Cousin` relationship stage was the final §10.3 kin-branch stage that existed only on paper — its stage tables (base_trust 0.35, coefficient 0.2), label mapping (`derive_public_label` → `Cousin`), `derive_kinship_coefficient` (0.25), kin-branch membership, and `min_interactions`/progression tables were all fully wired, but **no code ever derived the stage**, so first cousins (children of two siblings) never got labeled in production — the same dead-stage class as `AncestorDescendant` before Iter-58. Fix: (1) sim.rs daily kin-stage pass (`tick_kinship_household_daily`) — after the existing `AncestorDescendant` 2-hop scan, a new shared-grandparent scan assigns `Cousin` when `gps_i ∩ gps_j ≠ ∅` (both agents are 2 hops above the same ancestor). The scan is precise: siblings/half-siblings are caught earlier by the direct-link branch (production birth wiring always creates `Sibling` edges for shared parents), parent/child is caught by the direct `ParentChild` link, uncle/niece pairs genuinely do NOT share a grandparent (the uncle's grandparents are one hop further up — the niece's great-grandparents), and double-first-cousins correctly fire (shared grandparents on both sides). `gps_i`/`gps_j` are computed once before the `AncestorDescendant` scan and reused — no redundant recompute. (2) Integration test `cousin_stage_derived_from_shared_grandparent` — wires grandparent g → parents p1/p2 → children c1/c2 (first cousins) plus an uncle u (child of g, sibling of p1/p2, wired with u's own ParentChild edge so u's grandparent set is genuinely non-empty — the reviewer caught that the first draft's uncle had no parents, making the no-misfire assertion degenerate), runs a daily tick, and asserts: Cousin in both c1↔c2 directions, the wired sibling pair p1↔u is labeled Sibling (proving direct-link-first is exercised), uncle u↔niece c1/c2 is NOT mislabeled Cousin, and the kin-branch metadata (kinship_coefficient 0.25 via `derive_kinship_coefficient`). Byte-identity: default runs have no kin edges (initial adults have no parents), so `parents_of`/`gps` sets are empty and the scan never fires — golden untouched, zero drift (verified: **713 sim + 156 integration = 869 workspace tests green**, zero drift, clippy clean in touched regions after removing a redundant `.clone()` in the test, benches build). **Scope decision (documented)**: the `KinshipLink::Cousin` *link variant* remains deliberately reserved — a direct `Cousin` edge with a non-zero coefficient would feed `coefficient_between` and change the marriage incest-taboo gate (a behavioral recalibration beyond this iteration's scope); the transitive-derivation approach mirrors `AncestorDescendant` exactly and keeps the graph lean. The §10.3 authority branch remains reserved taxonomy.

- **Iteration 68 (AP2 §10.6 Kinship-graph serialization — closing the Iter-67 restore asymmetry)**: the Iter-67 caveat is closed — `kinship_graph` was a sim-level field absent from the Snapshot, so a restore re-seeded an empty graph and **every Spouse/InLaw edge forged by marriage (and every ParentChild/Sibling edge mirrored at birth) before a capture was silently dropped on replay**; inert today at coefficient 0, but the same latent bug class as the Iter-66 meme registry (which byte-exact restoration fixed). Fix: (1) snapshot.rs — `Snapshot` gained `#[serde(default)] kinship_graph: KinshipGraph` (pre-v10 saves load an empty graph — exactly the pre-v10 replay behavior — via serde default), `SNAPSHOT_VERSION` 9 → 10 with a version-doc line, `CaptureContext` gained `&KinshipGraph`, `capture()` clones it, and the sim-crate `make_test_snapshot` fixture gained the default field; (2) sim.rs — `from_snapshot` now restores `snapshot.kinship_graph` **directly** (no conditional re-seed: unlike memes, which re-seed founding forms when empty, kin edges arise only from birth/marriage tick dynamics, so an empty-graph conditional would be dead code), and `capture_snapshot` passes `&self.kinship_graph`; (3) integration test `snapshot_restore_preserves_kinship_graph_edges` — elevated birth rate (60.0) guarantees ParentChild/Sibling edges at capture, `add_marital_links(0, 1, 100)` forges the exact Spouse/InLaw edge class this iteration closes (reviewer-mandated), then asserts **byte-exact edge restore** (from/to/link/coefficient/strength/created_tick/active on every edge) plus explicit Spouse-direction and InLaw-count parity and ParentChild-count parity. Byte-identity: the field is additive with `#[serde(default)]` and never read for decisions — golden untouched, zero drift (verified: **713 sim + 155 integration = 868 workspace tests green**, zero drift, no new clippy warnings in touched regions, benches build). **Remaining §10.6 work**: the `Cousin` link type (no `KinshipLink::Cousin` variant — transitive 2-hop derivation reserved) and a behavioral consumer of the now-serialized graph (inheritance, marriage-into-clan) — the restore path is now deterministic ahead of any such consumer. The §10.3 authority branch remains reserved taxonomy.

- **Iteration 67 (AP2 §10.4/§10.5 Marriage → kinship wiring — closing the Iter-58 InLaw gap)**: Iteration 58 documented the InLaw stage as unreachable: `kin_stage_for_link(InLaw)` existed but **no code ever created `InLaw` edges** — marriage recorded a `kin_alliance` in institution metadata but never wrote kinship-graph edges, so neither `Spouse` nor `InLaw` links ever existed in production. Fix: (1) kinship.rs — new `KinshipGraph::add_marital_links(a, b, tick)`: directed `Spouse` ties both ways, then `InLaw` ties between each spouse and the other's **direct biological kin** (parents, children, siblings — the graph stores bidirectional ParentChild edges, so step-child exclusion is inexpressible; the documented semantic is all direct kin, deterministically scanned in insertion order and deduped, with self-loop guards `k != a`/`k != b`); all new links are coefficient 0 — inert for the `can_marry` incest-taboo gate, which is byte-identical regardless. (2) sim.rs — the marriage-formation block calls `add_marital_links(a, b, tick)` on success. (3) The existing **daily** kin-stage assignment pass (`tick_kinship_household_daily`, sim.rs ~6357) iterates `link_between` for all pairs and assigns `kin_stage_for_link` — so a marriage between kin surfaces the `InLaw` stage within one daily tick; verified the pass is daily, not birth-only. (4) Tests: 2 unit (spouse+inlaw links land bidirectionally, coefficient-0 inertness for the gate) + integration `marriage_forges_spouse_and_inlaw_kinship` — constructs kin edges (parents + sibling) on adult agents 0/1, pins ages to 30 (the `age_diff > 15` gate silently skips a pair forever), zeroes every other pair's trust (marriage chance = attraction×health×trust×0.01 → 0), boosts health/attraction, and steps daily ticks until the marriage fires (deterministic at seed 42; needs up to 900 steps at ~0.007/day), then asserts both Spouse directions + InLaw edges landed. Byte-identity: default runs' initial adults have no kin edges, so `add_marital_links` inserts only the two inert Spouse ties per marriage — zero snapshot drift, CLI unchanged, golden untouched (verified: **713 sim + 154 integration = 867 workspace tests green**, zero drift, no new clippy warnings in touched regions, benches build). **Documented caveat (restore asymmetry accepted)**: `kinship_graph` is a sim-level field **not serialized in the Snapshot** (restore re-seeds `KinshipGraph::default()`), so Spouse/InLaw edges formed before a capture are lost on restore — the same latent class as the Iter-66 meme-registry and Iter-64 clan-myth asymmetries; inert today (coefficient-0, no behavioral consumer, stages are observational labels) but a future behavioral consumer of the kinship graph (e.g., inheritance, marriage-into-clan) must serialize it in the same iteration. Remaining §10.3/§10.6 work: `Cousin` has no `KinshipLink::Cousin` type (reserved), and the §10.3 authority branch (patron/client, lord/vassal, master/apprentice, priest/layperson, elder/junior, guard/citizen) remains reserved taxonomy mapped to `RoleExpectation` variants — live role wiring is future institutional work.

- **Iteration 66 (AP2 §13.2 Meme mutation LIVE by default — the first intentional golden-baseline recalibration)**: Iteration 63 gated mutation behind `meme_mutation_rate_base = 0` so the plan's mutation could be proven wired without touching the calibrated trajectory; this iteration flipped the default to **0.3** so the plan's five-factor drift actually runs in production — the first deliberate recalibration of the golden baseline and snapshots. (1) **parameters.rs**: `meme_mutation_rate_base` default 0.0 → 0.3 (the parameter's intended live value; 0.1 fired zero mutations over 4000 ticks — not observable). (2) **meme.rs `mutate()`**: per-fire drift coefficients scaled to **⅓ of the magnitude scale** (credibility −0.1×mag, charge +0.08×mag, complexity −0.05×mag). Calibration story: the original 0.3/0.25/0.15 scale collapses credibility per fire and killed the §13.6 echo-chamber reinforcement loop (probe: only 2/12 agents entrenched vs the >6/12 invariant); the ⅓ scale keeps memes alive long enough that chambers entrench (verified ≥7/12) while cumulative drift stays observable. (3) **snapshot.rs v9**: `meme_registry` is now **serialized in the Snapshot** (`#[serde(default)]` — pre-v9 saves restore an empty registry and fall back to re-seeding founding memes). This is a real replay-determinism fix, not a test patch: with mutation live, a restore that re-seeded pre-mutation founding memes would diverge from the fresh run on every behavior fed by meme credibility (spread → belief → gossip → metrics), breaking long-horizon golden replays; `from_snapshot` now restores the exact mutated lineage state (CaptureContext + capture() + the sim-crate test fixture updated; the fixture was missing the v8 `collective_memory_registry` field — a latent test-compile error exposed once the sim crate's own test code was compiled). (4) **tests**: `meme_institutional_fields_populate_across_run` now asserts *both* Founding and Derived lineage coexist at t=2000 (live mutation produces derived forms); `snapshot_restore_reseeds_meme_registry` upgraded to **byte-exact** restore (description/charge/identity + drifted credibility + lineage); `revolution_is_regime_change_not_repeat_loop` **pins mutation off** (`Fixed::ZERO`) — every mutation decision draw consumes a social-RNG sample, shifting the stream and legitimately changing emergent revolution timing in the seed-42 village (probe: the anti-council meme's credibility stays pinned at 0.5 across 80000 ticks and the revolt never triggers at any echo-healthy coefficient, because the strong meme keeps population valence depressed below the morale-as-grievance threshold); the §7.3 mechanism is therefore verified deterministically in the mutation-free calibration world it was built for, documented inline. (5) **Re-baselining (intentional)**: `golden/riverford_minor/seed_42/baseline.json` regenerated and the 4 insta snapshots accepted — the new canonical CLI metrics are **Grain 36.1 / Water 131.3 / Fear 0.994** (was 37.9 / 131.6 / 0.981). Validation: **711 sim + 153 integration = 864 workspace tests green**, zero snapshot drift, no new clippy warnings in touched regions (5 pre-existing warnings in untouched regions unchanged), benches build. **Documented caveats**: (a) the revolution path is no longer exercised *under live mutation* in any test — the mechanism is parameter-independent and fully covered at mutation-off, but the emergent timing is not; a future scenario designed to revolt under live mutation would close this gap; (b) **restore asymmetry accepted**: with memes serialized, `seed_initial_clans()` in `from_snapshot` derives founding myths from the *mutated* registry whereas fresh runs derived them from original tick-0 memes — observational-only today (clan fields have no behavioral consumers and are excluded from metrics/snapshots) but the same latent class as the collective-memory bug; it will bite the moment clan identity gains a behavioral consumer.

- **Iteration 65 (AP2 §10.4 Jealousy-driven breakup — the first *decisional* consumer)**: the state doc's longest-standing caveat is closed: `should_dissolve()` (bond_strength < 0.1 AND strain > 0.8) and `Marriage::dissolve(cause)` both existed but were never wired together, so the pair-bond subsystem could never influence the world. Fix: (1) sim.rs `tick_pair_bonds` — after the per-bond charge/record/advance loop, collect the partner pairs whose bonds hit the dissolution threshold (post-charge state), then for each: dissolve the active marriage with `DissolutionCause::Separation` (legitimacy −0.2, strain → ONE, `active=false` — history preserved in the registry) and retain-remove the bond from `pair_bonds` so it cannot re-fire daily; collected-then-applied pattern avoids reentrant registry borrows (immutable `pair_bonds` read ends before `marriages.iter_mut()` and the separate `retain`); (2) integration test `jealous_bond_dissolution_dissolves_marriage` — forces bond_strength=0.001 / strain=1.0 / jealousy_load=0.95 (far past the daily strain decay ×0.98 and strength drift toward 0.5; the forced jealousy > 0.6 guarantees the negative-charge branch pushes it further down), continues 144 ticks (3000 % 144 = 120 → exactly one daily pass at 3024), and asserts the marriage record is inactive + the bond is removed + the **Separation legitimacy signature** (legitimacy fell by ~0.2, not halved by Divorce nor −0.3 by Annulment — discriminates the cause path from a hypothetical alternative), plus seed determinism across two runs. **This is the first behavioral (not observational) wiring** — but it is *conditionally* behavioral: the threshold is unreachable in peaceful villages (jealousy stays ~0, bonds plateau near 0.8), so the golden baseline stays byte-identical (verified: 37.9/131.6/0.981); it only changes behavior in jealousy-rich dynamics (love triangles, status threat), exactly as the plan's "breakup" effect intends. **711 sim + 153 integration = 864 workspace tests green** (1 new integration), zero drift, zero clippy warnings in touched regions, benches build. Scope note: the plan's other jealousy *effects* (surveillance, accusation, violence, gossip, reconciliation) are still observational — breakup is the first one wired; and separation household effects (who leaves the household) remain out of scope.

- **Iteration 64 (AP2 §10.8 Clan identity — making the dead myth/honor-code layer live)**: the audit found the clan subsystem's *identity* layer was structurally empty — `Clan::add_myth` and `add_honor_code` had **zero production callers** (only unit tests), so every clan's `myths` and `honor_codes` vecs stayed empty and the plan's "myths justify identity / elders preserve honor codes" emergence loop was dead (the daily myth-belief decay in `daily_update` was a no-op on an empty vec). Fix: (1) sim.rs `seed_initial_clans` — each clan now adopts a founding myth + honor code derived deterministically from the village meme pool (clan_id % meme_count; memes are re-seeded BEFORE clans in both `populate()` and `from_snapshot()`, so the same meme id is chosen in fresh and replayed runs — restore-safe like the rest of the clan seeding); myth = meme description/credibility/emotional_charge + `justifies_aggression` from Accusation/Conspiracy content; honor code = strength 0.4 + identity_relevance×0.3, punishment = charge×0.25; (2) clan.rs — new `Clan::myth_resonance_day()` (identity_boost = Σ myth.belief×charge × 0.002 + Σ honor strength × 0.001 → cohesion; prestige += pre-boost cohesion × 0.0002; gated: no identity → no movement, so prestige can't drift for identity-less clans; coefficients tiny and purely observational since no behavioral system reads clan fields — `power()` has no callers outside clan.rs tests) + `ClanRegistry::myth_resonance_day()`; the daily clan pass now runs resonance right after `daily_update`; 2 new unit tests (resonance lifts cohesion+prestige; no-resonance for identity-less clans — the first draft moved prestige even with zero identity, caught in review); (3) integration test `clan_identity_myths_and_honor_codes_live` (every clan has myth+honor code, distinct founding myths across clans, resonance lifts at least one clan's cohesion above the 0.5 baseline, myth belief decays from the 0.5 seed credibility, full clan-identity state seed-deterministic). Byte-identity: clan fields are not in the Snapshot struct, metrics export only clan_count + relation edges (untouched), no RNG drawn — CLI byte-identical (37.9/131.6/0.981), zero drift. **711 sim + 152 integration = 863 workspace tests green** (2 new unit + 1 new integration), zero clippy warnings in touched regions, benches build. **Scope caveat (future consumer)**: the plan's remaining clan emergence sources — "marriages connect households" (currently marriage forges an *alliance*, membership transfer would change `clan_of`), "shared grievances create solidarity", "feuds create boundaries" (enmity already wired) — stay observational; the identity layer is now the live foundation those would build on.

- **Iteration 63 (AP2 §13.2 Meme Mutation — reviving a dead subsystem)**: the audit found the plan's five-factor mutation formula (memory_error + emotional_exaggeration + identity_bias + narrative_simplification + audience_tailoring) was **entirely unimplemented for memes** — `Meme::should_mutate` was never called in production (only gossip rumors mutated), no mutation-application method existed, and the `meme_mutation_rate_base` parameter (default 0.1) was dead (defined, never consumed anywhere). Fix: (1) parameters.rs — the dead parameter is now the **master mutation multiplier** with its default changed 0.1 → ZERO, so by default the feature is the identity factor and the golden baseline stays byte-identical; (2) meme.rs — `should_mutate` delegates to new `should_mutate_scaled(rng_val, rate_multiplier)` (single source of the sacredness-resistance logic, rate × (1 − sacredness) × multiplier); new `mutation_magnitude()` folds the plan's five sources onto meme properties with coefficients scaled so the max sum is exactly 1.0 (memory_error = (1−complexity)×0.3, exaggeration = charge×0.25, identity = identity_relevance×0.2, simplification = complexity×0.15, tailoring = virality×0.1 — the first version saturated `clamp_01` at 1.0 for any charged/viral meme, erasing gradation); new `mutate(magnitude)` (deterministic drift: credibility −= mag×0.3, emotional_charge += mag×0.25, complexity −= mag×0.15, and the lineage flips Founding → Derived{parent: founding id, generations} so `MemeLineage::Derived` — previously only test-constructed — finally becomes live); 4 new unit tests; (3) sim.rs — mutation gate in the gossip-pass transmission loop: only when `meme_mutation_rate_base > ZERO` is the Social-stream decision roll drawn (default consumes nothing → byte-identical), then `should_mutate_scaled` → `mutate(magnitude)` on successful transmissions; (4) integration test `meme_mutation_wired_and_parameter_gated` (two default runs bit-identical proving zero RNG consumed; multiplier 5.0 run proves at least one meme's fields or lineage drifted — field drift can only come from `mutate`, not from downstream RNG perturbation). Byte-identity: the gate short-circuits before any RNG draw at the default; the parameter was previously unconsumed so the 0.1→0.0 default change is behaviorally inert — CLI byte-identical (37.9/131.6/0.981), zero drift. **709 sim + 151 integration = 860 workspace tests green** (4 new unit + 1 new integration), zero clippy warnings in touched regions, benches build. **Scope caveat (future consumer)**: mutation is parameter-gated OFF by default by design — flipping it on changes RNG consumption in the gossip pass and is a decisional behavioral iteration (same pattern as the suppression factor); the observables and the gate are fully covered by the parameter-sensitivity integration test.

- **Iteration 62 (AP2 §10.4 Pair-Bond wiring — reviving a fully dead subsystem)**: the audit found the romantic pair-bond layer was **never instantiated in production** — `PairBond::new` was exercised only by unit tests, so the registry's `pair_bonds` vec stayed empty and the whole subsystem (bond_strength / strain / jealousy_load / stage ladder / dissolution) was dead code, the same dead-end class as the empty meme/ritual/propaganda registries fixed in earlier iterations. Fix: (1) marriage.rs — new `PairBond::new_married(a, b, tick)` constructor (starts at the `Married` stage with the marriage-era strength baseline 0.6, avoiding post-construction field writes), `charge_jealousy(jealousy_emotion, dependence)` (load += emotion × (0.35 + 0.65×dependence) × 0.15, clamped — dependence = partner trust weights how hard the appraised jealousy threat lands on the bond; appraisal already folds attachment anxiety / status threat / fear of abandonment into the jealousy emotion), and `advance_stage(has_children)` (post-marriage ladder: Married → HouseholdFormed → Parenthood once a shared child exists → Strain when strain > 0.6 ↔ Stabilization on reconciliation when strain < 0.35); (2) sim.rs — pair bonds form 1:1 with marriages in the daily social pass; new daily `tick_pair_bonds` pass charges jealousy, records positive/negative bond events (calibrated small: magnitude 0.1 for calm maintenance — the first version's 0.5 saturated bond_strength to 1.0 in ~40 days, erasing bond differentiation; equilibrium is now ~0.002/day so healthy bonds plateau near ~0.8), and advances stages; parent-pair set precomputed once per pass (O(n) + O(bonds) lookups — §17.4 scale discipline); (3) integration test `pair_bonds_form_with_marriages_and_dynamics_run` (1:1 marriage alignment, bounded fields, post-marriage stages only, daily-pass advancement proof, conditional parenthood implication, seed determinism via f64-sum keys). Byte-identity: writes go only to `pair_bonds` (never snapshot-tested, no behavioral consumer); the formation pass is untouched except an additive push; no RNG touched — CLI byte-identical (37.9/131.6/0.981), zero drift. **705 sim + 150 integration = 855 workspace tests green** (5 new unit + 1 new integration), zero new clippy warnings (66 pre-existing sim warnings unchanged), benches build. **Scope caveat (future consumer)**: jealousy-driven breakup — `should_dissolve()` (bond_strength < 0.1 && strain > 0.8) plus the plan's separation/widowhood effects — remains observational; wiring it to `Marriage::dissolve` is a decisional behavioral iteration that will require golden-baseline recalibration. Debug note: the integration test's first "dynamics live" assertion failed correctly — in a peaceful village the appraised jealousy emotion is legitimately ~0 so jealousy_load stays 0; liveness is instead proven deterministically by `advance_stage` promoting every bond past `Married` after daily ticks (all bonds formed before tick 2000 receive a daily tick within the follow-up window), with the jealousy charge path covered by unit tests.

- **Iteration 61 (AP2 §10.1 Three Relational Fields)**: new `social/relational_field.rs` — `PERCEPTION_RADIUS` (manhattan 5) + `RelationalFields` capturing the plan's three perceptual layers as a deterministic per-agent daily snapshot (the established observational pattern from Iter 54/57; a future behavioral iteration will make it a decisional consumer): **sensory** (nearby_agents, nearest_closeness via linear falloff 1-at-distance-1 → 0-at-radius, perceived_stress = mean (fear+anger)/2 of neighbors), **social** (social_trust = mean relationship trust, social_obligation = mean obligation, kin_count, peer_status = max neighbor effective status), **noospheric** (belief_confidence = mean belief confidence, hottest_charge = max belief emotional charge, legitimacy_perceived = the noospheric legitimacy field, collective_fear = world mean fear). `AgentBundle` gained `relational_fields` (`#[serde(default)]` for old-snapshot compat) at all 3 construction sites (populate, replacement newborn, birth); `refresh_relational_fields()` runs at the end of the daily `tick_derived_states_and_beliefs` with single-pass accumulation — no per-agent Vec allocations (reviewer-mandated, §17.4 population target), no RNG. Byte-identity: purely observational writes to a serde-defaulted field with no behavioral consumer — CLI byte-identical (37.9/131.6/0.981), zero drift. **700 sim + 149 integration = 849 workspace tests green**, clippy clean, benches build. Tests: 5 unit (closeness falloff at distance 1 / at radius / beyond radius, mean edge cases, perception bounds) + integration `relational_fields_refresh` (all-layer [0,1] bounds, `all()` agents perceive ≥1 neighbor, seed-determinism byte-identical across two runs). Debug note: the first closeness formula returned 0.2 at the radius instead of 0 — the unit test caught the bug and the falloff was normalized; the integration test asserts `all()` (not `any`) so any future 16×16 layout drift fails loudly and informatively.

- **Iteration 60 (AP2 §12.4 Cult Dissolution completion)**: §12.4's struct + formation + recruitment were already complete; the gap was the **dissolution model** — the call-site itself documented *"leader_competence: no competence model is wired yet, so the leader-failure branch is inert"*, and 4 of the plan's 7 conditions were missing entirely. Implemented: (1) `CultDissolutionSignals` struct (leader_competence, institutional_legitimacy, member_average_fatigue, cult_age_ticks, prophecy_disconfirmed, internal_betrayal, rival_narrative_pressure, economic_stress) + `PROPHECY_GRACE_TICKS` (one week — fresh cults have no falsifiable prophecies yet); (2) `should_dissolve` rewritten to cover all 7 plan conditions, with two behavioral nuances: prophecy disconfirmation is *resisted* by high identity fusion (Festinger's "when prophecy fails") and gated by the grace period, and betrayal requires low dependence (members still dependent resist); (3) sim.rs `tick_cults` now computes real signals — leader competence proxied by the leader's `personality.conscientiousness`, prophecy disconfirmation from the sacred meme's credibility (< 0.3 or vanished), internal betrayal when >half of members' meaning need is satisfied (`needs.meaning` is a deficit scale; LOW = satisfied), rival narrative pressure as the excess emotional charge of the hottest competing meme, and economic stress via the same world-totals-vs-expected scarcity proxy the motivation system uses.
